# Mayflower Guild Cook Book (1930)

*An edited, organized, kitchen-ready edition of recipes compiled by the Mayflower Guild of First Congregational Church, Kalamazoo, Michigan.*

This is the recipe collection **as organized and edited by us**: original contributors and recipe identities retained, ingredient lists put into a single column, and incomplete or unworkable procedures reconstructed using culinary judgment. It is not a facsimile or verbatim transcription. The original 1930 scans remain the source of record.

**708 distinct recipes · 31 chapters · Edition v8**

## Children's Food

### Bread Pudding

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 11, scan p. 16 · `mg1930-p016-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups stale bread crumbs
- 2 eggs
- 1 qt. scalded milk
- ½ tsp. salt
- ½ cup sugar
- 1 tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Soak bread crumbs in milk. When cool add sugar, slightly beaten eggs, salt and flavoring. Bake in moderate oven about 1 hour or until set and firm.

**Variations:**

1. Use brown sugar.

2. Caramelize 1/3 cup sugar, add milk and scald. When smooth proceed as given.

3. Melt 2 squares of chocolate. Add milk and scald.

4. Add 1/2 cup raisins and 1/2 cup currants.

5. Save one egg white. When pudding is baked spread with jelly and meringue and brown.

---

### Chocolate Cornstarch Pudding

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 11, scan p. 16 · `mg1930-p016-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups milk scalded
- ⅓ cup sugar
- 4 tbsp. cornstarch
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- 2 tbsp. cocoa
- ½ tsp. vanilla
- 1/4 cup cold milk

**Preparation**

Mix cornstarch, cocoa, sugar and salt with a little cold milk (1/4 cup). Add to scalded milk in top part of double boiler. Stir constantly until thick. Cover and cook ½ hour. Add vanilla and put in molds.

---

### Cornstarch Pudding

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 11, scan p. 16 · `mg1930-p016-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups milk scalded
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- 4 tbsp. cornstarch
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- 1/4 cup sugar
- 1/4 cup milk cold

**Preparation**

Mix cornstarch, sugar, and salt with cold milk. Add to hot milk and stir until thick. Cover and cook ½ hour. Add vanilla and put in molds in a cool place.

**To vary:**

Color half the pudding pink, or other desired color with vegetable coloring. Or all of the pudding may be colored. Flavor with orange, lemon, maple or strawberry flavoring.

---

### Steamed Custard

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 11, scan p. 16 · `mg1930-p016-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups milk, scalded
- ½ tsp. vanilla
- 2 eggs
- nutmeg
- ⅓ cup sugar
- pinch salt

**Preparation**

Beat eggs, add sugar, salt and vanilla; beat well. Then add hot milk slowly. Place in glass bowl or other serving dish. Add a little grated nutmeg. Steam about 10 minutes or until mixture is thick in center. Serves four. Fine for children.

---

### Macaroni With Carrots And Eggs

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 12, scan p. 17 · `mg1930-p017-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup macaroni
- 1 cup white sauce
- 1 cup cooked carrot cubes
- bread crumbs
- 4 hard-boiled eggs

**Preparation**

Boil macaroni. Drain. Slice eggs. Put ingredients by layers into a casserole. Top with fine crumbs. Bake in moderate oven until crumbs are brown. Time in combining, 5 minutes. Time in cooking, 20 minutes. Temperature, 350 degrees. Recipe makes four servings.

---

### Children's Salad

*Mrs. Katherine Russell. · Original printed p. 12, scan p. 17 · `mg1930-p017-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 raw carrots
- small onion
- 1 stalk celery
- salad dressing
- 1 cup chopped cabbage

**Preparation**

Put all through grinder and mix with a very little dressing. Serves 3 to 5. Increase as desired.

---

### Cream Of Spinach Soup

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 12, scan p. 17 · `mg1930-p017-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lb. spinach
- 1 tbsp. flour
- 1 cup water
- 2 cups milk or white stock
- ½ tsp. salt
- 1/4 tsp. pepper
- 2 tbsp. butter

**Preparation**

Put water in kettle and when boiling add washed spinach. Cook uncovered until tender. Rub through a ricer. Make white sauce, season and add spinach. Reheat and serve.

---

### Vegetable Sandwiches

*Mrs. K. S. Russell. · Original printed p. 12, scan p. 17 · `mg1930-p017-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 carrot, turnip, or small handful shredded cabbage
- 2 tbsp. cream cheese
- Whole-wheat bread, for sandwiches

**Preparation**

Grind 1 carrot, or turnip, or a little cabbage, and mix with a little cream cheese and spread on whole wheat bread. Very hearty for school lunches.

---

### Children's Raisin Candy

*Mrs. E. J. Anderson. · Original printed p. 12, scan p. 17 · `mg1930-p017-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup powd. sugar
- 2 tbsp. melted butter
- 2 tbsp. cocoa
- ½ tsp. vanilla
- enough boiling water to mix stiff

**Preparation**

Take a little of the mixture on the end of a spoon and form into a ball, taking 2 raisins to each ball and press together until nearly flat. If white candy is preferred, omit cocoa.

---

### Caramel Milk

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 12, scan p. 17 · `mg1930-p017-r06`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 tbsp. sugar
- 3/4 cup hot milk
- 3 tbsp. water

**Preparation**

Caramelize half the sugar. Add boiling water. When smooth add remaining sugar and milk. Serve hot or very cold. Serves one. (To caramelize sugar, place it in an iron or aluminum spider. Heat it slowly stirring constantly until sugar melts. )

---

## Beverages

### Chocolate Egg And Milk Shake

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson · Original printed p. 15, scan p. 20 · `mg1930-p020-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 tbsp. finely crushed ice
- 1 egg
- 2½ tbsp. chocolate syrup
- ⅔ cup milk

**Preparation**

Put ingredients in glass and shake thoroughly (using one of the shakers that may be bought at any kitchen furnishing store), and strain into another glass for serving. A few gratings of nutmeg or a few sprinklings of cinnamon may be placed on top. The ice may be omitted if the ingredients have been thoroughly chilled in the ice box.

---

### Ginger Ale Soda

*Mrs. James Rork. · Original printed p. 15, scan p. 20 · `mg1930-p020-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 large tbsp. vanilla ice cream in each glass.

**Preparation**

Fill glass with ice cold ginger ale. Stir until frothy. Serve at once.

---

### Grape Juice

*Mrs. E. J. Anderson. · Original printed p. 15, scan p. 20 · `mg1930-p020-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 large cup of washed grapes.
- ½ cup sugar

**Preparation**

Put in quart jar and fill with boiling water. Seal. Let stand until after the Holidays.

---

### Hot Chocolate

*Mrs. D. C. Rockwell. · Original printed p. 15, scan p. 20 · `mg1930-p020-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 qt. milk
- 1 sq. chocolate
- 2 tbsp. sugar
- pinch salt

**Preparation**

Place in double boiler until chocolate melts, then whip with dover eggbeater.

---

### Iced Cocoa

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 15, scan p. 20 · `mg1930-p020-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup cocoa
- 1/4 cup flour
- ½ cup sugar
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- 1 qt. boiling water
- 1 tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Mix dry ingredients in top of double boiler, add boiling water. Cook until smooth, cool and add vanilla. Put 2 tbsp. in glass with cracked ice and fill glass with milk.

---

### Iced Tea

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 15, scan p. 20 · `mg1930-p020-r06`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 stalk mint
- Hot tea, as needed
- Ginger ale, half as much as the tea
- Ice

**Preparation**

Place mint in the bottom of a pitcher, pour hot tea over it, then add half as much ginger ale as tea. Serve over ice.

---

### Iced Tea With Ginger Ale

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 16, scan p. 21 · `mg1930-p021-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pt. hot tea
- Cracked ice
- 1 pt. ginger ale
- Lemon juice
- sugar

**Preparation**

Pour the tea over the cracked ice, and when thoroughly chilled add ginger ale. Serve with sugared lemon slices, made by covering the thin slices with sugar and allowing them to stand until syrupy.

---

### Mint Ginger

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 16, scan p. 21 · `mg1930-p021-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups ginger ale
- ½ cup fresh mint leaves
- 1/4 cup lemon juice
- 2 cups orange juice
- ½ cup powdered sugar
- 6 sprigs fresh mint
- 3 slices lemon

**Preparation**

Rub mint leaves and sugar together until well mixed, and add lemon juice and orange juice. Allow to stand at least 15 minutes in a cold place. Strain, add ginger ale and pour into glasses a third full of crushed ice. Garnish each glass with a sprig of mint and a half slice of lemon.

---

### Orange Grape Juice Punch

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 16, scan p. 21 · `mg1930-p021-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups orange juice
- 2 cups grape juice
- 1/4 cup lemon juice
- 2 cups ice water
- ½ cup sugar
- 3 slices orange
- 6 Maraschino cherries

**Preparation**

Stir the sugar into the orange and lemon juices until dissolved. Add grape juice and chilled water. Pour into glasses containing half an orange slice, one cherry and a small piece of ice.

---

### Oriental Punch

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 16, scan p. 21 · `mg1930-p021-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 lemons
- 1/2 cup sugar
- 6 whole cloves
- 3 cups orange juice
- 2 cups freshly made hot tea
- 2 cups grape juice

**Preparation**

Slice lemons through rind and pulp. Put with cloves and sugar in pitcher or bowl and pour hot tea over them. Cover and let stand until chilled. Add orange and grape juice and serve, iced.

---

### Pineappleade

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 16, scan p. 21 · `mg1930-p021-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup sugar
- 1 cup canned pineapple juice
- 1 cup water
- ⅓ cup lemon juice
- 1 qt. water

**Preparation**

Boil the sugar and 1 cup water for ten minutes. Add ingredients as given. Serve iced. Serves 6 to 8.

---

### Spiced Blocks

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 17, scan p. 22 · `mg1930-p022-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup water
- ½ cup sugar
- 1 tbsp. whole cloves
- 2 inch piece cinnamon
- 2 lemons, juice
- water to make 2 cups
- Maraschino cherries
- 1/4 slice lemon
- strong tea
- 2 cubes ice

**Preparation**

Put in a small sauce pan the water and sugar and bring to the boiling point. When the sugar is dissolved add the cloves and cinnamon and boil 10 minutes. Add juice of lemons and water. Strain through fine strainer into mold and put in glasses with cubes of ice.

---

### Soft Drink

*Mrs. James Rork. · Original printed p. 17, scan p. 22 · `mg1930-p022-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 bottles ginger ale
- 1 bottle white rock
- 2 cups strong tea
- 4 tbsp. sugar
- 1 dozen Maraschino cherries
- 1 cup orange juice
- ½ cup lemon juice

**Preparation**

Mix, fill glasses half full of ice, add sprig of mint.

---

### Varsity Punch

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 17, scan p. 22 · `mg1930-p022-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 cups orange juice
- 1 cup water
- ½ cup lemon juice
- 1½ cup sugar
- 1 cup crushed pineapple

**Preparation**

Boil sugar and water 3 minutes and chill. Add fruit juices and crushed pineapple and pour over ice in pitcher or glasses.

---

### Watermelon Quencher

*Mrs. E. J. Anderson. · Original printed p. 17, scan p. 22 · `mg1930-p022-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 qt. watermelon pulp
- Juice of 1 lemon
- Juice of 1 orange
- 3 pints water
- 1 cup sugar

**Preparation**

Crush watermelon pulp. Add strained lemon and orange juice and water. Let stand 4 hours; strain. Stir in sugar until dissolved and serve.

## Breads, Griddle Cakes and Waffles

### Sour Milk Griddle Cakes

*Mrs. James L. Sigler. · Original printed p. 19, scan p. 24 · `mg1930-p024-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 egg, well beaten
- 1 cup sour milk
- flour
- 1 tsp. soda
- pinch salt

**Preparation**

Mix above ingredients in order given, and add enough flour for a smooth batter. Bake on a hot and well greased griddle.

---

### Griddle Cakes

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 19, scan p. 24 · `mg1930-p024-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups flour
- 2 eggs
- 3 tsp. baking powder
- 1/4 cup melted butter
- 1½ cup milk
- ½ tsp. salt

**Preparation**

Sift dry ingredients, add milk and eggs, beat well, add melted butter. Bake on an ungreased aluminum griddle or on a greased griddle iron. Huckleberries or corn may be added. For waffles add more butter. Serves six.

---

### Waffles

*Mrs. V. Paul Van Keuren. · Original printed p. 19, scan p. 24 · `mg1930-p024-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 eggs
- 2 cups flour
- 1 tsp. salt
- 3 tsp. baking powder
- 2 cups milk
- 4 tbsp. melted butter

**Preparation**

Beat yolks, add salt and milk. Add baking powder and flour, which have been sifted together twice. Add melted butter. Fold in stiffly beaten egg whites. Makes ten waffles.

---

### Waffles

*Mrs. D. C. Rockwell. · Original printed p. 19, scan p. 24 · `mg1930-p024-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup milk
- 2 eggs
- 1½ cups of flour
- 2 tsp. baking powder
- ½ tsp. salt
- 4 tbsp. melted butter

**Preparation**

Beat eggs separately. To the yolks add milk and sifted dry ingredients and melted butter. Fold in beaten egg whites. Sour milk may be substituted for sweet in which case add ½ tsp. soda.

---

### Waffles

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 19, scan p. 24 · `mg1930-p024-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- [Level Measurements]
- 2 cups Swansdown cake flour
- 4 tbsp. melted butter
- 3 tsp. baking powder
- ½ tsp. salt
- 1 cup milk
- 3 eggs

**Preparation**

Sift together the dry ingredients. Add the egg yolks, beaten and mixed with the milk, then the melted butter. Fold in the egg whites beaten stiff. Have both sides of the waffle iron hot and well greased. Put a tablespoon of the mixture in each compartment and let down the top. When the mixture is baked on one side, turn the iron to brown the other side. Remove the cooked waffles with a fork. Serve with butter and syrup.

---

### Waffles ( With Electric Iron)

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 20, scan p. 25 · `mg1930-p025-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ cups pastry flour
- 6 tbsp. melted butter
- 2 tsp. baking powder
- 1 cup milk
- ½ tsp. salt
- 2 eggs
- 1 tbsp. sugar

**Preparation**

Sift dry ingredients together, then add melted butter, milk and yolks which have been beaten. Stir until batter is evenly formed. Beat whites of eggs to a stiff froth and add last. Bake in electric waffle iron.

---

### Delicious Waffles

*Mrs. LeRoy Bibby. · Original printed p. 20, scan p. 25 · `mg1930-p025-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups all-purpose flour
- 2 eggs, separated
- 4 tsp. baking powder
- 1¾ cups milk
- ½ tsp. salt
- ½ cup butter, melted and slightly cooled
- 2 tsp. sugar

**Preparation**

Whisk flour, baking powder, salt and sugar. Beat egg yolks with milk, then stir into dry ingredients. Fold in stiffly beaten egg whites and finally the melted, cooled butter. Bake in a thoroughly heated waffle iron until golden and crisp.

---

### Excellent Waffles

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 20, scan p. 25 · `mg1930-p025-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups bread flour, sifted 3 times
- ½ tsp. salt
- 1 pt. milk
- ½ cup melted butter
- 1 tsp. baking powder
- 3 eggs beaten separately

**Preparation**

Mix dry ingredients, add milk, butter, and egg yolks. Fold in whites just before baking.

---

### Waffles, Easy

*Mrs. Earl G. Clark. · Original printed p. 20, scan p. 25 · `mg1930-p025-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 egg
- 1½ cups sour milk or buttermilk
- 1 tsp. salt
- 1 heaping tsp. baking soda, stirred into milk
- Flour, enough to make a thin batter

**Preparation**

Try a little batter in the waffle iron.

---

### Universal Waffle Recipe

*Mrs. James L. Sigler. · Original printed p. 20, scan p. 25 · `mg1930-p025-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups flour
- 2 eggs
- 1 tsp. salt
- 2 cups milk
- 3 tsp. baking powder
- 4 tbsp. melted butter

**Preparation**

Sift dry ingredients into mixing bowl. Beat yolks of eggs well; add milk, and beat well, adding this to dry ingredients, slowly, beating until smooth. Add 4 tbsp. melted butter, and fold in stiffly beaten egg whites.

---

## Biscuits and Shortcake

### Ice Box Biscuits

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 23, scan p. 28 · `mg1930-p028-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup boiling water
- 2 cakes yeast
- 3/4 cup " Silverleaf" Lard
- 1/4 cup luke warm water
- 1 cup cold water
- 1 tbsp. salt
- 2 Brookfield eggs, beaten
- 7 cups flour
- ¾ cup sugar
- Melted Brookfield Butter

**Preparation**

Pour boiling water over lard. When melted add cold water, beaten eggs and sugar. Dissolve cakes of yeast in lukewarm water, and add to first mixture. Add salt and flour. Put in ice box over night. When ready to use pinch off piece the size of a walnut. Roll in melted butter. Put 3 in each muffin tin. Let stand 2½ hours. Bakes at 400 degrees for 15 to 20 minutes. Dough will keep a week, at least.

---

### Baking Powder Biscuit

*Mrs. James L. Sigler. · Original printed p. 23, scan p. 28 · `mg1930-p028-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 cups flour
- 2 tbsp. shortening, rounding
- 4 tsp. baking powder, rounding
- pinch salt
- milk enough for a soft dough

**Preparation**

Mix in order given. Roll out lightly to ½ inch thick, and cut with biscuit cutter. Place in greased pan, and bake for 15 minutes at 450 degrees.

---

### Baking Powder Biscuit

*Mrs. C. L. Mohney. · Original printed p. 23, scan p. 28 · `mg1930-p028-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups flour
- 2 tablespoons fat
- 4 teaspoons baking powder
- 3/4 cup milk
- ¼ teaspoon salt

**Preparation**

Sift dry ingredients; rub in fat with tip of fingers; add milk to make soft dough and toss on well floured board roll to one inch thick and cut. Bake 10 or 15 minutes.

---

### Baking Powder Biscuits

*Mrs. V. Paul Van Keuren. · Original printed p. 23, scan p. 28 · `mg1930-p028-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pt. flour (2 cups)
- 2 tsp. baking powder (rounding) Royal
- ½ tsp. salt
- 1 tbsp: lard
- milk for a soft dough

**Preparation**

Sift dry ingredients three times, and mix in lard well. Add milk. Flour board well. Roll out dough and cut. Bake at 400 degrees about twelve minutes. This makes about fifteen medium sized biscuits.

---

### Cheese Biscuits

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 24, scan p. 29 · `mg1930-p029-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups flour
- 3 tbsp. shortening
- 1 tsp. salt
- 1/4 cup grated cheese
- 4 tsp. baking powder
- 3/4 cup milk

**Preparation**

Mix dry ingredients. Cut in shortening. Add grated cheese and then milk. Roll ½" thick and cut with small cutter. Bake at 350°.

---

### Coffee Cake

*Mrs. Lawrence Fuller. · Original printed p. 24, scan p. 29 · `mg1930-p029-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 tbsp. sugar
- 1 egg
- 1 tbsp. butter
- ⅔ cup milk
- 1½ cups flour
- 2 tsp. baking powder
- cinnamon, salt

**Preparation**

Save out a small portion of the butter and sugar. Mix the rest of the ingredients in the manner of a muffin recipe. Bake in a shallow, square pan. When done and still hot, sprinkle butter, sugar and cinnamon over the top and put back in oven for about 5 minutes. Will serve 4.

---

### German Coffee Cake

*Mrs. L. L. Taylor. · Original printed p. 24, scan p. 29 · `mg1930-p029-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2¼ cups flour
- 2 tsp. sugar
- 1 tsp. salt
- 1 tbsp. butter
- 2 heaping tsp. baking powder
- 1 egg
- milk

**Preparation**

Sift dry ingredients together, rub in butter. Beat egg, add milk to egg to make 1¼ cups, stir all together. Turn into biscuit pan, and spread evenly. Brush top with melted butter. Bake at 400 degrees for about fifteen minutes. Sprinkle top with sugar and cinnamon.

---

### Short Cake Sour Milk

*Mrs. James L. Sigler. · Original printed p. 24, scan p. 29 · `mg1930-p029-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 qt. flour
- 2 heaping tbsp. shortening
- 1 level tsp. soda
- 2 tbsp. sugar

**Preparation**

Mix well. Add enough sour milk to make a stiff dough. Bake in greased pans in a hot oven, 425 degrees for fifteen minutes.

---

### Short Cake — Sweet Milk

*Mrs. J. L. Sigler. · Original printed p. 24, scan p. 29 · `mg1930-p029-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 cups flour
- 6 to 8 tbsp. " Silverleaf" lard
- 8 tsp. baking powder
- 2 tbsp. sugar

**Preparation**

Combine flour, baking powder and sugar. Cut in lard until crumbly, then add enough milk to form a stiff dough. Pat into greased shallow pans and bake at 450°F for about 15 minutes.

---

## Gingerbread

### Ginger Bread

*Mrs. James E. Rork. · Original printed p. 27, scan p. 32 · `mg1930-p032-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup sugar
- 1 tsp. soda in 1 tbsp. hot water
- 2 tbsp. butter
- ½ cup molasses
- 1 egg
- cinnamon
- 1 cup flour
- 1 tsp. ginger
- ⅓ cup hot water
- 1 tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Cream butter and sugar, add egg and flour. Add molasses mixed with soda and tsp. hot water. Last add spices and 1/3 cup of hot water. Beat well. Bake about 25 minutes in medium oven.

---

### Ginger Bread

*Mrs. D. Boudeman. · Original printed p. 27, scan p. 32 · `mg1930-p032-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup granulated sugar
- 2 tsp. soda, dissolved in hot water
- ½ cup butter
- 2½ cups of flour
- 1 cup hot water
- 1 tsp. cinnamon
- 1 cup dark molasses
- 1 tsp. ginger
- 2 eggs, well beaten (specified in printed directions)

**Preparation**

Add 2 well beaten eggs the last thing. Bake slowly in moderate oven. Makes 1 loaf.

---

### Ginger Bread

*Hazel Menzie. · Original printed p. 27, scan p. 32 · `mg1930-p032-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup butter and lard mixed
- ½ cup sugar
- ½ cup molasses
- ½ cup sour milk
- ½ tsp. soda dissolved in boiling water
- 1 large tbsp. ginger
- 1 tsp. cinnamon
- 1 egg, well beaten
- 1¼ cups sifted flour (level)

**Preparation**

Put together in order given and bake in a quick oven. Nice for small family.

---

### Soft Molasses Gingerbread

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 27, scan p. 32 · `mg1930-p032-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup molasses
- 2¼ cups flour
- 1/4 cup melted butter
- 2 tsp. ginger
- 1¾ tsp. soda
- ½ tsp. salt
- 1 cup sour milk
- 2 tbsp. sugar

**Preparation**

Put milk and molasses into mixing bowl. Sift and add all dry ingredients; beat vigorously; add melted butter. Bake in pan nine inches square, 45 minutes at 350°.

---

### Ginger Cake

*Mrs. James Sigler. · Original printed p. 28, scan p. 33 · `mg1930-p033-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup sugar
- 1 cup molasses
- ½ cup Swift's Premium Margarine
- 2 Brookfield Eggs
- 1 tsp. each ginger, cinnamon, cloves
- 2 tsp. soda dissolved in 1 cup boiling water
- 2½ cups flour

**Preparation**

Add eggs, well beaten, the last thing before baking. Bake in a large square pan, 45 minutes at 350 degrees.

---

### Gingerbread Soft

*Mrs. R. Mickey. · Original printed p. 28, scan p. 33 · `mg1930-p033-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- ⅓ cup shortening
- 1 egg or 2 yolks
- ½ cup molasses
- ½ cup sugar
- 1 tsp. cinnamon
- 2 cups flour
- ½ cup hot water or ¾ cup sour milk
- ¼ tsp. soda with the sour milk
- ½ tsp. salt
- 1 tsp. ginger

**Preparation**

Heat oven to 325°F and grease an 8-inch square pan. Cream shortening and sugar; beat in egg and molasses. Combine flour with cinnamon, ginger and salt. Stir in the dry mixture alternately with hot water, or use sour milk instead and dissolve baking soda in it. Spread in pan and bake 30–40 minutes, until center tests clean.

---

### Soft Ginger Bread

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 28, scan p. 33 · `mg1930-p033-r03`*

- ½ cup shortening
- 1 tsp. cinnamon
- 2 eggs
- 1 tsp. alspice
- ½ cup sugar
- 1 tsp. cloves
- 1 cup Brer Rabbit molasses
- 1 tsp. ginger
- 1 tsp. soda
- 2 cups flour
- 1 cup boiling water

Mix in order given. Bake 45 minutes at 350 degrees.

### Soft Ginger Bread

*Mrs. Earl G. Clark. · Original printed p. 28, scan p. 33 · `mg1930-p033-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup sugar
- 1 tsp. soda
- 1 tsp. ginger
- 4 cups flour
- 1 tsp. cinnamon
- Raisins, if desired

Cream butter and molasses, add sugar, spices, eggs, then sour milk and soda dissolved in a little boiling water. Add flour and raisins if desired. Bake 30 to 40 minutes at 350 degrees in two medium sized loaf pans.

### Date Bread

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 32, scan p. 37 · `mg1930-p037-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 eggs
- 1½ cups white flour
- ¼ tsp. salt
- 1 package dates (about 8 oz.), pitted and chopped
- 1 cup brown sugar
- 1 cup walnut pieces
- 1 tsp. baking soda
- 1 cup sour milk

**Preparation**

Heat oven to 325°F. Grease a 9 × 5-inch loaf pan. Beat eggs with brown sugar; stir in sour milk. Sift flour, salt and baking soda together. Combine wet and dry ingredients, fold in dates and walnuts and spread in the pan. Bake about 55–65 minutes, until a tester comes out clean. Cool before slicing.

### Date And Nut Bread

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 32, scan p. 37 · `mg1930-p037-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 3/4 cup brown sugar
- 1 cup cut dates
- 1 egg
- ½ cup walnut meats
- 1 tbsp. melted butter
- 1 cup boiling water
- 1½ cups bread flour
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- 1 tsp. baking soda

**Preparation**

Beat egg and sugar together, add butter. Over the cut dates sprinkle soda, then pour hot water over all. Then add flour, salt and nutmeats. Beat all well. Bake in one loaf or muffin tins. If baked in loaf, bake slowly for one hour at 300 degrees; in muffin tins at 350 degrees.

---

### Graham Prune Bread

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 32, scan p. 37 · `mg1930-p037-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup sugar
- 1/4 tsp. baking powder
- 1 egg, beaten
- 1 cup nut meats

- ½ tsp. salt
- 1 cup thick sour milk
- 1 cup stewed prunes, cut fine
- ½ cup prune juice
- 1½ cups bread flour
- 1 tsp. baking soda
- 1 cup graham flour

**Preparation**

Heat oven to 350°F and grease a loaf pan, or heat to 375°F for muffins. Mix bread flour, graham flour, baking soda, baking powder and salt. Beat egg with sugar, melted butter, sour milk and prune juice. Stir wet mixture into dry just until combined; fold in prunes and nuts. Bake a loaf about 60–85 minutes, until a tester comes out clean; for muffins bake about 18–25 minutes.

---

### Graham Bread

*Mrs. Walter Graham. · Original printed p. 32, scan p. 37 · `mg1930-p037-r04`*

- 1½ cups white flour

**Preparation**

Heat oven to 350°F and grease two loaf pans. Whisk graham flour, white flour and baking soda. Mix egg, brown sugar, molasses, sour milk and melted fat; combine with dry ingredients without overmixing. Divide between pans and bake 40–55 minutes until set.

### Blueberry Muffins

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 37, scan p. 42 · `mg1930-p042-r04`*

**Preparation**

If canned blueberries are used, have them well drained. Beat egg light with Dover egg beater, add sugar gradually. Then add butter and milk, then flour sifted with baking powder and salt, and then berries. Beat well. Should make 12 large muffins. Bake about 20 minutes at 425 degrees.

### Marshall Field: Potato Flour Muffins

Add ice water last. Spoon into greased muffin tins and bake 15–20 minutes in a moderate oven (about 350°F). Makes about 8 muffins.

## Doughnuts

### Doughnuts

*Mrs. J. L. Stewart. · Original printed p. 41, scan p. 46 · `mg1930-p046-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup sugar
- 1 Brookfield egg
- 1 tbsp. melted Silverleaf lard
- 1 cup sour milk
- 1 tsp. soda (in sour milk)
- 1 tbsp. vanilla
- ¼ tsp. nutmeg
- 3 cups flour (or enough to handle)

**Preparation**

handle)

Beat egg, add sugar, then melted lard, and other ingredients in order given. Add flour gradually, using just enough to make a soft dough. Roll a small portion out on board, and cut with a doughnut cutter. Fry in deep Jewel Shortening or " Silverleaf" Lard.

**Variation: Roll dough ¼ inch thick. Cut in strips one inch**

wide and about two inches long. Wrap each strip around a date which has been stuffed with a pecan nutmeat, and fry in deep Jewel Shortening or " Silverleaf " Lard.

---

Put together in order named and add only enough flour to handle.

- vanilla

### Drop Date Cookies

### Raisin Filled Cookies

*Mrs. J. W. Spalsbury. · Original printed p. 52, scan p. 57 · `mg1930-p057-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- [Batter]
- 1½ cups brown sugar
- 1 cup shortening
- 3/4 cup sour milk
- 1 tsp. soda
- 1 tsp. cinnamon
- 1 tsp. salt
- flour
- [Filling]
- 1 cup chopped raisins
- 1 cup sugar
- ½ cup water
- 1 tbsp. flour

**Preparation**

Mix and cook until thick. For dough, cream butter and sugar, add milk and sifted dry ingredients with flour enough to make a dough that can be rolled out. Cut cookies and place 1 tsp. of filling on cookie and place another cookie on top and press edges together. Bake on greased pan in moderate oven.

---

### Sugar Cookies

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 52, scan p. 57 · `mg1930-p057-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup butter ( scant)
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- 1 cup sugar
- 1 tsp. baking powder
- 2 eggs
- flour to make a dough to roll
- 1 tbsp. water
- thin on board

**Preparation**

Cream butter and sugar, beat eggs until light, add to creamed mixture, beat well, add water and extract. To 1/2 cup sifted flour add baking powder and sift into other ingredients and beat until light. Add about 2 level cups of flour or enough for soft dough. Turn on to a floured board and knead lightly, roll out very thin and cut with cookie cutter and bake in a quick oven (400 ° ) for ten minutes. These cookies are as good at two weeks as when baked. One tsp. of crushed caraway seeds may be substituted for vanilla. Sufficient for 50 cookies. They are delicious when filled with the following filling: 12 package of dates, one cup black walnut meats, one cup sugar and one cup water, and two tbsp. flour. Mix flour, sugar, nuts and dates together, then add water and cook until thick. Let cool before putting between cookies. Chill dough before rolling.

- 3 eggs
- 1 tsp. baking powder

- 1 tsp. nutmeg

**Preparation**

Cream sugar and fat; beat in eggs and milk. Sift together flour as needed, soda, baking powder and nutmeg, and work in enough flour to make a soft rollable dough. Chill briefly, roll ¼ inch thick, cut into shapes and place on a greased baking sheet. Bake at 375°F for about 12–15 minutes.

## Cakes Without Butter

### Angel Food

*Mrs. J. L. Stewart. · Original printed p. 59, scan p. 64 · `mg1930-p064-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 12 egg whites
- 1½ cups sifted sugar
- 1 cup Swansdown cake flour (sifted once before measuring, then sifted four times)
- pinch salt
- 1 tsp. cream of tartar
- ½ tsp. each almond, vanilla, and lemon extract

**Preparation**

Beat egg whites with salt until frothy. Add cream of tartar and beat until it stands alone, but not until dry. Fold in sugar gradually, add extract, and fold in flour. Pour into ungreased angel food tin, and place in cold oven. Increase heat gradually every fifteen minutes, for one hour, when cake should be done. Temp. 300 degrees.

---

### Angel Cake

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 59, scan p. 64 · `mg1930-p064-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup fine granulated sugar
- pinch salt
- ½ cup water
- 3/4 cup flour
- 1 tsp. cream of tartar
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- 1 cup egg whites

**Preparation**

To the sugar add water and 1/4 tsp. cream of tartar. Stir until sugar is dissolved, and boil until a little tried in cold water forms a firm ball ( 250 ° F. ). While the syrup is cooking beat the egg whites until stiff and dry, adding the salt before beating. Pour the syrup slowly on to the whites, beating constantly, and beat until cool. Add vanilla. Fold in the flour, which has been sifted several times with the remainder of the cream of tartar. Turn into an ungreased tube pan, place in a cold oven, and raise temperature gradually to 300°. Time in combining, 30 minutes; time in cooking, 1 hour 10 minutes. Temperature, 300°. Recipe makes loaf in tube pan 8 inches in diameter.

---

### Economy Angel Food Cake

*Mrs. R. Mickey. · Original printed p. 59, scan p. 64 · `mg1930-p064-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 3/4 cup chilled egg whites
- 3/4 tsp. cream tartar
- 1/4 cup ice cold water
- 1 cup sugar
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- 1/4 cup prepared cake flour
- ¼ tsp. almond or rose extract

**Preparation**

Break eggs into a measuring cup until 3/4 full. Fill cup with cold water. Pour on a large platter, add salt and beat with a flat wire egg beater until foamy. Add cream of tartar and beat egg whites until stiff but not dry. Fold in the sugar one tbsp. at a time. Add flavoring. Have flour previously sifted, measured and sifted again three times. Fold flour in in the same manner as sugar. Bake in an angel cake tin in a slow oven ( 315 ° ) from 50 to 60 minutes.

---

### Angel Food Cake

- 1 level tsp. cream of tartar
- 1½ cups granulated sugar
- 1 cup flour
- vanilla (amount not specified)

Beat egg whites lightly, adding cream of tartar when partly beaten. The source lists sugar, flour and vanilla without specifying how to incorporate them. Bake in a moderate oven at first, increasing the heat near the end, about 45 minutes.

- 1 cup milk
- 2 egg whites, beaten stiff

**Preparation**

Sift dry ingredients 3 times. Add scalded milk while hot. Fold in beaten whites. Bake slowly in an ungreased angel food pan. Remove from oven, invert pan, put wet cloth over whole pan until cake leaves sides of pan.

---

### Yellow Angel Food Cake

*Mrs. C. L. Miller. · Original printed p. 60, scan p. 65 · `mg1930-p065-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ cups sugar, sifted twice
- 4 egg yolks
- 1 tbsp. cold water
- ½ cup boiling water
- 1½ cups flour, sifted twice
- ½ tsp. cream of tartar
- 4 egg whites, beaten stiff

**Preparation**

Beat the sugar, egg yolks and cold water 10 minutes. Add the boiling water, sifted flour and cream of tartar. Fold in the beaten egg whites. Bake in an ungreased and unfloured pan for 50–60 minutes in a slow oven. Leave in pan until cold.

---

### Yellow Cake

*Mrs. J. B. Fleugel. · Original printed p. 60, scan p. 65 · `mg1930-p065-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 7 egg yolks, beaten stiff
- 1½ cups water
- 1 tsp. cream of tartar
- 1 tsp. vanilla or lemon flavoring
- 1¼ cups sugar
- 7 egg whites, beaten slightly
- 1 cup sifted flour

**Preparation**

Boil water and sugar until it hairs, pour hot syrup over egg yolks and let stand. Beat whites of eggs with cream of tartar until stiff but not dry. Add to yolks alternately with flour sifted 3 times. Bake in angel food pan that has been rinsed in cold water but not dried. Bake in warm oven about 55 minutes, about 325 degrees F. Turn upside down on rack and cake will come out.

---

### Fruit Angel Food Cake

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 61, scan p. 66 · `mg1930-p066-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1¼ cups egg whites
- 1¼ cups sugar
- 1 cup cake flour
- 1 tsp. cream of tartar
- ¼ tsp. salt
- 1 tsp. almond extract
- ¼ cup chopped nutmeats
- ¼ cup candied cherries, cut fine

**Preparation**

Beat eggs until foamy, add cream of tartar and salt. Beat until stiff but not dry, add sugar 2 or 3 tbsp. at a time and beat. Do this until all sugar has been used up. Then sprinkle all the flour carefully over the top, and fold into the batter. Add extract just as carefully. Pour 1½ batter into tube pan, then sprinkle the cherries and nuts over this, then add the rest of the batter. Run a knife to the bottom of the pan, and gently mix the fruit all around. Start cake in a cold oven, and bake 60 minutes from the time it reaches 300 degrees, and then 15 minutes at 325 degrees.

---

### Chocolate Angel Food Cake

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 61, scan p. 66 · `mg1930-p066-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1¼ cups egg whites
- 1¼ cups sugar
- ¾ cup cake flour
- ¼ cup cocoa
- 1 tsp. cream of tartar
- ¼ tsp. salt
- 1 tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Beat egg whites until foamy, add cream of tartar and salt and beat until stiff enough to make a dog ear when you lift out your whip. Add sugar 2 or 3 tbsp. at a time and beat with your whip until all has been used. Then sprinkle in all of the flour carefully so as not to break down the beaten whites. Fold in the flour. Bake in tube pan at 300 degrees for 1 hour, then for 15 minutes longer at 325 degrees. Start cake in a cold oven. Count the time from the time the temperature reaches 300 degrees. Never use a Dover egg beater, use a flat wire whip.

---

### Sunshine Cake

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 61, scan p. 66 · `mg1930-p066-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 eggs
- 1 cup sugar
- 1⅓ tsp. cream of tartar
- ⅔ cup flour
- ¼ tsp. salt
- 1 tsp. vanilla or lemon extract

**Preparation**

Beat yolks of eggs until thick and lemon color. Beat whites until stiff. Into these whites put cream of tartar and salt. Add sugar, beaten yolks and flavoring. Last sift flour four times before measuring and then fold into the mixture. Bake in an angel cake tin for 45 minutes in a slow oven at 325 °. Invert tins when cooling.

---

### Cream Sponge Cake

*Mrs. R. Mickey. · Original printed p. 61, scan p. 66 · `mg1930-p066-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- Yolks of 4 eggs
- 1 cup sugar
- 3 tbsp. cold water
- 1½ tbsp. cornstarch
- Flour, enough to fill the measuring cup containing the cornstarch and baking powder
- 1¼ tsp. baking powder
- ¼ tsp. salt
- Whites of 4 eggs
- 1 tsp. lemon extract

**Preparation**

Beat egg yolks with cold water until thick and lemon-colored. Add sugar gradually; beat two minutes. Place cornstarch in a cup and fill with flour, together with baking powder and salt. Add to the egg mixture. Fold in stiffly beaten egg whites and add lemon extract. Bake 30 minutes in a moderate oven (325°F). The source recommends the cake for whipped-cream pies.

---

### Sponge Cake Using Yolks Only

*Mrs. C. D. Brown. · Original printed p. 62, scan p. 67 · `mg1930-p067-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 egg yolks
- 1 cup sugar
- ½ cup boiling water
- ½ tsp. salt
- 2 tsp. baking powder
- 1½ cups flour, sifted before measuring
- 1 tsp. flavoring

**Preparation**

Beat yolks until light with dover egg beater; add sugar gradually then hot water, beating meanwhile. Add sifted flour with baking powder and salt and heat thoroughly. Bake in moderate oven 45 minutes or in 2 layers about 25 minutes. Put layers together with jelly, caramel or cream filling.

---

### Sponge Cake

*Mrs. J. W. Spalsbury. · Original printed p. 62, scan p. 67 · `mg1930-p067-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 egg yolks
- 1 cup sugar
- 3 tbsp. cold water
- 1½ tbsp. cornstarch
- 1¼ tsp. baking powder
- Flour, enough to fill measuring cup with cornstarch and baking powder
- ½ tsp. salt
- 4 egg whites
- 1 tsp. lemon flavoring

**Preparation**

Mix ingredients in order given. Place cornstarch and baking powder in cup and fill with flour, sifting several times. Bake in moderate oven, 350 to 325 degrees.

---

### Hot Water Sponge Cake

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 62, scan p. 67 · `mg1930-p067-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 egg yolks
- 1 tsp. baking powder
- 1 cup sugar
- 2 tbsp. hot water
- 1 cup flour
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- 1 tbsp. lemon juice
- 3 egg whites

**Preparation**

Beat yolks until lemon colored and thick, add sugar and beat. Add flour sifted with baking powder and salt. Stir in hot water and fold in whites beaten stiff. Flavor with lemon juice or extract. Bake at 325 ° for 45 minutes.

---

### Cocoanut Sponge Cake

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 62, scan p. 67 · `mg1930-p067-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 eggs
- 2 cups flour
- 1½ cups sugar
- ½ tsp. vanilla
- ½ cup boiling water
- ½ tsp. lemon extract
- 2 tsp. baking powder

**Preparation**

Beat eggs until light with dover beater; add sugar gradually and continue beating. Add boiling water, then flour which has been sifted, then measured and baking powder added, then sifted 3 times. Add flavoring. Beat all with dover beater and bake in two layer tins in a medium oven. Put together with any good custard with cocoanut added. Ice with boiled icing and sprinkle with cocoanut.

## Cakes With Butter

### Apple Sauce Cake

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 65, scan p. 72 · `mg1930-p072-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup sugar
- 1 tsp. soda
- ½ cup butter
- 2 cups flour
- 1 cup apple sauce
- ½ cup seeded raisins
- 1 tsp. each cloves, cinnamon and nutmeg

**Preparation**

Cream butter and sugar. Mix soda in apple sauce and add to butter and sugar. Mix and sift flour and spices reserving a little flour to mix with raisins. Add flour and spices and raisins last. Bake in loaf tin 45 minutes at 350°.

---

### Apple Sauce Cake

*Mrs. J. B. Fleugel. · Original printed p. 65, scan p. 72 · `mg1930-p072-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup brown sugar
- 1 cup cold unsweetened applesauce
- 1½ tsp. cinnamon
- salt
- ¼ cup butter
- 1 scant tsp. soda
- 1 cup raisins
- 1¾ cups flour

**Preparation**

Cream sugar and butter, add applesauce, flour and fruit. Bake in greased pan in slow oven.

---

### Apple Sauce Cake

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 65, scan p. 72 · `mg1930-p072-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup brown sugar
- 1 tsp. soda
- 2 tbsp. fat
- ½ tsp. cinnamon
- 1 cup applesauce
- ½ tsp. cloves
- ⅔ cup raisins
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- 2 cups flour
- 1/4 tsp. nutmeg

**Preparation**

Heat oven to 325°F and grease an 8-inch square cake pan. Cream fat with brown sugar. Stir baking soda into applesauce, then add to sugar mixture. Whisk flour, cinnamon, cloves, nutmeg and salt, and fold into wet mixture. Stir in raisins; spread in pan and bake 35–45 minutes, until cake tests clean.

---

### Brownies

*Mrs. Harry Becker. · Original printed p. 65, scan p. 72 · `mg1930-p072-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup sugar
- 3/4 tsp. vanilla
- 1/4 cup melted butter
- ½ cup pastry flour
- 1 egg unbeaten
- ½ cup walnut meats cut in pieces
- 2 sq. Baker's chocolate melted

**Preparation**

(bitter)

Mix ingredients in the order given. Line a seven inch square pan with paraffin paper. Spread evenly, and bake in a slow oven. Turn from the pan, remove the paper, and cut the cake into strips, using a sharp knife.

### Brownies

- 1 scant cup sugar
- ½ cup melted butter
- 1/2 cup flour
- 2 sq. melted chocolate
- 1 cup nut meats
- salt
- vanilla

**Preparation**

Mix eggs and sugar, add flour, melted butter and chocolate and nuts. Pour out 1/4 inch deep in greased pan and bake in a very slow oven. Cut in squares as soon as taken from the oven.

---

### Brownies

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 66, scan p. 73 · `mg1930-p073-r02`*

- 2 cups sugar
- 2 sq. chocolate
- 1 cup butter
- 1 cup nuts
- 4 eggs
- Flavoring
- 1 cup flour

**Preparation**



1. Cream sugar and butter.

2. Add well beaten eggs.

3. Add sifted flour, melted chocolate and nutmeats which have been broken.

4. Spread on cookie sheet.

5. Cut in strips when baked. Time, 15 to 20 minutes. Temperature 400° F. ( Moderately hot oven). Amount, 2 dozen average sized strips.

Cream butter, add sugar and mix well. Add beaten eggs, then flour, cocoa, and nutmeats. Bake slowly about twenty minutes. To be baked in a cake pan and cut in squares before cold.

Cream butter and sugar and add beaten eggs, then milk and flour sifted with baking powder, and add vanilla last. Bake in sheet or layers, 30 minutes at 350 degrees.

### Chocolate Cake

*Mrs. Robert Caine. · Original printed p. 67, scan p. 74 · `mg1930-p074-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup shortening, scant
- 1 cup brown sugar
- 1 egg
- 2 tbsp. cocoa dissolved in ½ cup boiling water
- ½ cup sour milk
- ½ tsp. soda
- 1½ cups flour

**Preparation**

Heat oven to 350°F and grease an 8-inch square cake pan. Dissolve cocoa in boiling water and cool. Cream shortening and brown sugar, beat in egg, then stir in cocoa. Sift baking soda with flour and add alternately with sour milk. Bake 25–35 minutes, until a tester comes out clean.

### Sweet Milk Chocolate Cake

- 1 egg
- 2 cups flour
- 1½ cups powdered sugar
- ⅔ cup milk
- 3 tsp. baking powder
- For chocolate paste: 2 squares chocolate
- For chocolate paste: ⅓ cup milk
- For chocolate paste: ⅓ cup powdered sugar
- vanilla

**Preparation**

Cream butter and sugar. Add egg and milk, then sifted dry ingredients. Separately melt the chocolate with the milk, powdered sugar, and vanilla to make a paste. Combine the two mixtures and bake in two layers in a moderate oven.

---

### Devil's Food

*Mrs. Herman Johnson. · Original printed p. 68, scan p. 75 · `mg1930-p075-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup brown sugar
- ½ cup butter, softened
- 1 egg
- 1 cup all-purpose flour
- Pinch of salt
- ½ cup sour milk
- ½ tsp. baking soda
- 2 rounded tbsp. cocoa powder
- ¼ cup boiling water

**Preparation**

Heat oven to 350°F and grease an 8-inch round or square cake pan. Blend cocoa with boiling water and set aside to cool slightly. Cream butter and brown sugar until light, then beat in the egg. Whisk flour, salt and baking soda separately. Add dry ingredients alternately with sour milk; mix in cocoa. Pour into the pan and bake 25–35 minutes, or until a tester inserted at the center comes out clean. Cool before frosting.

### Devil's Food Cake

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 68, scan p. 75 · `mg1930-p075-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup shortening
- 1 tsp. vanilla

- 1 level tsp. soda

- 2 sq. melted chocolate
- 1 tsp. salt
- (each sq. one ounce)

**Preparation**



1. Cream sugar and shortening together

2. Add the well beaten eggs.

3. Sift flour, salt and soda together.

4. Add to the above mixture alternately with the milk.

5. Add melted chocolate.

6. Add vanilla.

7. Mix well and pour into well greased oblong pans or layer pans. Time: Bake 40 minutes. Temperature: 350° F. Size of pan: layer pans, 9 inch diameter; oblong, 9x16 in. Amount: Layer, 16 servings; oblong pan, 32 servings, 2 in. square.

Cream shortening and add sugar. Add beaten eggs, then add alternately the sour milk and flour into which the baking powder has been sifted. Either loaf or layer. Bake at 350° for 30 minutes.

### Hot Water Chocolate Cake

### Simple Chocolate Two Layer Cake

- 1 egg

- 1 cup sour milk
- 1 tsp. vanilla

After creaming sugar and butter together, add cocoa, cream. add egg beaten light. Then add sour milk and soda dissolved in the boiling water. Into the flour, put the baking powder, and then sift into the batter. The flour should be measured after sifting. After adding vanilla, beat hard, then place in two tins for layer cake. ### English White Fruit Cake

### Feather Cake

### Fruit Cake

- 1 tsp. cinnamon
- 1½ cups grated apple
- 1 tsp. cloves
- 1 tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Cream butter, add sugar, egg. Sift and mix dry ingredients, and add to first. Add raisins, nutmeats and citron, cut up and dredged with a little flour. Apple may be ground in food grinder. Add vanilla.

Add the well beaten eggs the last thing before baking. Bake in a large square pan 45 minutes at 350°.

### Gold Medal Cake

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 74, scan p. 81 · `mg1930-p081-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup shortening
- 1 tsp. flavoring
- 1 cup sugar
- 3/4 cup milk
- 2 eggs
- 2 cups Gold Medal flour
- 1/8 tsp. salt
- 3 tsp. baking powder
- 1 tbsp. water

**Preparation**



1. Cream shortening and sugar together.

2. Add the yolks, which have been beaten until light, with the water and flavoring.

3. Mix well.

4. Sift the dry ingredients together, and add to the first mix-ture alternately with the milk.

5. Fold in the beaten egg whites to which the salt has been added before beating.

6. May be baked as a loaf cake or in two layers. Time: Bake 40 minutes for loaf cake, 20 minutes for layer cake. Temperature: 325° F. for loaf cake and 375° for layer cake. Size of pan: Layer pans 9 inch diameter, oblong pan 9x16 in. Amount: 16 servings.

- pinch salt

- 1/4 cup milk
- ½ tsp. orange extract

**Preparation**

Cream butter and sugar, add yolks of eggs well beaten. Sift flour, baking powder and salt together, and add alternately with milk to egg mixture. Stir in extract and turn into a well greased and floured small loaf pan. Bake in moderate oven 350° for 30 minutes.

### Grandma's Special Cake (Large Cake)

*Mrs. Geo. Williamson. · Original printed p. 74, scan p. 81 · `mg1930-p081-r03`*

- 3 cups flour
- 2 heaping tsp. baking powder
- 1 tsp. orange extract

**Preparation**

Heat oven to 350°F and prepare a large tube pan or two 9-inch cake pans. Cream butter with granulated sugar. Mix cornstarch, flour and baking powder; add alternately with milk or cream. Fold in stiffly beaten egg whites, powdered sugar and orange extract gently. Bake approximately 35–50 minutes, depending on pan depth, until cake tests clean.

- For filling: 2 cups powdered sugar
- For filling: 2 tbsp. cream
- For filling: 1 tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Cream butter and sugar. Add beaten egg yolks and milk. Add sifted flour and baking powder; beat well. Add flavoring and fold in beaten egg whites. Bake in a 9- or 10-inch square sheet pan about 35 minutes at 350°F. Cool and cut into 1×2-inch bars.

### Pineapple Butter Scotch Cake

- For pan: 3 tbsp. butter

**Preparation**



1. Sift dry ingredients.

2. Cream sugar and shortening together.

3. Add well beaten eggs.

4. Add milk alternately with the sifted dry ingredients.

5. Add the vanilla and mix well. Preparation of pan: Grease a round pudding pan, line with brown sugar about 1/2 inch deep. Dot with 3 tbsp. butter. Place in the pan slices of pineapple with maraschino cherry in center of each.

### Pecan Christmas Cake

**Preparation**

Cream butter and sugar. Add beaten egg yolks. Then pour brandy on slowly. Add flour which has been sifted three times with nutmeg, raisins and nuts which have been dredged with some flour. Then fold in the beaten egg whites. This is a most delicious cake and will keep indefinitely. Should be baked in two tube pans for 60 minutes at 300 degrees.

### Hot Water Spice Cake

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 77, scan p. 84 · `mg1930-p084-r02`*

- 2 cups flour
- and lemon extract
- ½ cup chopped raisins
- 1 tsp. soda
- 1 cup boiling water

**Preparation**

Cream sugar and melted butter together, and add eggs beaten until thick and lemon colored. Then add Karo and molasses and chopped raisins. Sift flour and spices and add to above mixture, beating well as it is added. Last add boiling water to which soda has been dissolved. Bake in 3 layer tins in moderate oven, 350° for 25 minutes. Put together with white boiled frosting.

### Spice Cake

*Esther Winters. · Original printed p. 77, scan p. 84 · `mg1930-p084-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup sugar
- ½ cup softened butter
- 3 eggs (use 3 yolks and 1 white in the cake; reserve 2 whites for frosting)
- 1 generous cup sour milk
- 1 scant tsp. baking soda
- 2 cups flour
- 1 tsp. cinnamon
- 1 tsp. ground cloves
- 1 tsp. nutmeg
- 1 tsp. allspice
- 1 tsp. baking powder

**Preparation**

Heat oven to 350°F. Grease a 9-inch cake pan or two 8-inch layer pans. Cream butter and sugar. Beat in three egg yolks and one egg white, saving the remaining two whites for frosting. Stir baking soda into sour milk. Whisk flour, baking powder and spices; add to batter in alternating additions with sour milk. Bake about 30–40 minutes in one pan, or 22–30 minutes in two layer pans, until a tester comes out clean. Cool and frost with an icing made using the reserved whites.

### Chocolate Cream Icing

- Part II: 1 tbsp. water

**Preparation**

Part I: Boil sugar, water and cream of tartar until a little syrup forms a soft ball in water. Add butter and vanilla, and cool without stirring. Beat until creamy and spread on cake.

- ½ cup water

**Preparation**



1. Cook together sugar, corn syrup and water until syrup spins a thread. (242° F. )

2. Pour the hot syrup slowly into the beaten egg whites, beat-ing during the addition.

3. Add vanilla and continue beating until the mixture will hold its shape. For Chocolate Comfort Icing, add two squares (each sq. one oz.) of melted chocolate to frosting during final beating.

---

### Fluffy Boiled Icing

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 87, scan p. 94 · `mg1930-p094-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 egg white
- 1 cup sugar
- Water enough to wet sugar thoroughly.
- Flavoring.

**Preparation**

Boil sugar and water until it forms a soft ball in cold water. Beat the egg white until stiff, and add syrup gradually, beating with the dover egg beater until it is the right consistency to spread on cake. Add flavoring.

---

### Frosting For Cookies

*Mrs. J. L. Stewart. · Original printed p. 87, scan p. 94 · `mg1930-p094-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ squares chocolate
- 1 cup powdered sugar
- Boiling water, enough to make a medium-thin icing
- 1 tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Melt chocolate. Gradually stir in powdered sugar, adding sufficient boiling water to make a medium-thin icing. Let cool, add vanilla and beat.

---

### Icing

*Mrs. Robert Caine. · Original printed p. 87, scan p. 94 · `mg1930-p094-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lb. powdered sugar
- 1 heaping tbsp. butter
- 3 tbsp. cocoa
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- Hot coffee, enough to thin

**Preparation**

Mix powdered sugar, butter, cocoa and vanilla; thin with hot coffee.

---

### Inch High Icing

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 87, scan p. 94 · `mg1930-p094-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 tsp. granulated gelatine
- 1/4 cup hot water
- 3 tsp. cold water
- 2 egg whites
- 1 cup sugar
- ½ tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Soak gelatine in cold water and dissolve over steam. Dissolve sugar in hot water and boil until the syrup will spin a thread. Strain gelatine into syrup. Have egg whites beaten very stiff on a platter, add syrup slowly, beating constantly with a wooden spoon. When icing is so thick that it holds to shape, it is ready for use.

---

### Lady Baltimore Icing

*Mrs. Paul Snauble. · Original printed p. 88, scan p. 95 · `mg1930-p095-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- Boiled icing (quantity not specified)
- ½ cup chopped raisins
- ½ cup chopped walnut meats

**Preparation**

To boiled icing add ½ cup chopped raisins, ½ cup chopped walnut meats, and 6 figs cut in shreds.

---

### Maple Frosting

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 88, scan p. 95 · `mg1930-p095-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup maple or coffee C sugar
- ½ cup thin cream

**Preparation**

Boil fifteen minutes, take from fire and stir until it thickens. Spread immediately.

---

### Nut Caramel Frosting

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 88, scan p. 95 · `mg1930-p095-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1/2 cup butter
- ½ cup milk
- 1 tbsp. flour
- 1/2 cup nutmeats
- 2 cups sugar
- ½ tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Cream together butter, 13/4 cups of the sugar, and flour. Add milk and cook until a little tried in cold water forms a soft ball. Caramelize the remaining quarter cup of sugar, and add carefully to the first mixture. Bring to boiling point, cool to lukewarm, add nuts and vanilla and beat until thick. Time in cooking, 30 minutes. Temperature, 238 °. Recipe covers cake 9x9.

---

### Rich Icing

*Mrs. Harris Meyer. · Original printed p. 88, scan p. 95 · `mg1930-p095-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup powdered sugar free from lumps.

**Preparation**

Add cocoa to give desired color and enough butter to cream well. Thin to desired consistency with cream and flavor with vanilla.

---

### Seven Minute Icing

*Mrs. J. L. Stewart. · Original printed p. 88, scan p. 95 · `mg1930-p095-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 egg whites
- 4 tbsp. cold water
- 1 cup sugar
- 1 tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Have water in lower part of double boiler boiling. Place the egg whites, water and sugar in double boiler and beat with Dover egg beater constantly for 7 minutes. Remove from stove and add vanilla.

---

### Seven Minute Icing

*Mrs. Walter Graham. · Original printed p. 88, scan p. 95 · `mg1930-p095-r06`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 egg white
- 3 tbsp. cold water
- 7/8 cup sugar
- vanilla

**Preparation**

Put unbeaten egg white, sugar and water in double boiler over fire and beat with Dover egg beater exactly seven minutes. Remove from fire add vanilla and spread on cake.

## Confections

### Candied Orange Peel

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 91, scan p. 98 · `mg1930-p098-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 oranges
- 1/2 cup water
- 1 cup sugar

**Preparation**

Remove peel from oranges in quarters. Cover with cold water (pinch of salt) and cook until soft, drain. With a spoon remove white portion and cut yellow portion into thin strips with scissors. Boil water and sugar until syrup spins a thread. Cook peel in syrup 5 minutes, drain, and coat with fine granulated sugar.

---

### Butter Scotch

*Mrs. Carl R. Johnson. · Original printed p. 91, scan p. 98 · `mg1930-p098-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup of white sugar
- 1 tbsp. of butter
- ½ cup of water
- 1 tsp. of vinegar
- a little nutmeg

**Preparation**

Boil until thick, then pour on buttered plate, and cut in squares, when cool. Peanuts stirred in, greatly improve the candy.

---

### Caramels

*Mrs. R. Mickey. · Original printed p. 91, scan p. 98 · `mg1930-p098-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups granulated sugar
- 1¾ cups light Karo syrup
- 2 cups top milk
- ½ cup butter
- 1 tbsp. vanilla
- 1 cup nuts

**Preparation**

Mix and bring to boiling point. Add slowly 2 cups top milk. Stir until it boils. Add 1/2 cup butter and cook to quite hard ball. (250° ). Add 1 tbsp. vanilla and 1 cup nuts. Pour into greased tins and cut when cool.

---

### Chocolate Orange Cream Wafers

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 91, scan p. 98 · `mg1930-p098-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- ¼ lb. bitter chocolate
- ¼ lb. sweet chocolate
- 1¼ cups confectioners' sugar
- 3/4 cup finely cut crystallized orange peel

**Preparation**

Melt the bitter chocolate in a double boiler and spread with a knife on heavy waxed paper on which has been drawn an oblong six inches by twelve inches. To the confectioners sugar add one teaspoon grated orange peel and two tablespoons juice ( enough to moisten the sugar so that it will spread without running). Add the crystallized orange peel and when the oblong of chocolate is hard spread on it the orange mixture as evenly as possible. Melt the sweet chocolate and spread on another sheet of waxed paper, making an oblong the same size as the first. Turn the first sheet face

down on this melted chocolate and press with the hand over the wrong side of the paper. When the chocolate is hard pull off the papers and cut the candy in squares to serve as a confection. This is really a very simple process, and the wafers are delicious, something one cannot buy at any candy shop.

---

### De Leon Creams

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 92, scan p. 99 · `mg1930-p099-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups sugar
- 1/4 tsp. cream of tartar
- 1 cup water
- ½ cup candied orange peel

**Preparation**

Put sugar, water, and cream of tartar in kettle on the stove. Stir gently until sugar is dissolved. Cook to soft ball stage (238 degrees F). Turn out on oiled platter (do not scrape kettle). Leave to cool until it will show the imprint of the finger. Beat until creamy. Work in the finely chopped orange peel, let stand until quite cool. Form into rolls about one inch thick. Roll in more chopped peel and slice. Set in a cool place before serving.

---

### Creole Pralines

*Mrs. Lawrence Fuller. · Original printed p. 92, scan p. 99 · `mg1930-p099-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups maple syrup
- ½ cup pecan halves

**Preparation**

Boil syrup until it forms a thread from tip of spoon. Remove from fire, add nuts and stir until it begins to sugar. Drop in flat round shapes on greased paper.

---

### Fudge

*Mrs. E. J. Anderson. · Original printed p. 92, scan p. 99 · `mg1930-p099-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 cups white sugar
- ¼ lb. bitter chocolate (or less, if desired)
- 1 cup water
- 1 tsp. butter
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- Pinch salt

**Preparation**

Cut up chocolate; add water, butter and salt. Cook over low heat, stirring until thick and smooth. Remove from heat and thoroughly stir in sugar. Return to heat and cook without stirring to the soft-ball stage when tested in cold water. Let cool completely, add vanilla and beat until creamy.

---

### Soft Chocolate Fudge

*Mrs. James E. Rork. · Original printed p. 92, scan p. 99 · `mg1930-p099-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups granulated sugar
- butter, size of 2 walnuts
- 3/4 cup top milk
- 1 tbsp. vanilla
- 2½ sq. bitter chocolate

**Preparation**

Cook the sugar, milk and chocolate together till it will cling together when tested in cold water. Then add butter and vanilla. Let stand till entirely cold. Beat for about fifteen minutes. Pour on buttered plate and cut in squares.

---

### Fudge

*Mrs. D. Boudeman. · Original printed p. 93, scan p. 100 · `mg1930-p100-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups granulated sugar
- 14 cake of Baker's chocolate
- 1 cup milk
- butter, size of walnut

**Preparation**

When it will harden in cold water, take from fire, stir until it is quite thick, then pour on buttered tins. Be sure to take from fire just the minute it will harden in cold water.

---

### Peanut Butter Fudge

*Mrs. M. E. Clark. · Original printed p. 93, scan p. 100 · `mg1930-p100-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups sugar
- ½ cup milk
- 2 heaping tbsp. peanut butter

**Preparation**

Boil exactly 5 minutes. Remove from the fire and beat until thick.

---

### Orientals

*Mrs. R. Mickey. · Original printed p. 93, scan p. 100 · `mg1930-p100-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- [Chocolate Creams]
- 2½ lbs. sugar
- whites of two eggs
- ½ tsp. glycerine
- dipping chocolate
- 6 drops acetic acid
- 2 cups water
- ½ tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Put sugar and water into kettle and stir well until dissolved. Add glycerine and heat until it boils. Add acid, clean sides of kettle, and boil until it forms a soft ball. Let cool. Pour out. Beat in beaten whites and vanilla. Beat until it sets and mould. Dip into the melted chocolate.

---

### Sea Foam

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 93, scan p. 100 · `mg1930-p100-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 2½ cups sugar
- ½ cup water
- ½ cup Karo syrup
- pinch salt
- 2 egg whites, beaten stiff

**Preparation**

Mix sugar, water, Karo syrup and salt; boil until syrup spins a thread. Pour syrup slowly over stiffly beaten egg whites, beating constantly, until half the syrup is used. Boil remaining syrup until it forms a hard ball in cold water. Beat it into the first mixture and continue beating until the desired consistency.

---

## Frozen Desserts

### Apricot Ice Cream

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 95, scan p. 102 · `mg1930-p102-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 large can apricots
- 3 cups sugar
- 2 oranges
- 1 pt. milk, top
- 2 lemons
- ½ pt. heavy cream, whipped

**Preparation**

Add juice of oranges and lemons to apricots. Add sugar to milk and cook until dissolved, cool and freeze to consistency of mush. Add fruit and continue freezing. Add whipped cream and finish freezing.

---

### Baked Alaska

*Mrs. James Rork. · Original printed p. 95, scan p. 102 · `mg1930-p102-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 thin slice sponge cake, per serving
- 1 slice firm ice cream, per serving
- 2 egg whites
- ¼ cup powdered sugar

**Preparation**

Thin slice of cake placed flat on dessert plate. Place slice of ice cream on top. Prepare meringue of egg whites and powdered sugar. Place meringue on ice cream and place in hot oven until brown.

---

### Baked Individual Alaska

*Mrs. Lawrence Fuller. · Original printed p. 95, scan p. 102 · `mg1930-p102-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 egg whites
- 1 cup sugar
- 1 qt. brick ice cream
- 1 sponge cake

**Preparation**

Beat eggs adding sugar slowly until very stiff. Heat broiling oven very hot. Cut cake into individual servings, place on plates. On cake place a square of ice cream and cover with meringue. Put in oven for a moment to brown. Serve immediately.

---

### Frozen Bisque

*Mrs. E. Ray Winters. · Original printed p. 95, scan p. 102 · `mg1930-p102-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pt. whipping cream
- 6 oatmeal or graham crackers
- 2 eggs
- ¼ cup cocoanut
- 3/4 cup powdered sugar
- 1½ tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Whip cream, add egg yolks well beaten, sugar, crackers rolled fine, cocoanut and vanilla. Fold in stiffly beaten egg whites. Put in freezer, or mold, pack in ice and salt and let stand 2 hours.

---

### Cold Dessert

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 95, scan p. 102 · `mg1930-p102-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- Chocolate ice cream
- Chocolate sauce
- Vanilla ice cream
- Strawberry ice cream, or whipped cream
- Cherry, for garnish
- For pink sauce: 1 cup sugar
- For pink sauce: ½ cup water
- Pink coloring, as needed

**Preparation**

Layer a scoop of chocolate ice cream with chocolate sauce, vanilla ice cream and pink sauce in tall glasses. Add a scoop of strawberry ice cream, or top with whipped cream and a cherry.

For pink sauce, boil sugar with water and color the syrup pink. Let stand 24 hours before using. Fresh orange, banana, pineapple or berries may be added just before serving.

---

### Grape Fruit Sherbet

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 96, scan p. 103 · `mg1930-p103-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 qt. boiling water
- 1 orange, 1 lemon, white 1 egg
- 2 cups sugar
- 2 level tsp. powdered sugar
- 2 large, or 3 smaller grape fruit

**Preparation**

Time: Preparation and freezing, 50 minutes. Number served: 10 to 12 persons.



Chip the yellow rind, not taking any of the white pulp, from half a grape fruit; add this and the sugar to the water, boil five minutes; cool; add the juice of the fruit; strain into a freezer and freeze. Beat egg to a stiff froth, add powdered sugar; add to the frozen mixture; beat; repack until ready to use.

---

### Ice Cream

*Mrs. C. V. Pierce. · Original printed p. 96, scan p. 103 · `mg1930-p103-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 qt. whole milk
- 1 egg
- 1 pt. cream
- 2 tbsp. cornstarch
- 1 cup sugar
- pinch salt
- vanilla

**Preparation**

Mix sugar, cornstarch, egg, and moisten with a little cold milk. Add to scalded milk and cook until it thickens. When cold, strain and add cream and vanilla to taste. Freeze. For chocolate ice cream, add 2 squares Baker's Chocolate to Custard when cooking. For strawberry ice cream, add 1 qt. crushed berries, with 1 cup sugar. Add when cream begins to freeze. For peach ice cream, add about 3 or 4 cups crushed peaches, sweetened to taste, when cream begins to freeze.

---

### Lemon Ice Cream

*Mrs. Paul Snauble. · Original printed p. 96, scan p. 103 · `mg1930-p103-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 qt. fresh milk
- 2 cups granulated sugar
- grated rind 1 lemon

**Preparation**

Scald and let cool thoroughly. Add:1 pt. cream. When half frozen add 1/2 cup lemon juice, and finish freezing.

---

### Lady Fingers — Ice Cream

*Mrs. James Rork. · Original printed p. 96, scan p. 103 · `mg1930-p103-r04`*

- 2–3 ladyfingers per serving
- 1 large scoop ice cream per serving
- Sweetened whipped cream, optionally tinted

**Preparation**

ICE CREAM

Place large spoonful ice cream between two or three lady fingers. Top with colored whipped cream and nuts and cherry. Serve on lace doilies.

---

### Maple Mousse

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 96, scan p. 103 · `mg1930-p103-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup maple syrup
- ½ pt. cream, whipped
- salt
- 2 egg whites,beaten stiff
- 1 tbsp. gelatine

**Preparation**

Let syrup and gelatine come to a boil. Take from fire, and cool. When it begins to set, add whipped cream and whites. Mold and pack in ice and salt. Serve with whipped cream.

---

### Maple Parfait

*Mrs. Paul Snauble. · Original printed p. 97, scan p. 104 · `mg1930-p104-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 eggs
- 1 cup hot maple syrup
- 1 pt. whipping cream

**Preparation**

Beat eggs slightly; pour on slowly, the maple syrup and cook in double boiler until mixture thickens. Cool and add cream, whipped stiff. Mold and pack in ice and salt, and let stand for 3 or 4 hours.

---

### Maple Parfait

*Mrs. James Rork. · Original printed p. 97, scan p. 104 · `mg1930-p104-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 eggs
- 1 cup hot maple syrup
- 1 pint thick cream
- salt

**Preparation**

Beat eggs slightly, pour syrup on slowly. Cook in double boiler until thick. Freeze in icebox tray.

---

### Maple Pecan Mousse

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 97, scan p. 104 · `mg1930-p104-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup maple syrup
- ½ pt. cream whipped stiff
- 2 egg yolks
- 1 cup ground pecans

**Preparation**

Beat eggs well. Add maple syrup. Let this mixture come to a boil, stirring constantly. Take off fire, and let cool. Meanwhile whip cream, and grind nut meats. When syrup is cold, fold all ingredients together. Pour into a covered mold, and pack in a large kettle with ice and salt. In the winter the salt and ice pack is not necessary, as it will freeze nicely out of doors in three or four hours. Before I had a covered mold, I used the top of my double boiler, which worked fine. Serves four.

---

### Orange Ice

*Mrs. Paul Snauble. · Original printed p. 97, scan p. 104 · `mg1930-p104-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 cups water
- 1/4 cup lemon juice
- 2 cups sugar
- grated rind of 2 oranges
- 2 cups orange juice

**Preparation**

Boil water and sugar 20 minutes gently. Add juice and rind. Cool and strain. Freeze. 1 tsp. softened gelatine may be added to boiling syrup, when it is taken from stove.

---

### Peanut Brittle Ice Cream

*Mrs. James Rork. · Original printed p. 97, scan p. 104 · `mg1930-p104-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ lb. peanut brittle ground fine
- 1 tbsp. Knox Gelatine
- 3 tbsp. cold water
- 2 bottles whipped cream

**Preparation**

Soak gelatine in water, melt over hot water. Add whipped cream and peanut brittle and freeze in ice box. Serve with 1 tbsp. maple syrup. Sugar stick candy or after dinner mints may be substituted for peanut brittle.

---

## Icebox Desserts

### Charlotte Russe

*Mrs. J. L. Stewart. · Original printed p. 99, scan p. 106 · `mg1930-p106-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pt. cream
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- 1 tbsp. Knox gelatin
- artificial coloring
- 1 cup powdered sugar

**Preparation**

Soak gelatin in 2 tbsp. of cold water for about 5 minutes. Dissolve in 2 tbsp. hot water. Let stand until cool, but not hard. Whip cream until stiff, add 2 tbsp. of above gelatin, and sugar, vanilla and coloring. Line serving dish with lady fingers, and fll with gelatin mixture. Serves 8.

---

### Fresh Strawberry Charlotte Russe

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 99, scan p. 106 · `mg1930-p106-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ pt. cream, whipped
- 2 egg whites
- 1 envelope sparkling gelatine
- 1 cup powdered sugar
- 1 pt. fresh strawberries, carefully cut up
- ¼ cup boiling hot milk

**Preparation**

Soak gelatine in 1/4 cup cold water. Have cream and eggs beaten, ready for use. Mix sugar with berries. Heat milk and pour over gelatine. Add at once to eggs, cream, and berries. Pour in mold lined with lady fingers, or in individual molds without lady fingers. Garnish with whipped cream and whole berries. Serves 6.

---

### Date And Macaroon Dessert

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 99, scan p. 106 · `mg1930-p106-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pt. milk
- 2 eggs
- ½ cup sugar
- ½ package Knox gelatin
- ½ lb. macaroons
- ½ cup dates
- ½ cup nutmeats

**Preparation**

Place milk, egg yolks and sugar in double boiler. When it is boiling hot add gelatin which has been dissolved in cold water. Then remove from fire and pour over egg whites which have been beaten stiff. Fold the mixture until blended. Have dates, nuts and macaroons cut up in your mold, then pour this mixture over it. Let stand in ice box until it jellies and serve with whipped cream.

---

### Ice Box Dessert

*Mrs. Frank Cornish. · Original printed p. 99, scan p. 106 · `mg1930-p106-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pkg. lemon Jello
- 12 macaroons, crumbled
- 1 small bottle Maraschino
- 1 tbsp. vinegar
- cherries
- 2 tsp. sugar
- 1/2 cup pecans, cut or chopped
- dash salt
- 12 marshmallows, cut in pieces
- 1 cup whipped cream

**Preparation**

Whip cream. Put vinegar, salt and sugar in jello while hot. When cool and partly congealed, whip. Add pecans, marshmallows, cherries, and fold in whipped cream and macaroons, and pour into mold. Chill or freeze and serve in slices.

---

### Cheese Ring

**Ingredients**

- 1 pkg. Knox gelatin
- 2 Philadelphia cheese
- ½ bottle thick cream
- 1 anona cheese

**Preparation**

Add 1/4 cup cold water to gelatin and melt over steam. Add to cream. Blend in the cheese and season. Place in mold and serve with crackers and jelly.

---

### Chocolate Ice Box Pudding

*Mrs. LeRoy Bibby. · Original printed p. 100, scan p. 107 · `mg1930-p107-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2-10c cakes Baker Sweet Chocolate 3 tbsp. water
- 4 eggs
- 2 tbsp. sugar

Melt chocolate in double boiler; add water and sugar. Add beaten yolks of eggs and boil. When cold add beaten whites. When ready to serve pour over lady fingers and serve with whipping cream.

---

### Ice Box Dessert

*Mrs. James L. Sigler. · Original printed p. 100, scan p. 107 · `mg1930-p107-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ lb. vanilla wafers
- 2 cups brown sugar
- 2 bananas
- 4 tbsp. cornstarch
- ½ pkg. dates

**Preparation**

Line bottom of dish with wafers, add bananas sliced, dates and nuts cut in pieces. Boil until thick, the sugar, cornstarch, and water, and pour over the contents of the dish. Cover with another layer of wafers and add rest of sauce. Chill. Serve with whipped cream.

---

### Ice Box Cake

*Mrs. C. O. White. · Original printed p. 100, scan p. 107 · `mg1930-p107-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ lb. unsalted butter
- ½ lb. powdered sugar
- 6 eggs, separated

**Preparation**

Cream the butter with the powdered sugar. Add egg yolks, ground almonds, and crushed macaroons. Fold in beaten egg whites last. Assemble with lady fingers as indicated by the source. Let stand in the icebox at least 36 hours. Serves 16.

---

### Maple Gelatine Pudding

*Mrs. D. C. Rockwell. · Original printed p. 100, scan p. 107 · `mg1930-p107-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 tbsp. Knox gelatine
- 1/2 cup cold water
- 2 cups maple syrup
- 6 eggs
- 2 bottles heavy cream
- salt

Soak gelatine in water 10 minutes. Let syrup come to boil and pour on gelatine. Add beaten egg yolks and set mixture to cool. When it begins to thicken add beaten egg whites and whipped cream. Mold and chill. Serves 16.

---

### Pineapple Ice Box Cake

*Mrs. C. O. White. · Original printed p. 100, scan p. 107 · `mg1930-p107-r06`*

**Ingredients**

- 36 ladyfingers
- 1 can shredded pineapple, well drained
- ½ lb. butter, softened
- ¾ lb. powdered sugar
- 4 egg whites, whipped stiff
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- Grated orange zest

**Preparation**

Cream softened butter with powdered sugar. Fold in drained pineapple, orange zest and vanilla, then gently fold in stiffly beaten egg whites. Line a serving dish with ladyfingers and alternate layers with pineapple filling. Chill at least 4 hours, until firm. Garnish with whipped cream and cherries before serving.

---

### Macaroon Chocolate Pudding

- 1 tbsp. Knox gelatine

Soak gelatine in water and melt over hot water. Whip cream and when gelatine has cooled slightly, add gelatine to cream and divide in two parts. To one half add the chocolate which has been melted over hot water, salt and vanilla. To the other half add the macaroons which have been pounded or ground up. Put in dish in layers and garnish with maraschino cherries and nuts. May be served with whipped cream.

---

### Pineapple Dessert

*Mrs. R. E. Valentine. · Original printed p. 101, scan p. 108 · `mg1930-p108-r02`*

Whip the cream stiff; gradually add drained pineapple and marshmallow pieces. Let stand about ½ hour on ice. Serve in sherbet glasses, sprinkled with chopped nutmeats and cinnamon.

---

### Pineapple Marshmallow Pudding

*Mrs. D. C. Rockwell. · Original printed p. 101, scan p. 108 · `mg1930-p108-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ lb. marshmallows
- ½ cup milk
- 1 can shredded pineapple
- juice, 1 orange

Put milk and marshmallows in double boiler and melt together. Add drained pineapple and orange juice. Cool and add 1 bottle whipped cream. Mold and chill.

---

### Pineapple Salad

- 1 cup rice
- 1 small can grated pineapple, drained
- ½ lb. marshmallows, halved
- 1 cup nutmeats
