Boil rice until done in plenty of boiling, salted water, and then wash in cold water. Sweeten a little and add pineapple, and let stand over night. Then add the other ingredients, and stir all together.

---

### Orange Apple Jelly

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 101, scan p. 108 · `mg1930-p108-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1orange
- 1 cup apple sauce
- 1 pkg. prepared jelly powder (orange flavor)
- 1 cup boiling water

**Preparation**

To one cup of boiling sweetened apple sauce add a package of prepared jelly powder and one cup boiling water. Line a mold with orange sections which are free from all membrane. When the jelly begins to set pour into the mold. Serve with cream, plain or whipped. Time in combining, 20 minutes. Recipe makes six servings.

---

### Rice And Pineapple Bavarian Cream

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 102, scan p. 109 · `mg1930-p109-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup cooked rice
- 1 cup drained crushed pineapple
- 1½ cups pineapple juice
- ½ cup cold water
- 1 tsp. lemon extract
- ½ cup sugar
- 1 envelope Knox sparkling gelatine
- ½ pt. cream, whipped

**Preparation**

Boil rice in plenty of water (use cold water to start with). When kernels are tender, pour in a strainer and run cold water over it. Add pineapple. Heat pineapple juice, sugar and lemon extract to boiling point. Soak gelatine in 1/2 cup cold water, then add boiling pineapple juice. Let cool. When it begins to thicken, beat until it is white and foamy, fold in rice, pineapple, and whipped cream. Chill, and serve with whipped cream garnish.

---

### Jellied Rhubarb

*Mrs. E. R. Valentine. · Original printed p. 102, scan p. 109 · `mg1930-p109-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 qt. diced rhubarb
- 2 lemons
- 2 cups water
- 2½ tbsp. Knox gelatin
- 2 cups sugar
- ½ cup cold water

**Preparation**

Bake (or simmer) slowly, the rhubarb covered with sugar and water until tender and pink. Strain. Soak gelatin in cold water, and add to the juice. Cool and stir in the grated rind and juice of the lemons. Pour in wet mold. Serve with whipped cream.

---

### Tapioca Icebox Dessert

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 102, scan p. 109 · `mg1930-p109-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 tbsp. pearl tapioca
- ½ cup hot water
- 2 cups milk
- 2 eggs beaten
- ½ cup sugar
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- 2 tbsp. Knox gelatine
- ½ pt. whipping cream
- ½ cup sugar
- 2 dozen lady fingers

**Preparation**

Pour hot water over tapioca and let stand 1 hour. Cook in double boiler until transparent. Add milk, eggs, sugar and cook until mixture coats a silver spoon lightly. Set aside. Soak gelatine in a little cold water for 5 minutes, add to first mixture while it is hot. Let it harden. Line an angel food tin with halves of lady fingers. Whip cream very stiff adding sugar slowly. Fold the hardened custard in lightly. Pour into lined tin and cover with lady fingers. Chill 2 hours. Serve with plain or whipped cream.

---

## Cold Desserts

### Brown Sugar Pudding

*Mrs. H. W. Anderson. · Original printed p. 105, scan p. 112 · `mg1930-p112-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup brown sugar
- 2 cups hot water
- 2 heaping tbsp. corn starch
- butter and vanilla
- salt

**Preparation**

Mix sugar, cornstarch and salt; add hot water; cook until thick then add butter and flavoring after it is removed from fire. Top with whipped cream. This is a very quick and easy dessert. Serves four.

---

### Cranberry Sauce

*Mrs. James L. Sigler. · Original printed p. 105, scan p. 112 · `mg1930-p112-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 qt. cranberries
- 2 cups sugar

**Preparation**

Cover and bake in a moderate oven, 350 degrees for 20 minutes.

---

### Cocoanut Blanc Mange

*Mrs. P. V. Sangren. · Original printed p. 105, scan p. 112 · `mg1930-p112-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 tbsp. cornstarch
- ½ can wet cocoanut
- 1 cup milk
- 2 egg whites, beaten
- ½ cup sugar
- vanilla
- 1/4 tsp. salt

**Preparation**

Cook milk and starch until thick, add other ingredients, folding in whites last. Serve with thin custard or fruit sauce.

---

### Cocoa Pudding

*Mrs. Lawrence Fuller. · Original printed p. 105, scan p. 112 · `mg1930-p112-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 tbsp. cocoa
- 3 tbsp. cornstarch
- 8 tbsp. sugar
- 1 pint water
- ½ tsp. salt
- 1 tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Mix all ingredients except vanilla and cook slowly in double boiler 25 minutes, stirring constantly until thick. Add vanilla, mold and cool. Serve with plain or whipped cream.

---

### Date Delight

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 105, scan p. 112 · `mg1930-p112-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lb. marshmallows
- ½ lb. walnut meats
- 1 lb. graham crackers
- ½ pint cream
- 1 lb. dates

**Preparation**

Roll the crackers until fine. Cut the dates and marshmallowsthe marshmallows should be fresh-cut in small pieces. Chop the nuts. Mix well together, moistening with the cream. Mold in a roll with oiled paper. Place in refrigerator overnight. Serve cut in slices with whipped cream or hard sauce topping each slice. Mrs. James Rork.

---

### Date Pudding

*Mrs. Paul Snauble. · Original printed p. 106, scan p. 113 · `mg1930-p113-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup powdered sugar
- 2 eggs, well beaten
- 2 tbsp. flour
- 1 cup nut meats, coarse
- 1 tsp. baking powder
- 1 cup dates, cut up

**Preparation**

Sift first three, mix and bake in shallow pan in water in moderate oven, 325 degrees, for 20 minutes. Serve cold with whipped cream. Serves 6.

---

### Date Pudding

*Mrs. LeRoy Bibby. · Original printed p. 106, scan p. 113 · `mg1930-p113-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup dates, cut fine
- ½ cup melted butter
- 1 cup nut meats, cut up
- 2 eggs (don't beat)
- 1 tbsp. flour
- 1 cup milk
- 1 cup sugar, scant
- 1 level tsp. baking powder

**Preparation**

Mix ingredients and bake in slow oven in small patty tins for individual portions.

---

### Date Pudding

*Mrs. Haskell Bogard. · Original printed p. 106, scan p. 113 · `mg1930-p113-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 small cup sugar
- 3 tbsp. flour
- 2 eggs
- 2 tsp. baking powder
- 1 box dates, cut up fine
- 3 tbsp. milk
- 1 cup nutmeats, cliopped

**Preparation**

This makes twelve pieces. Serve with whipped cream. Bake in slow oven 45 minutes.

---

### Date Pudding

*Mrs. H. B. Rix. · Original printed p. 106, scan p. 113 · `mg1930-p113-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup dates
- 1 egg
- ½ cup nutmeats
- 1 cup hot water
- 1 cup sugar
- 1/4 cupflour

**Preparation**

Bake in muffin tins and serve with whipped cream.

---

### Date Pudding

*Mrs. Harry Becker. · Original printed p. 106, scan p. 113 · `mg1930-p113-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 egg
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- ½ cup sugar
- 1 cup nuts (chopped)
- 1/4 cup flour
- 1 cup dates (cut in pieces)
- 1 tsp. baking powder

**Preparation**

Beat egg until light and foamy; add the sugar. Mix the flour, baking powder and salt with the nuts and dates. ( Walnuts are excellent. ) Combine the two mixtures. Pour into an oiled pan. Bake in a slow oven about thirty minutes, or until firm. Serve with whipped cream. Six large, or eight medium helpings.

---

### Date Pudding

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 106, scan p. 113 · `mg1930-p113-r06`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup dates
- 1 cup nuts
- 1½ cups flour
- ½ tsp. salt
- 2 tsp. baking powder
- 1 cup sugar
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- 4 beaten whites
- 4 beaten yolks

**Preparation**

Chop dates and nuts, add flour, salt and baking powder, sugar and egg yolks, vanilla and egg whites. Bake in an angel food pan. Slice and serve with cream. Serves 12.

---

### Graham Cracker Pudding

*Mrs. Geo. Williamson. · Original printed p. 107, scan p. 114 · `mg1930-p114-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ cups graham crackers
- 2 cups sugar
- 5 eggs
- 1 tsp. baking powder
- 1 cup nuts

**Preparation**

Mix dry ingredients, and add yolks, then whites and vanilla. Bake slowly, 45 or 50 minutes. 8 large servings.

---

### Left Over Ice Cream

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 107, scan p. 114 · `mg1930-p114-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- Leftover ice cream
- Milk, as needed to thin
- Cornstarch, about 1 tbsp. per 2 cups thinned ice cream

**Preparation**

When ice cream is left over, add enough milk to thin slightly, scald and add a little cornstarch to thicken. Put in custard cups that have been rinsed in cold water.

---

### Lemon Sponge Custard

*Mrs. L. D. Barnhart. · Original printed p. 107, scan p. 114 · `mg1930-p114-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lemon, juice and grated rind
- 1 tbsp. butter
- 1 cup sugar
- 2 eggs
- 2 tbsp. flour
- 1 cup milk

**Preparation**

Mix first four ingredients. Beat egg yolks and add milk, then mix with above. Fold in whites and put in unbaked crust. Bake quickly, 400 degrees, 10 minutes and then reduce heat to about 350 degrees or less and bake very slowly until a silver knife will come out clean. This may be served in custard cups without pastry.

---

### Lemon Cream

*Mrs. Paul Snauble. · Original printed p. 107, scan p. 114 · `mg1930-p114-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 5 eggs
- 1 tsp. lemon rind
- 1⅓ cups powdered sugar
- 2 tbsp. lemon juice

**Preparation**

Separate eggs. In double boiler cook yolk, sugar, rind, and juice about 8 minutes or until like custard. Then add stiffly beaten whites and turn out in sherbet glasses. Serve cold.

---

### Lemon Pudding

*Mrs. Harry Becker. · Original printed p. 107, scan p. 114 · `mg1930-p114-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup sweet milk
- 1 egg
- 2/3 cup sugar
- juice and rind of 1 lemon
- 1 tbsp. cornstarch

**Preparation**

Heat milk, then add sugar. Meanwhile mix cornstarch with a little cold milk, and add the egg, well beaten. Add this mixture to the hot milk, and let boil till thick. Remove from fire, let cool, then add juice and grated rind of 1 lemon. Serve with whipped cream. This will make four servings.

---

### Pineapple Tapioca

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 107, scan p. 114 · `mg1930-p114-r06`*

**Ingredients**

- ⅓ cup minute tapioca
- 2 cups water and juice
- ½ cup sugar
- 2 slices pineapple
- pinch salt
- 1 tsp. butter
- 1 tsp. lemon juice

**Preparation**

Place all ingredients except tapioca in double boiler. When hot add tapioca; stirring until thick. Cook until tapioca is clear about 20 minutes. Serves 5 or 6. Serve with coffee cream or whipped cream.

---

### Orange Parisian

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 108, scan p. 115 · `mg1930-p115-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 oranges
- 2 fresh pears, or 4 canned pear halves
- ½ cup powdered sugar
- ¼ cup fresh mint, finely chopped
- ½ cup whipping cream
- 2 tbsp. additional powdered sugar, for whipped cream

**Preparation**

Pare and slice oranges, reserving all juice that escapes. Cut pears in slices and mix with oranges. Divide into six portions, and place in sherbet glasses. Mix the juice from oranges, the sugar and the mint and pour over the fruit. Set in cold place until ready to serve. Serve topped with a spoonful of whipped cream, sweetened with 2 tbsp. powdered sugar.

---

### Fresh Pineapple Cup

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 108, scan p. 115 · `mg1930-p115-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup sugar
- 1/4 cup lemon juice
- 1/3 cup water
- 1 pineapple
- ½ cup orange juice
- ½ cup cocoanut

**Preparation**

Boil sugar and water together for 5 minutes. Cool and add fruit juices. Cut fresh pineapple cylinders, using apple corer. Put in glasses with cocoanut and cover with the syrup.

---

### Tapioca Pudding

*Mrs. Irene Nicholson. · Original printed p. 108, scan p. 115 · `mg1930-p115-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup pearl tapioca
- Pineapple
- 2 cups light brown sugar
- Nutmeats

**Preparation**

Whipped cream

Soak tapioca over night in water to cover. In the morning add sugar and cook in double boiler until well done or tapioca is transparent. Cut and drain pineapple, and add it with nutmeats to whipped cream for top of pudding.

---

### Blitz Torte

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 108, scan p. 115 · `mg1930-p115-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1/4 cup butter
- 2 tsp. baking powder
- ½ cup sugar
- 1/4 cup milk
- 4 egg yolks, beaten
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- 1 cup flour
- speck salt

**Preparation**

Cream butter and sugar. Add yolks and vanilla. Mix flour and baking powder. Add to first mixture, with milk. Spread in two shallow pans. Cover dough with 4 egg whites, beaten stiff, mixed with 3/4 cup sugar; and sprinkle with 1/4 cup almonds, sliced. Bake in moderate oven ( 300 degrees ) about 1/2 hour. Spread custard filling between layers. Custard filling: Beat 1 egg thoroughly, scald 1 cup milk, add ½ cup sugar. Stir 1 heaping tsp. cornstarch with a little of the milk and cook until thick. Remove from fire, and add ½ tsp. vanilla. Mrs. C. A. Buskirk.

---

### Cheese Torte

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 109, scan p. 116 · `mg1930-p116-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- Custard:
- 1
- 2 lbs. cottage cheese
- 1 heaping tbsp. flour (more if
- ½ pt. cream
- cheese is mushy)
- 4 eggs
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- 1 cup sugar

**Preparation**

Outside: 1 pkg. zweibach or Holland rusk, put through food chopper, using finest knife. 1 cup sugar 1 tsp. cinnamon 1/4 cup melted butter Mix together. Butter tin and line bottom and sides, keeping out 1 cup of crumbs. Put in custard and cover with the remaining crumbs. Even off edge. Bake in torte pan 1/4 hours, at 350 degrees. Let cool. Remove sides of pan, place torte on platter and cut.

---

### Date Torte

*Mrs. H. W. Anderson. · Original printed p. 109, scan p. 116 · `mg1930-p116-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1/2 cup sugar
- 2 eggs
- 1 tbsp. flour
- 1 cup chopped walnut meats
- 1 tsp. baking powder
- 1 cup chopped dates
- salt

**Preparation**

Bake in slow oven about 3/4 hour in shallow pan. Top with whipped cream. Serves six people.

---

### Date Torte

*Mrs. E. Ray Winters. · Original printed p. 109, scan p. 116 · `mg1930-p116-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 or 3 eggs
- 1 cup dates, chopped
- 1 cup sugar
- ½ cup walnuts
- 1 cup bread crumbs
- 1 tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Beat egg whites, then add yolks and other ingredients. Bake slowly in moderate oven, 325 degrees, for 25 minutes.

---

### Date Dessert Torte

*Mrs. R. Mickey. · Original printed p. 109, scan p. 116 · `mg1930-p116-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup nuts
- 2 tbsp. flour
- 1 cup seeded dates, cut up
- 1 tsp. baking powder
- 3 egg yolks
- 3 egg whites
- 1 cup sugar
- 1 tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Beat egg yolks, add sugar gradually and beat well until they are light. Add chopped nuts and dates. Add flour and baking powder. Fold in beaten whites and the vanilla. Bake Torte in 2 layers in a slow oven ( 275 ° ) for 3/4 hour. Serve with whipped cream.

---

### Nut Torte

*Mrs. Arthur Thornton. · Original printed p. 109, scan p. 116 · `mg1930-p116-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 egg yolks
- 1½ tsp. baking powder
- 1 cup sugar
- 1 cup whole pecans
- 1½ cups graham cracker crumbs, rolled medium fine
- 3 egg whites

**Preparation**

Beat egg yolks, add sugar and cream well. Add cracker crumbs, baking powder, and pecans. Beat egg whites stiff, and fold into mixture. Bake at 300 degrees. Serve with whipped cream.

---

### Maple Nut Torte

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 110, scan p. 117 · `mg1930-p117-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1/2 cup cold water
- 1 cup cream, whipped
- 3/4 cup maple syrup
- 2 eggs
- ¾ cup chopped nuts
- 10 macaroons
- Gelatine (amount not stated in source)
- Lady fingers (to line the mold)

**Preparation**

Soak gelatine in water 5 minutes. Beat yolks lightly, add maple syrup and cook until well blended and thick. Add soaked gelatine and cool. Before it sets, fold in stiffly beaten egg whites, macaroons, nuts and whipped cream. Line mold with lady fingers, and pour in mixture. Chill. Unmold and garnish with whipped cream, whole nuts and candied fruits.

---

### Nutzen Tart

*Mrs. C. O. White. · Original printed p. 110, scan p. 117 · `mg1930-p117-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup sugar
- ½ cup butter
- 4 egg yolks
- 4 tbsp. milk
- ½ cup plus 1 tbsp. flour, sifted with 1 tsp. baking powder
- 4 egg whites, for meringue
- ½ cup sugar, for meringue
- ½ cup chopped nuts, for topping

**Preparation**

Put batter into two layer-cake pans. Make meringue with stiffly beaten egg whites and ½ cup sugar. Cover layers with meringue, sprinkle with ½ cup chopped nuts, and bake about 20 minutes in a slow oven (300°F). Use whipped cream and fruit, or custard, between layers. The source does not separately state a mixing method for the batter.

---

### Schaum Tarte

*Mrs. C. D. Brown. · Original printed p. 110, scan p. 117 · `mg1930-p117-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- Whites of 6 eggs
- 2 cups sugar
- 1 tbsp. vinegar
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- 1 quart strawberries or other fruit
- ½ pint whipped cream

**Preparation**

Beat egg whites lightly; add sugar gradually and beat vigorously until dissolved. Add vinegar and vanilla. Bake in two buttered cake tins lightly dusted with flour and sugar, in a very slow oven for 45 minutes.

Add one quart strawberries or other fruit. Crush half for the first layer and top with whole berries. At serving, cover with ½ pint whipped cream. The torte may be made a day ahead, but do not put fruit on until ready to serve. Serves eight.

---

### Dainty Dessert

*Mrs. L. L. Taylor. · Original printed p. 110, scan p. 117 · `mg1930-p117-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 qts. tuitti fruitti ice cream
- 1 pt. whipping cream
- 12 cream puff shells
- 1 bottle cherries, maraschino

**Preparation**

Cut tops off of cream puff shells, fill with ice cream, and replace top. Serve with whipped cream and a cherry on top. It is not necessary to serve cake with this, as it is quite complete in itself.

**Variations:**

Fill shells with plain ice cream, leaving top off, cover with crushed strawberries, and whipped cream. Fill shells with pineapple sherbet, and cover with chocolate sauce.

---

### Strawberry Cream Puffs

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 111, scan p. 118 · `mg1930-p118-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pt. whipping cream
- 1 qt. strawberries
- powdered sugar
- 12 cream puff shells

**Preparation**

Cut berries into halves, cover with powdered sugar. Just before serving mix with whipped cream. Slit puff and insert filling.

---

### Strawberry Meringue Cake

*Mrs. Paul Snauble. · Original printed p. 111, scan p. 118 · `mg1930-p118-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups Swansdown cake flour
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- 4 tsp. baking powder
- ½ cup sugar
- ½ cup butter
- 4 egg whites
- 1 cup sugar
- 1 qt. fresh strawberries
- 4 egg yolks, beaten
- ½ cup sugar
- ¾ cup milk

**Preparation**

Sift flour once, measure, add baking powder and sift together 3 times. Cream butter, add 1 cup sugar, and cream together until light and fluffy. Add egg yolks, then sifted flour, alternately with milk, a small amount at a time. Beat after each addition until smooth. Add vanilla. Bake in two 9 inch layer pans at 375 degrees for 25 minutes. Remove from pans and cool. Fold 1/2 cup sugar slowly into beaten whites. Place 2 layers on baking sheet. Pile meringue lightly on them and return to oven, baking 15 minutes at 350 degrees. Wash berries, and hull. Reserve choice ones for garnishing. Crush remainder of berries with ½ cup sugar. Spread between layers. Garnish top with whole berries and serve at once. Serves 8.

---

## Hot Desserts

### Apple Pudding

*Mrs. James L. Sigler. · Original printed p. 113, scan p. 120 · `mg1930-p120-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- Sliced apples, enough to cover bottom of dish
- Sugar and butter, to sprinkle over apples (amounts not specified)
- Batter: ½ cup sugar
- Batter: 1 cup milk
- Batter: 1 egg
- Batter: pinch salt
- Batter: 1½ cups flour
- Batter: 1 tsp. baking powder
- Batter: 1 tbsp. melted lard

**Preparation**

Cover the bottom of a baking dish with sliced apples and sprinkle with sugar and dots of butter. Pour the batter over the apples. Bake 1 hour at 325°F.

---

### Apple Roll

*Esther Winters. · Original printed p. 113, scan p. 120 · `mg1930-p120-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 medium apples
- 1½ cups sugar
- 1 pt. water
- rich biscuit dough

**Preparation**

Peel, core and slice apples. Put sugar and water in deep baking pan over slow fire. Make rich biscuit dough and roll out about 1/2 inch thick, spread with apples and roll into a long roll; cut into pieces about 1½ inches long; place with cut side down in hot syrup; put small piece of shortening on top and sprinkle with cinnamon and sugar. Bake in a hot oven until apples are done and the crust a golden brown. Turn out on platter and pour syrup around them. May be served plain or with whipped cream. Cherries or peaches may be substituted for apples.

---

### Scalloped Apples

*Mrs. M. Ellis. · Original printed p. 113, scan p. 120 · `mg1930-p120-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 tbsp. butter
- ½ cup sugar
- 3 cups soft bread crumbs
- 1/4 tsp. cinnamon
- 1 qt. tart apples, cubed
- ½ tsp. nutmeg
- ½ cup water

**Preparation**

Melt the butter and mix with the bread crumbs. Mix the apples, sugar, spices and water. Arrange the buttered crumbs and apple mixture in alternate layers. Reserve 1/2 the buttered crumbs for the top layer. Bake covered for 2½ hours. Serve with cream or fruit sauce.

---

### Apricot Bread Pudding

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 113, scan p. 120 · `mg1930-p120-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ cups stale bread crumbs
- Syrup from 1 can Del Monte peaches
- 1 cup hot milk
- 2 egg whites, beaten
- 3 tsp. sugar
- A few apricots

**Preparation**

Soak bread crumbs in hot milk. Add sugar, syrup, beaten egg whites and a few apricots. Pour into a greased baking dish. Bake until firm and serve garnished with apricots.

---

### Bird Nests

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 114, scan p. 121 · `mg1930-p121-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup milk
- 1 egg
- 1 cup flour
- 1 tsp. baking powder
- salt
- 4 apples

**Preparation**

•

sugar cinnamon

Pare, core and roll apples in sugar and cinnamon. Sift flour, baking powder and salt, add milk and beaten eggs. Pour over apples and bake until apples are done.

---

### Cherry Pudding

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 114, scan p. 121 · `mg1930-p121-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- ¼ cup melted butter
- 1 egg, placed in a cup, with milk added to fill cup
- 1 cup flour
- Scant ½ cup sugar
- 1 tsp. baking powder
- ½ tsp. salt

**Preparation**

Sift dry ingredients and mix together; add to liquid ingredients gradually beating well as you go. Put a box of fresh cherries pitted and well sugared in bottom of baking dish. Cover with batter and bake until nice and brown about 35 minutes at 350 °. Other fruits may be used as well as cherries.

---

### Brown Betty

**Ingredients**

- Sliced apples
- Sugar, to taste
- Butter, for layering
- Cinnamon

**Preparation**

Put in a baking dish a layer of sliced apples, sugar, butter and cinnamon. Add a layer of bread crumbs, alternate until dish is full with a layer of crumbs and butter on top. Cover bottom of dish with water. Bake 3/4 hour.

---

### Cherry Cup Pudding

*Mrs. Harry Becker. · Original printed p. 114, scan p. 121 · `mg1930-p121-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- [Batter]
- 1 cup flour (heaping)
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- 1 tsp. baking powder
- milk enough for stiffening

**Preparation**

Combine in order named. Butter cups, fill half full of cherries, then the batter. Steam about 15 minutes. Serve with the following sauce:

1 cup sugar 1 tbsp. flour butter size of a walnut

Juice from cherries. Add water if there is not enough juice. Let boil for a few minutes. This makes 4 cup puddings. Canned peaches make excellent puddings, using same batter and sauce recipes. If peaches are used add 1 teaspoon vanilla to sauce.

### Steamed Cherry Pudding

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 114, scan p. 121 · `mg1930-p121-r05`*

- 1 tsp. cinnamon

### Chocolate Roll

Beat egg yolks and sugar together. Add cocoa and vanilla. Add stiffly beaten whites. Spread in shallow pan, and bake 30 minutes at 300 degrees or less. Serves 4 or 5.

### Chocolate Steamed Pudding

*Mrs. J. B. Fleugel. · Original printed p. 115, scan p. 122 · `mg1930-p122-r02`*

- ½ cup milk

Mix egg, sugar, milk, flour, salt and baking powder; add chocolate and butter melted together. Steam 1 hour in a coffee can or other suitable mold.

### Lemon Sauce

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 115, scan p. 122 · `mg1930-p122-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 egg
- 1 cup sugar
- 1 scant tbsp. flour
- Just under 1 pint boiling water

- Lemon juice or zest, to taste

**Preparation**

Beat together until foamy 1 egg, and 1 cup sugar mixed with 1 scant tbsp. flour. Add nearly a pint of boiling water and butter size of a walnut. Let come to a boil and flavor with lemon. :

---

### Date Pudding

*Mrs. J. B. Fleugel. · Original printed p. 115, scan p. 122 · `mg1930-p122-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pkg. Dromedary dates
- 1 cup boiling water
- 1 tbsp. butter
- 1 cup sugar
- 1 egg
- 1⅓ cups flour
- 1 scant tsp. soda
- grated orange peel

**Preparation**

Soak dates cut up in boiling water until mixture cools. Cream butter and sugar, add eggs, flour, soda and grated rind of one orange. Add water and dates and bake in a greased pan in a very slow oven. Serve warm with the following sauce: 1 egg yolk, beaten 1 egg white beaten 2 tbsp. powdered sugar vanilla ½ bottle whipped cream Pudding may be kept and served later by steaming it when • wanted.

---

### Steamed Date And Nut Pudding

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 116, scan p. 123 · `mg1930-p123-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 egg
- 1 tsp. cloves
- 1/3 cup butter
- 1 tsp. allspice
- 1 cup sugar
- 1 tsp. nutmeg
- ½ cup molasses
- 4 tsp. baking powder
- 1 cup milk
- ¾ lb. dates, cut up
- 3 cups bread flour
- ½ cup nut meats
- 1 tsp. salt

**Preparation**

Mix as you would a cake, and steam 3 hours in a tube cake pan for mold. Serve with Foamy Sauce.

---

### New England Indian Pudding

*Mrs. J. B. Fleugel. · Original printed p. 116, scan p. 123 · `mg1930-p123-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 qt. milk
- 2 eggs
- 1 cup molasses
- 2 tbsp. butter
- 1 cup cornmeal
- salt
- 2 cups hot water

**Preparation**

Stir meal into hot water to make a mush, add other ingredients and bake very slowly 3 hours. Serve with melted butter or hardsauce.

---

### Fruit Fritters

*Ida Bailey Allen. · Original printed p. 116, scan p. 123 · `mg1930-p123-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup flour, scant
- 2 eggs
- 2 tbsp. Argo or Kingsford
- 1 tsp. Mazola
- Cornstarch
- 2/3 cup milk
- ½ tsp. salt
- 1 cup prepared fruit
- 1 tsp. baking powder

**Preparation**

Thefruits that may be used in making these fritters are: thinly sliced apple, sliced fresh or canned peaches, stoned halved prunes, or sliced bananas. Sift together the dry ingredients, then beat in the milk, the egg yolks, beaten until creamy, and the Mazola. Stir in the fruit, fold in the egg whites, whipped, and drop by small tablespoonfuls into Mazola heated to 350 degrees F., sufficient to brown a bit of bread in a minute in a deep fat frying kettle. Cook ten minutes, drain on brown paper, dust with sugar and serve with fruit sauce or Karo.

---

### Pineapple Tart

*Mrs. C. A. Buskirk. · Original printed p. 116, scan p. 123 · `mg1930-p123-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- Tart bottom: 2 tbsp. butter
- Tart bottom: 1 cup brown sugar
- Tart bottom: 6 slices pineapple
- Tart bottom: 8 maraschino cherries
- Batter: 3 eggs
- Batter: 1½ cups granulated sugar
- Batter: ½ cup water
- Batter: ¼ tsp. salt
- Batter: 1½ cups flour
- Batter: 1½ tsp. baking powder
- Batter: ½ tsp. vanilla

**Preparation**

Arrange butter, brown sugar, pineapple and cherries in order in the bottom of a tart pan.

Batter: Beat eggs separately. Add water to yolks, then half the sugar. Add remaining sugar to stiffly beaten whites. Pour yolk mixture over whites and continue beating; add remaining batter ingredients and beat smooth. Pour over fruit and bake 1 hour at 325°F. Serve warm with whipped cream. Serves six.

---

### Peach Cobbler

*Mrs. D. C. Rockwell. · Original printed p. 117, scan p. 124 · `mg1930-p124-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 beaten egg
- 1 cup sugar
- 3 tbsp. butter
- 1/2 cup milk
- 1 tsp. baking powder
- 1½ cups flour
- salt
- vanilla

**Preparation**

Cream butter and sugar, add egg beaten, milk and dry ingredients. In the bottom of a buttered baking dish put peaches. If canned peaches are used no sugar is needed, but sugar must be sprinkled on fresh peaches. Pour batter over peaches and bake 1 hour. Apples or other fruit may be substituted for peaches.

---

### Peach Cobbler

*Mrs. Carl R. Johnson. · Original printed p. 117, scan p. 124 · `mg1930-p124-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups peaches, in bottom of basin
- 1 egg, beaten
- ½ cup sugar
- 1 tbsp. shortening
- 1 cup sweet milk
- 1 heaping tsp. baking powder
- 1½ cups flour

**Preparation**

Heat oven to 350°F. Spread peaches in a buttered baking dish. Cream shortening and sugar; beat in egg, then add milk. Sift flour with baking powder and stir into the mixture. Pour batter over peaches and bake 35–45 minutes until the topping is golden and cooked through.

---

### Poor Man's Pudding

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 117, scan p. 124 · `mg1930-p124-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 cups flour
- 3 tbsp. melted butter
- 1 cup whole seeded raisins
- 1 egg
- 1 cup molasses
- 1 tsp. cinnamon
- 1 cup hot water
- 1 tsp. cloves
- 1 tsp. soda in hot water

**Preparation**

Mix in order given, and steam 3 hours. 10 good sized servings. Sauce: 3 eggs vanilla 1 cup sugar nutmeg

Beat yolks, add sugar, and then beaten whites, and flavorings to taste. ( I use 1/3 or 23 of recipe, depending on number to be served).

---

### Prune Whip

*Hazel Menzie. · Original printed p. 117, scan p. 124 · `mg1930-p124-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- Whites of 3 eggs
- ½ cup sugar
- ⅓ lb. or 1 dozen prunes
- 1 tbsp. lemon juice

**Preparation**

Whites of 3 eggs 3 lb. or 1 doz. prunes 1½ cup sugar 1 tbsp. lemon juice Cook prunes with sugar and lemon juice, then cool. Beat eggs and add prune mixture. Bake in buttered baking dish 20 or 30 minutes in hot oven. Set in pan of water to bake. Serve with soft custard or whipped cream.

---

### Rice Pudding

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 117, scan p. 124 · `mg1930-p124-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- ⅓ cup rice
- 1 pint hot milk, for initial cooking
- 1 additional pint hot milk
- ½ cup sugar
- ½ tsp. cinnamon
- ¾ tsp. nutmeg
- 1 tbsp. butter

**Preparation**

Cook rice with 1 pint hot milk in a double boiler until tender. Add another pint hot milk, sugar, cinnamon, nutmeg, and butter. Brown in oven, stirring several times.

---

### Raspberry Pudding

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 118, scan p. 125 · `mg1930-p125-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup butter
- ½ cup sugar
- 2 eggs
- 2 tbsp. raspberry jam
- ½ cup flour
- ¾ tbsp. soda, dissolved in 1 tbsp. water

**Preparation**

Steam in small angel cake pan exactly 1 hour. Keep covered while steaming. Serve with whipped cream to which 3 tbsp. of Raspberry Jam has been added.

---

### Sailor's Duff Pudding

*Mrs. E. J. Anderson. · Original printed p. 118, scan p. 125 · `mg1930-p125-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- Whites of 2 eggs
- ½ cup molasses
- 2 tbsp. melted butter
- 1½ cups flour
- ½ tsp. soda, dissolved in ½ cup water
- Sauce: ½ pt. whipped cream
- Sauce: yolks of 2 eggs
- Sauce: 3 tbsp. sugar
- Sauce: vanilla

**Preparation**

Mix egg whites, molasses, butter, flour, and soda dissolved in water in order named. Steam ¾ hour.

Serve warm with a sauce of whipped cream, egg yolks, sugar and vanilla. Makes six servings.

---

### Strawberry Meringue

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 118, scan p. 125 · `mg1930-p125-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 cups stale bread- crumbs
- Grated rind half a lemon
- 1 qt. milk
- ½ level tsp. salt
- 4 eggs
- ½ cup powdered sugar
- 3/4 cup sugar
- 1 cup crushed berries
- 1 tsp. melted butter

**Preparation**

Time: Preparation and baking, 30 minutes at 350°. 20 minutes at 335 °. Number served: 6 to 8 persons.



Soak bread crumbs in milk; when soft, add sugar, salt, beaten yolks, lemon rind, butter; mix. Pour into a shallow greased dish, and bake in a moderate oven until set about thirty minutes. When done, cover the top with berries, sweetened as necessary, and heap with stiff meringue made from the whites of eggs and powdered sugar. Brown in moderate oven. Serve cold. The pudding may be made several hours before serving, but the berries and meringue should both be added shortly before serving. Jam may be substituted for berries.

---

### Steamed Marmalade Pudding

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 118, scan p. 125 · `mg1930-p125-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ cups flour
- 1 egg
- 1/4 cup sugar
- 1 tbsp. baking powder
- 2 tbsp. shortening
- 1 tbsp. milk
- 6 tbsp. marmalade
- ½ tsp. salt

**Preparation**

Cream shortening and sugar. Add beaten egg and marmalade. Mix baking powder and flour and add to mixture. Add milk if needed to moisten. Steam two and one-half hours. Time in cooking, 2½ hours. Recipe makes one small loaf. Serves four.

---

## Dessert Sauces

### Caramel Whipped Cream

*Ida Bailey Allen, · Original printed p. 121, scan p. 128 · `mg1930-p128-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup whipped cream
- 2drops vanilla
- 3 tbsp. Karo, blue or orange label

Beat the cream until thick, then gradually beat in the Karo and vanilla. The Karo keeps the cream from separating.

---

### Chocolate Sauce

*Hazel Menzie. · Original printed p. 121, scan p. 128 · `mg1930-p128-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 sq. chocolate melted in double boiler
- 4 tbsp. sugar-let melt, then add

Boil 10 minutes slowly then add ½ cup milk or cream gradually. Good hot poured over ice cream.

---

### Cold Chocolate Sauce

*Mrs. Lawrence Russel. · Original printed p. 121, scan p. 128 · `mg1930-p128-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup scalded milk
- 1/4 cup flour
- ½ cup sugar
- 1 egg
- ½ cup butter
- 2 sq. chocolate
- 1 cup powdered sugar
- milk
- vanilla
- salt

**Preparation**

Scald milk, pour on flour and sugar mixed together, add egg beaten. Cook to a paste. Melt butter and chocolate and mix into the paste. Add powdered sugar, vanilla and salt and thin to desired consistency with milk. This will keep soft for days.

---

### Fluffy Fruit Sauce

Use orange, pineapple, grape or loganberry juice. Beat the egg whites stiff and whip in the Karo and fruit juice. Serve at once. This is excellent instead of whipped cream.

### Foamy Sauce

- 2 eggs
- 1 cup whipped cream
- 1 cup powdered sugar
- 1 tsp. vanilla or brandy (if you

**Preparation**

have it) Beat eggs until yellow and thick, add sugar gradually, beating constantly. Fold in cream and flavoring.

Boil mixture, then add small tbsp. cornstarch mixed with a little cold water. Add juice of 1 lemon and a little lemon extract, when you remove sauce from stove.

### Lemon Pudding Sauce

Boil sugar, butter and water, add beaten egg and cook gently until thick.

### Orange Sauce

Cook sugar, lemon juice, orange rind and juice and egg yolks over a double boiler, stirring constantly until thick. Chill over ice; when cold, beat in ½ pint whipped cream. Serve with angel food cake. Makes a large recipe.

## Pastry

### Paste

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 123, scan p. 130 · `mg1930-p130-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- [Large Recipe]
- 1 lb. " Silverleaf" lard
- 1 tsp. baking powder
- 9 cups flour
- 3 tsp. salt

**Preparation**

Sift dry ingredients together into large mixing dish. Add lard, and mix with a pastry mixer, or lightly with the hands. Be careful not to mix too much. It is the small particles of lard which. rolled out, make the paste flaky. Store in a cold place. Add cold water to about 2 cups of this mixture for one double crust pie. Recipe makes about 5 double crust pies.

---

### Pie Crust

*Mrs. James L. Sigler. · Original printed p. 123, scan p. 130 · `mg1930-p130-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup flour
- 2 tbsp. lard (rounding )
- pinch salt
- cold water to make stick

**Preparation**

together Mix ingredients in order given.

---

### Pie Crust

*Mrs. Frank Geer. · Original printed p. 123, scan p. 130 · `mg1930-p130-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup flour
- ½ cup lard
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- 3 tbsp. water

**Preparation**

Cut lard into flour and salt, add water slowly. Make into ball. put on floured board and roll out.

---

### Pie Crust

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 123, scan p. 130 · `mg1930-p130-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1¼ cups cake flour
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- 1/4 tsp. baking powder
- 5 tbsp. shortening
- 1/4 cup cold water

**Preparation**

Whisk flour, salt and baking powder. Cut in shortening until the mixture resembles coarse crumbs. Add cold water a little at a time and gather into a dough. Chill 20–30 minutes, then roll out and fit a 9-inch pie dish; this amount makes one crust, not a full double-crust pie.

For a filled pie, start at 475°F for 5 minutes, then reduce to 375°F and bake until the filling is cooked and the pastry is golden; timing depends on the filling. For a blind-baked shell, line with weights, bake at 400°F for 12–15 minutes, remove weights and continue until golden.

---

### Hot Water Pie Crust

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 123, scan p. 130 · `mg1930-p130-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup " Silverleaf" lard
- 3 cups flour
- 2 tsp. salt
- 1 tsp. baking powder
- ½ cup boiling water

**Preparation**

Add boiling water to lard and salt. Sift in flour and baking powder. Let stand until cold, and store in a covered glass jar in refrigerator. Makes 2 double crust pies.

---

### Apple Pie

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 124, scan p. 131 · `mg1930-p131-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- pastry
- 1/4 cup brown sugar
- apples, rather tart
- 1/4 tsp. cinnamon, scant
- 1 tbsp. flour
- 1/4 tsp. nutmeg, scant
- 1 cup sugar
- butter

**Preparation**

Line tin with pastry, building it up around the edge. Mix flour, sugars and spices. Sprinkle a very little flour over the bottom crust, and add a little of the sugar mixture, and mix gently with the fingers. ( This is to keep the bottom crust from becoming soggy. ) Place about half of the sliced apples in the tin, and add about half of the sugar mixture. Then add remaining apples and rest of sugar mixture. Dot generously with butter. Cut the top crust just large enough to cover apples without reaching under crust. Bake 400 degrees 10 minutes 375 degrees 40 to 50 minutes.

---

### Butterscotch Pie

*Mrs. E. J. Anderson. · Original printed p. 124, scan p. 131 · `mg1930-p131-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ cups brown sugar
- 2 eggs
- 1½ cups boiling water
- 3 tbsp. butter
- 3 tbsp. flour
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- 3 tbsp. corn starch
- few grains salt

**Preparation**

Pour boiling water on sugar. Sift dry ingredients with 2 tbsp. white sugar. Add to first mixture. Cook in double boiler until thick. (About 30 minutes.) Separate eggs, beat yolks. Stir a little of thick mixture into eggs, then add eggs to mixture; cook 1 minute longer. Remove from fire, add vanilla and butter and put in baked pie crust. Beat egg whites, add ½ tsp. baking powder, beat till creamy. Put in oven and brown.

---

### Butterscotch Pie

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup brown sugar
- 2 tbsp. butter

**Preparation**

Melt together then add: 2 tbsp. corn starch Put 2 cups milk in double boiler, when warm add sugar mixture, yolks of 2 eggs and cook until thick. Flavor with vanilla. Beat whites of eggs and put on top and brown.

---

### Butterscotch Pie

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 124, scan p. 131 · `mg1930-p131-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 baked pastry shell
- 2 eggs, separated
- 2 cups milk
- 4 heaping tbsp. flour
- 1½ cups medium dark brown sugar
- 2 tbsp. butter
- 2 tsp. vanilla
- 2 tbsp. granulated sugar, for meringue

**Preparation**

Beat egg yolks and milk together, add butter, place over fire in double boiler. Mix brown sugar and flour thoroughly. Add to first mixture when hot, stirring frequently. Add vanilla after taking off stove. Pour into the baked shell, cover with merigue made from the 2 egg whites beaten stiff with 2 tbsp. granulated sugar. Brown in medium oven 350 degrees for 12 minutes.

---

### Chess Cakes

*Mrs. C. V. Pierce. · Original printed p. 125, scan p. 132 · `mg1930-p132-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup brown sugar
- 1 tsp. vanilla
- 4 egg yolks

**Preparation**

Cream sugar and butter, add egg yolks, and beat until creamy. Add vanilla and cocoanut. Line gem pans with rich pie crust, and place a spoonful of above mixture in each shell. Bake at 350 degrees until a golden brown. Makes one dozen. This is a splendid way of using egg yolks when making an angel food cake. Serve as French Pastry.

### Cocoanut Cream Pie

- 1/4 tsp. salt
- pastry

Mix cornstarch with a little cold water. Add rest of water, milk, and salt. Cook in double boiler until thickened, stirring constantly. Add cocoanut, eggs slightly beaten, and vanilla. Pour into plate lined with pastry and bake until firm. Time in combining, 5 minutes. Temperature, 400° 10 minutes, 325° 30 minutes.

---

### Cream Pie

Cook 6 tbsp. sugar, egg yolk, cornstarch, and milk until mixture thickens. Add vanilla, and pour into baked crust. Beat egg white until stiff, and fold in 1 tbsp. sugar. Place on top of filling. Bake for 20 minutes at 325 degrees.

Beat well together. 1 pt. milk in double boiler heating. Cook till thick.

### Lemon Fluff Pie Filling

### Lemon Pie

### Lemon Pie Filling

### Lemon Pie

Grate rind and extract juice of lemon. Put first four ingredients on stove to boil. Beat egg yolks well, add 2 to 3 tbsp. water and cornstarch, and add to first mixture when it boils. Cook until it thickens, and pour into a baked crust. Beat egg whites stiff. Sprinkle on 1 tbsp. sugar, and fold in. Add to top of pie, and bake 20 minutes at 325 degrees.

### Luncheon Pies

*Mrs. James Rork. · Original printed p. 127, scan p. 134 · `mg1930-p134-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup sugar
- 1 cup raisins
- 3 egg yolks
- ½ cup butter
- 1 cup boiling water
- 1 tbsp. cornstarch
- ¾ cup chopped nuts
- For shells: 1½ cups flour and ½ cup lard
- Whipped cream, to serve

**Preparation**

Make pastry from flour and lard with a little cold water. Roll and shape it over the backs of muffin tins; bake at 400°F until golden, about 12–15 minutes, then cool. For filling, simmer sugar, raisins, boiling water and butter until softened. Whisk egg yolks with cornstarch and some hot liquid to temper, then stir back into pan; cook gently until thick. Fold in nuts, cool completely and fill pastry shells. Serve with whipped cream.

### Orange Pie

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 127, scan p. 134 · `mg1930-p134-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup sugar
- 1 tsp. orange rind, grated
- 3 tbsp. flour
- 1 cup orange juice
- 3 tbsp. cornstarch
- 2 egg yolks
- ⅛ tsp. salt
- 2 tsp. butter
- ½ cup water
- 2 tbsp. lemon juice

**Preparation**

Mix sugar, flour, cornstarch and enough cold water to moisten. Add the hot water and bring to the boiling point directly over the heat, stirring continuously. Remove from the heat, add orange juice and rind, stir until well mixed and cook in double boiler for 20 minutes. Add butter, lemon, and slightly beaten egg yolks, and cook until eggs thicken, about 3 minutes. Cool slightly and pour into baked crust. Cover with meringue and bake at 325 degrees for about 12 minutes.

---

### Pineapple Pie

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 127, scan p. 134 · `mg1930-p134-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- Plain or puff paste
- 2 eggs
- 1 ripe pineapple
- 1/4 cup water
- 1 level tbsp. flour
- level tsp. salt
- 3/4 cup sugar
- 6 level tbsp. powdered sugar

**Preparation**

Time: Preparation and baking. Bake 30 minutes 400°. Bake meringue 15 minutes 325°.



Pare pineapple, chop fine. Measure three cupfuls. Add sugar, cook until tender, about thirty minutes. Add flour moistened with water cook five minutes. Remove from fire, add yolks and salt. Line a pie-plate with a well-made paste, rolled thin. Pour in the pineapple, bake in a hot oven thirty minutes. Beat whites until stiff,

adding pinch of salt; add powdered sugar gradually. Heap meringue on top of pie, sprinkle with sugar, brown in moderate oven. Serve cool. For a flaky paste, allow one-third cupful shortening to threefourths cupful flour. Work one-half into flour, moisten with ice water, pat, roll out; fold in remainder, pat, roll; repeat.

---

### Pineapple Pie

*Mrs. C. V. Pierce. · Original printed p. 128, scan p. 135 · `mg1930-p135-r01`*

- 1 cup sugar
- 1 cup chopped pineapple, strained
- 1 tbsp. melted butter
- ½ cup pineapple juice

- 3 egg whites

**Preparation**

yolks 3 eggs Cream together sugar, butter and cornstarch, and then add egg yolks, then pineapple and juice. Fold in stiffly beaten egg whites and bake in one rich crust which has not been previously baked. Bake 5 minutes at 400 degrees and 30 minutes at 350 degrees.

---

### Pumpkin Pie

*Mrs. L. D. Barnhart. · Original printed p. 128, scan p. 135 · `mg1930-p135-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 tbsp. butter
- ½ tsp. salt
- 3/4 cup sugar
- 1/4 tsp. cinnamon, nutmeg, ginger
- 2 eggs
- 1¼ cups pumpkin
- 1 cup milk

**Preparation**

Mix in order given and bake slowly in raw crust. This recipe never fails. Bake at 400 degrees for 10 minutes and then at 350 degrees for about 30 minutes.

---

### Pumpkin Pie

*Mrs. V. Paul Van Keuren. · Original printed p. 128, scan p. 135 · `mg1930-p135-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups stewed pumpkin
- 2 eggs
- 2 cups rich milk or cream
- ¼ tsp. ginger
- 3/4 cup brown or white sugar
- ½ tsp. salt
- 1 tsp. cinnamon

**Preparation**

Mix pumpkin with milk, sugar, and well beaten eggs, ginger salt and cinnamon. Beat 2 minutes. Pour in unbaked pie crust. Put in hot oven, 400 degrees, for fifteen minutes. Reduce heat and bake forty- five minutes or more at 350 degrees. To bring out flavor of pumpkin it must be well baked.

---

### Rhubarb Custard Pie

*Mrs. J. W. Spalsbury. · Original printed p. 128, scan p. 135 · `mg1930-p135-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups diced rhubarb
- 1 cup sugar
- 1 tbsp. butter
- 1 tbsp. flour
- 2 eggs
- 3 tbsp. powdered sugar
- salt

**Preparation**

Pour enough boiling water over rhubarb to almost cover, add sugar, flour, butter. Bring to boiling point, cook until smooth and thick. Beat one whole egg and 1 yolk and stir in. Turn into piepan lined with unbaked shell. Bake until crust is done. Use white of egg to make merignue and brown in oven.

---

## Preserves, Pickles, and Relishes

### Cocktail Sauce

*Mrs. L. D. Barnhart. · Original printed p. 129, scan p. 136 · `mg1930-p136-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup tomato catsup
- 1 tsp. horseradish
- 1 lemon, juice

**Preparation**

Mix and chill. Good for any meat. The amount of horseradish varies with the strength.

---

### Chili Sauce

*Mrs. Carl R. Johnson. · Original printed p. 129, scan p. 136 · `mg1930-p136-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ peck tomatoes
- 2 large onions
- 4 red peppers
- 2½ cups vinegar
- 2 cups brown sugar
- 2 tsp. salt
- 1 tbsp. stick cinnamon
- 1 tbsp. cloves

**Preparation**

Core and chop tomatoes, onions and peppers. Put cinnamon and cloves in a tied spice bag. Combine vegetables, vinegar, brown sugar and salt in a heavy pot; add spice bag. Simmer uncovered over medium-low heat, stirring occasionally, about 60–90 minutes or until thick enough to spoon. Remove spice bag. Refrigerate after cooling.

---

### A Winter Chili Sauce

*Mrs. M. E. Clark. · Original printed p. 129, scan p. 136 · `mg1930-p136-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cans tomatoes, diced
- 2 tbsp. sugar
- 3 green peppers, diced
- 1 tsp. cloves
- 2 onions, diced
- 1 cup vinegar
- 1 tbsp. cinnamon

**Preparation**

Chop tomatoes, green peppers and onions. Combine with sugar, cloves, vinegar and cinnamon in a heavy saucepan. Simmer uncovered over medium-low heat, stirring occasionally, about 45–75 minutes until reduced to a spoonable sauce. Cool and refrigerate.

---

### Uncooked Chili Sauce

*Mrs. Frank Geer. · Original printed p. 129, scan p. 136 · `mg1930-p136-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ peck ripe tomatoes
- ½ cup salt
- ½ cup sugar
- 2 cups celery
- 2 cups onions
- 2 small seeded peppers
- 1 cup white mustard seed
- 1 tbsp. black pepper
- 1 tbsp. cinnamon
- 1 qt. vinegar

**Preparation**

Chop or grind vegetables, mix all ingredients and let stand in crock. It makes 1 gallon.

---

### Chili Sauce

*Mrs. Frank Geer. · Original printed p. 129, scan p. 136 · `mg1930-p136-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pk. ripe tomatoes
- 3 green peppers
- 1 qt. chopped onions
- 3 red peppers
- ½ cup white mustard seed
- 3 cups vinegar
- 1 tsp. cinnamon
- 1½ cups sugar
- 4 tbsp. salt
- 1 tsp. cloves
- red pepper to taste

**Preparation**

Cook tomatoes, peppers, and onions 1 hour. Add the remaining ingredients and cook until thick.

---

### Pepper Hash

*Mrs. M. Ellis · Original printed p. 129, scan p. 136 · `mg1930-p136-r06`*

**Ingredients**

- 12 green peppers
- 6 large onions
- 8 red peppers

**Preparation**

Chop fine and cover with boiling water. Let stand 10 minutes, and drain water off and cover again with fresh water and let it come to a boil. Drain again and add 1 pt. of vinegar, 1½ cups white sugar, 1 tbsp. salt and boil 15 or 20 minutes. Put in cans.

---

### Relish

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 130, scan p. 137 · `mg1930-p137-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 12 green tomatoes
- 2 cups vinegar
- 12 medium sized onions
- 3 cups sugar
- 12 cucumbers
- 1 tsp. black mustard seed
- 3 green peppers
- 1 tsp. yellow mustard seed
- 3 red peppers
- 1 tsp. celery seed

**Preparation**

Grind tomatoes, peppers, cucumber and onions. Add vinegar, sugar, seeds and salt. Cook for 30 minutes. Put in jars and seal.

---

### Winter Salad

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 130, scan p. 137 · `mg1930-p137-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 qts. green tomatoes
- 1 tbsp. turmeric
- 4 qts. shredded cabbage
- 2 tbsp. mustard seed
- 1½ qts. little white onions
- 4 tbsp. salt
- 6 red peppers ( sweet)
- 1 cup sugar
- 2 qts. vinegar, dilute if very strong

**Preparation**

Slice green tomatoes and let stand over night in brine. Then drain and chop. Parboil onions in weak vinegar water about 10 minutes. Chop red peppers and mix all together. Mix turmeric, mustard seed, salt and sugar and vinegar and heat. Then let cool and pour over chopped ingredients.

---

### Corn Relish

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 130, scan p. 137 · `mg1930-p137-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 24 ears corn, kernels cut from cob
- 6 red peppers
- 6 green peppers
- 6 large onions
- 1 large cabbage
- ½ cup salt
- 1 qt. vinegar, for first cooking
- 1½ qts. chopped cucumbers
- 4 cups sugar
- ¾ tsp. ground mustard
- 3 tbsp. mustard seed
- 3 tbsp. celery seed
- 1 qt. vinegar, for second cooking

**Preparation**

Cook the corn, peppers, onions, cabbage, salt, and first quart of vinegar for 20 minutes. Add chopped cucumbers, sugar, ground mustard, mustard seed, celery seed, and another quart of vinegar. Mix and cook 20 minutes more. Seal tightly in jars.

---

### Corn Relish

*Mrs. J. L. Stewart. · Original printed p. 130, scan p. 137 · `mg1930-p137-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 18 ears corn ( 10 cups)
- 4 tbsp. mustard seed
- 10 cups chopped celery
- 3 large onions
- 1 large head cabbage
- ½ gal. vinegar
- 2 green peppers
- 2 level tbsp. salt
- 2 red peppers
- 1 or 2 cups sugar

**Preparation**

Cut corn from cob, put celery, cabbage, onions, and peppers through food chopper, using coarse knife. Add the mustard seed, vinegar, salt and sugar, and cook slowly for one hour. Recipe makes `eleven quarts of relish.

---

### Cucumber Relish

*Mrs. R. Mickey. · Original printed p. 130, scan p. 137 · `mg1930-p137-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 12 large cucumbers
- 6 large onions

**Preparation**

Peel and slice thin. Soak in salt water for a few hours. Drain. Take 1 pt. of vinegar and add 1 cup sugar, 1 tsp. white mustard seed, 1 tsp. celery seed, 1 tsp. whole allspice and 2 level tsp. turmeric. Cook all together until tender and can.

---

### Sweet Tomato Pickles

*Mrs. Paul Snauble. · Original printed p. 131, scan p. 138 · `mg1930-p138-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pk. green tomatoes
- 2 qts. water
- 6 large onions, sliced
- 1 qt. vinegar
- 1 cup salt

**Preparation**

Sprinkle salt on tomatoes and onions, and let stand over night. Drain, and add water and vinegar. Boil 15 minutes then drain again and throw the vinegar and water away. Add to the pickle: 2 lbs. sugar 2 tbsp. mustard 2 qts. vinegar 2 tbsp. allspice 2 tbsp. cloves 2 tbsp. cinnamon 2 tbsp. ginger 1 tsp. cayenne Boil 15 minutes. (Allspice and cloves should be tied in bag. ) Will keep in stone jar.

---

### Celery Ice Pickles

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 131, scan p. 138 · `mg1930-p138-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- Medium cucumbers, enough to fill the jars
- 1 large onion slice per jar
- 6 pieces fresh celery per jar
- 2 tsp. celery seed per jar
- 1 qt. vinegar
- 1 cup sugar
- ½ cup salt

**Preparation**

Cut scrubbed cucumbers lengthwise and soak in ice water for 4 hours. Put an onion slice, celery pieces and celery seed in each clean jar; pack with drained cucumbers. Bring vinegar, sugar and salt to a boil, then cool completely before pouring over cucumbers. Refrigerate.

---

### Turmeric Pickles

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 131, scan p. 138 · `mg1930-p138-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 qts. peeled and sliced cucumbers
- 1 qt. sliced onions

**Preparation**

Let stand in weak brine two hours. Boil together 1 qt. vinegar, 1/2 cup salt, 3 cups brown sugar, 1 tbsp. celery seed and ½ tsp. turmeric powder. Thicken with 2 tbsp. flour in a little water. Drain pickles and onions, put all together and boil ten minutes.

---

### Oil Pickles

*Mrs. Frank Geer. · Original printed p. 131, scan p. 138 · `mg1930-p138-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 100 medium cucumbers, sliced
- 1 oz. white pepper
- 1½ cups salt
- 1 oz. white mustard seed
- 1 oz. celery seed
- 3 pints small onions
- 1 pint olive oil
- Vinegar, enough to cover

**Preparation**

Let the sliced cucumbers stand with the salt for 3 hours. Drain; add small onions, white pepper, mustard seed, celery seed and olive oil. Cover with vinegar and seal in cans.

---

### Sliced Pickles

*Mrs. J. W. Spalsbury. · Original printed p. 131, scan p. 138 · `mg1930-p138-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 8 or 10 medium sized cucumbers
- 8 onions
- 2 stalks celery. Slice and let stand in salt water over night.

**Preparation**

Do not peel cucumbers 1 qt. vinegar (dilute a little) 2 cups sugar 1 tsp. mustard seed 1 tsp. black pepper 1 tsp. turmeric powder Put all together and let boil up good, but not too long so as to make pickles soft.

---

### Small Cucumber Pickles

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 132, scan p. 139 · `mg1930-p139-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- Small cucumbers, enough to fill jars
- 1 gallon vinegar
- 1 cup mustard
- 1 cup salt
- 2 cups sugar
- Pickling spices, to taste

**Preparation**

Scrub pickles and pack into jars. To one gallon of vinegar add 1 cup mustard, 1 cup salt and 2 cups sugar. Spice to taste. Mix and pour over pickles cold. Seal jars and invert for several days until mustard has worked through well.

---

### Aristocrat Pickles

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 132, scan p. 139 · `mg1930-p139-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 peck small cucumbers (about quarter-sized)
- 11 cups sugar
- 3 lb. small silverskin onions
- 3 red sweet peppers
- 1 tbsp. yellow mustard seeds
- 1 tbsp. celery seeds
- 7 cups vinegar
- Strong salt brine, to cover vegetables

**Preparation**

Slice the cucumbers. Blanch onions briefly with boiling water, then peel; chop sweet peppers. Soak cucumbers and onions separately in strong brine for 3 hours, then drain well. Bring vinegar, sugar, mustard seeds and celery seeds to a boil. Add vegetables and gently boil 10 minutes. Cool and refrigerate.

---

### Mustard Pickles

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 132, scan p. 139 · `mg1930-p139-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 gallon cucumbers
- 1 cup salt
- 1 cup sugar
- 1 cup mustard
- Vinegar, enough to cover

**Preparation**

To 1 Gal. of pickles add: - 1 cup salt 1 cup sugar Vinegar to cover 1 cup mustard

---

### Chunk Pickles

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 132, scan p. 139 · `mg1930-p139-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- Cucumbers, as needed
- Strong brine, for soaking
- Weak vinegar, for simmering
- Pinch powdered alum (historical ingredient; omit in edited preparation)
- 3 lb. brown sugar
- 3 pints vinegar
- 1 oz. stick cinnamon
- 1 oz. celery seed
- 1 oz. allspice

**Preparation**

Soak whole cucumbers in strong brine for 3 days. Drain and soak in fresh water for 3 days, changing water daily. Cut into chunks and simmer gently in weak vinegar until tender, about 1–2 hours; drain. Boil brown sugar, vinegar, cinnamon, celery seed and allspice into a syrup and pour over the chunks. Drain, reheat and pour back the syrup once daily for 3 days. Cool and refrigerate.

---

### Chunk Pickles

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 132, scan p. 139 · `mg1930-p139-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 10 lbs. cucumbers, cut in chunks
- 1½ lbs. salt
- Water, to cover
- Weak vinegar, for initial cooking
- Small piece alum
- Syrup: 3 pints vinegar
- Syrup: 3 lbs. sugar
- Syrup: 1 oz. whole cloves
- Syrup: 1 oz. stick cinnamon
- Syrup: 1 oz. celery seed

**Preparation**

Cover cucumber chunks and salt with water; let stand 3 days. Drain and soak in clear water 3 days. Drain, then cook in weak vinegar with a small piece of alum for 2 hours. Combine syrup ingredients and bring to a boil; pour over pickles and can.

---

### Ripe Cucumber Pickles

*Mrs. C. V. Pierce. · Original printed p. 132, scan p. 139 · `mg1930-p139-r06`*

**Ingredients**

- Very ripe yellow cucumbers
- 2 tsp. powdered alum (historical ingredient; omitted in edited preparation)
- 1 qt. water
- 1 qt. vinegar
- 2 lb. sugar
- 2 tbsp. whole cloves
- 2 tbsp. stick cinnamon
- 1 tbsp. salt

**Preparation**

Peel very ripe yellow cucumbers; discard seeds and soft centers, and cut firm flesh into strips. Simmer strips very briefly in water, then drain and chill. Tie cloves and cinnamon in a spice bag. Simmer vinegar, sugar, salt and spice bag until sugar dissolves. Add cucumber strips and simmer 10–15 minutes, then cool in syrup. Refrigerate overnight; drain and reheat syrup once more before storing refrigerated.

---

### Currant Relish

*Mrs. C. A. Buskirk. · Original printed p. 133, scan p. 140 · `mg1930-p140-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 5 qts. currants
- 2 oranges
- 5 pounds sugar
- 1 lb. raisins

**Preparation**

Grate outside of orange, pare off white and slice inside. Mix, and cook until as thick as jelly.

---

### Spiced Currants

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 133, scan p. 140 · `mg1930-p140-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 lbs. fruit
- 2 lbs. sugar
- 1 tbsp. cinnamon
- ½ tbsp. allspice
- ½ tbsp. cloves
- ½ pt. vinegar

**Preparation**

Boil ½ hour, seal in glass jars. Brown sugar may be used and gooseberries or cherries may be used in place of currants.

---

### Cherry And Raspberry Conserve

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 133, scan p. 140 · `mg1930-p140-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 box cherries or currants
- 1 box raspberries
- 1 qt. rhubarb, chopped
- 2 oranges, zest and juice
- 2 lemons, zest and juice
- 3 lb. sugar
- Raisins and chopped nuts, optional

**Preparation**

Add raisins and nuts if desired. Boil until it jells.

---

### Spiced Gooseberries

*Mrs. E. J. Anderson. · Original printed p. 133, scan p. 140 · `mg1930-p140-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 qts. gooseberries, stemmed and washed
- 1 small cup vinegar
- 1½ cups brown sugar
- cinnamon and cloves to taste

**Preparation**

Cook until thick. Put in jelly glasses. Delicious with meats.

---

### Mint Jelly

*Mrs. J. W. Spalsbury. · Original printed p. 133, scan p. 140 · `mg1930-p140-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1¾ cups water
- 1/4 cup vinegar
- 2 tbsp. sugar
- 1/4 cup chopped mint leaves
- 1 package lemon Jello
- ½ cup green cherries

**Preparation**

Let this come to boil. Strain and dissolve 1 pkg. lemon Jello in this liquid. As it begins to set stir ½ cup green cherries in it.

---

### Pear Jam

*Mrs. Harry Becker. · Original printed p. 134, scan p. 141 · `mg1930-p141-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 lbs. pears
- 1 lemon
- 1 lb. figs
- 3 lbs. sugar
- 2 oranges

**Preparation**

The pears should be peeled and run through food chopper. The figs are also run through the chopper. Grate the yellow off the oranges and lemon, being careful not to get any of the white, as that makes it bitter. Remove the white and seeds, put rest of fruit through the chopper. Add the grated rind and sugar. Boil slowly. This doesn't have to be sealed air tight.

---

### Peach Conserve

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 134, scan p. 141 · `mg1930-p141-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 lbs. peaches, cut fine
- 3 oranges, grated rind of 2, juice of 3
- 1 small can pineapple
- 3½ lbs. sugar

**Preparation**

Combine peaches, pineapple, sugar, orange juice and grated orange zest in a wide heavy pan. Bring to a simmer; cook, stirring often, approximately 1¼ hours, until the fruit is translucent and the conserve has visibly thickened. Cool and refrigerate. Makes about 12–14 small glasses.

---

### Pineapple Jam

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 134, scan p. 141 · `mg1930-p141-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- Pineapple, grated or ground
- Sugar, 1 lb. per 1 lb. prepared pineapple

**Preparation**

Grate pineapples or put them through the grinder. Allow 1 lb. sugar to 1 lb. fruit. Cook 3/4 hour or until clear. Put in glasses and seal.

---

### Peach Conserve

*Mrs. J. W. Spalsbury. · Original printed p. 134, scan p. 141 · `mg1930-p141-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 5 lbs. peaches
- 3 lbs. sugar
- 2 small bottles Maraschino cherries

**Preparation**

Slice peaches thin. Add sugar, and cook until it begins to thicken, then add cherries, cut in small pieces, and cook until thick as conserve.

---

### Orange Marmalade

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 134, scan p. 141 · `mg1930-p141-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 oranges
- 4 lbs. sugar (2 qts.)
- 1 grapefruit
- 3 qts. water
- 1 lemon

**Preparation**

First day: Wash and slice whole fruit very thin. Cover with cold water. Second day: Put above on to boil, slowly, until tender, about 1 hour. Pinch between finger to try. Remove from fire, and add 4 lbs. sugar while mixture is hot. Stir several times while cooling. Third day: Put on stove again, and bring to boil, stirring often to prevent burning. Let boil several hours, until it will jell. Makes 11 glasses. Use porcelain, enamel, or aluminum kettle, and wooden spoon.

---

### Orange Marmalade

*Mrs. Paul Snauble. · Original printed p. 135, scan p. 142 · `mg1930-p142-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 naval oranges
- 3 yellow skinned grapefruit
- 3 lemons
- 5 lbs. granulated sugar

**Preparation**

Wash and slice fruit-the thinner the better and let stand 12 hours in 1 gallon water. Bring to boiling point, add sugar, which has been heated in the oven, and boil slowly for 1½ hour. If put in glasses it will thicken.

---

### Rhubarb And Fig Preserves

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 135, scan p. 142 · `mg1930-p142-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 lbs. rhubarb
- 3/4 lb. candied orange peel
- 1 lb. figs
- 3½ lbs. sugar
- 2 lemons

**Preparation**

Cut rhubarb into inch pieces and figs into strips. Add juice and grated rind of lemons, orange peel and sugar. Let stand in preserving kettle over night. Cook slowly about 1 hour or until figs are tender. Stir occasionally.

---

### Strawberry Preserves

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 135, scan p. 142 · `mg1930-p142-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 cups berries
- 5 cups granulated sugar

**Preparation**

Put in large kettle, covering berries with sugar. Put over simmering burner, turned as low as possible, until sugar dissolves. Turn on gas full, and let cook vigorously for 10 minutes. Set aside to cool all night. Can cold in sterilized jars in the morning. 8 cups berries make 2/2 qts. preserves. 3 qts. berries make 8 cups. If you double recipe, use 2 kettles.

---

### Strawberry Preserves

*Mrs. J. W. Spalsbury. · Original printed p. 135, scan p. 142 · `mg1930-p142-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 qts. berries

**Preparation**

Cover with boiling water and let stand 5 minutes. Drain. Add 4 cups sugar and boil 7 minutes. Add 2 more cups sugar and let boil 5 minutes. Pour into glasses.

---

### Strawberry And Pineapple Jam

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 135, scan p. 142 · `mg1930-p142-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups grated pineapple
- 3 tbsp. lemon juice
- 2 cups canned strawberries
- ½ cup commercial pectin
- 3 cups sugar

**Preparation**

Mix fruit and sugar and boil gently for ten minutes. Add pectin and boil rapidly for five minutes. Pour into hot sterilized jars. Time in combining, 5 minutes. Time in cooking, 15 minutes. Recipe makes six 6-ounce jars.

---

### Pickled Peaches

*Esther Winters. · Original printed p. 136, scan p. 143 · `mg1930-p143-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- Peaches, peeled (1 qt. prepared)
- 3 whole cloves per peach
- 2 cups sugar
- 1 cup vinegar
- Stick cinnamon, to taste

**Preparation**

Pare peaches by pouring hot water over them, then put 3 cloves in each peach. For each quart of peaches, make a syrup of 2 cups of sugar and one cup of vinegar, with some stick cinnamon in it. After syrup boils put in peaches and boil (8) minutes.

---

### Water Melon Rind Pickles

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 136, scan p. 143 · `mg1930-p143-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- Rind 1 large water melon
- 7 lbs. granulated sugar
- 1 gal. water
- 1 pkg. mixed spices
- 3 tbsp. salt
- stick cinnamon, cloves, cassia
- 2 qts. cider vinegar
- bud
- 1 qt. Maraschino cherries

**Preparation**

Cut peeled rind in pieces, and put into kettle with 1 gal. water and salt. Let stand over night. Boil in same brine until tender. Rinse and drain well and wipe dry. Add sugar to vinegar, and spices in a bag, boil 20 minutes. Add fruit and boil 40 minutes. Let stand over night. Then bring to boiling point again; remove from stove and add cherries. Put into stone jar. (These are delicious, and look so pretty, with the red coloring. )

---

## Salad Dressings

### French Dressing

*Louise Reifsnyder. · Original printed p. 139, scan p. 146 · `mg1930-p146-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ tsp. dry mustard
- 2 tbsp. sugar
- 1 tbsp. Worcestershire Sauce
- ½ cup catsup
- 1 tsp. pepper
- ½ cup vinegar
- 1 tsp. salt, scant
- 3/4 cup oil
- 1 tsp. paprika
- 5 drops Tobasco sauce

**Preparation**

Beat or shake in closed bottle until creamy.

---

### Tasty French Dressing

*Mrs. Harris Meyer. · Original printed p. 139, scan p. 146 · `mg1930-p146-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 tbsp. salad oil
- 2 tbsp. of catsup
- juice of ½ lemon
- 1 teaspoon of sugar
- ½ teaspoon salt

**Preparation**

Mix well, chill and serve on crisp head lettuce. A hard boiled egg mashed may be added to this dressing just before serving.

---

### French Dressing, One Pint

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 139, scan p. 146 · `mg1930-p146-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ tsp. dry mustard
- 1 tsp. salt
- ½ tsp. white pepper
- 2 tbsp. sugar
- 1 tsp. paprika
- 2 tbsp. catsup

**Preparation**

oil and vinegar

Put seasonings in a pint Mason jar, and fill one half full with a good salad oil or olive oil. Finish filling jar with one half cider vinegar and one half tarragon vinegar. Place rubber and top on jar, and shake hard until mixture is creamy.

---

### Tomato French Dressing

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 139, scan p. 146 · `mg1930-p146-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup salad oil
- ½ tsp. sugar
- 2 tbsp. lemon juice
- clove garlic
- 1 tbsp. catsup
- ½ tsp. paprika
- 1 tsp. salt
- ½ tsp. mustard

**Preparation**

Put seasonings in a cold bowl, the inside of which has been rubbed with the clove of garlic. Then add the oil slowly, while beating constantly. Next add the lemon juice and catsup, continuing to beat until thickened. Serve very cold. Serves 8.

---

### Mazola Mayonnaise

*Mrs. D. Boudeman. · Original printed p. 139, scan p. 146 · `mg1930-p146-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ tsp. mustard
- 1 whole egg
- ½ tsp. salt
- 1 cup Mazola oil
- pinch paprika
- 1 tbsp. vinegar or lemon juice

**Preparation**

Mix dry ingredients; then add egg and stir. Add Mazola oil a tablespoon full at a time, beating continually with Dover egg beater. At last add vinegar or lemon juice and beat in thoroughly.

---

### Mayonnaise

*Mrs. Wm. J. Herring. · Original printed p. 140, scan p. 147 · `mg1930-p147-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 tbsp. sugar
- ½ cup vinegar
- 2 tsp. mustard
- 3/4 cup Mazola oil
- 2 eggs
- 4 tbsp. cornstarch
- 1 tsp. salt
- 1 cup water
- 1/4 tsp. paprika

**Preparation**

Make a paste of cornstarch and water and cook until thick. Mix all other ingredients, and add paste and beat well with rotary beater. Chill.

---

### Cooked Mayonnaise With Oil

*Hazel Menzie. · Original printed p. 140, scan p. 147 · `mg1930-p147-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 tbsp. oil, for white sauce
- ½ cup flour
- 1 cup cold water, for white sauce
- 2 egg yolks
- 1 tsp. sugar
- 2 tsp. salt
- 1 tsp. dry mustard
- 1 tbsp. vinegar
- 1 tbsp. lemon juice
- 1 cup oil, added gradually while hot

**Preparation**

Make a white sauce with 3 tbsp. oil, flour and cold water. While hot, gradually pour in 1 cup of oil. The source gives no further mixing directions for the egg yolks and seasonings. Nice for Thousand Island Dressing.

---

### Salad Dressing

*Mrs. M. E. Clark. · Original printed p. 140, scan p. 147 · `mg1930-p147-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 egg yolks
- 1/2 cup water
- 2 tbsp. water
- ½ cup vinegar
- 2 tbsp. sugar
- 1 tbsp. butter
- 1 tsp. salt
- 2 egg whites
- 1 tbsp. flour
- 1 tsp. mustard

**Preparation**

Beat well egg yolks and the 2 tbsp. water. Put dry ingredients into a bowl, and beat in gradually the first mixture. Add vinegar and water. Cook until thick. Add butter. When cool add stiffly beaten egg whites.

---

### Salad Dressing

*Mrs. V. Paul Van Keuren. · Original printed p. 140, scan p. 147 · `mg1930-p147-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 tbsp. sugar
- 2 eggs
- 3 tsp. salt
- 1½ cups diluted vinegar
- 3 tsp. flour (rounding)
- lump butter
- 3 tsp. mustard
- 1 small can Pet milk

**Preparation**

Put sugar, salt, flour, mustard, eggs in double boiler and mix well. Add diluted vinegar, stir and set on stove to cook. When thick add butter and milk, stirring continually. This will keep indefinitely.

---

### Salad Dressing

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 140, scan p. 147 · `mg1930-p147-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 tsp. mustard
- 1 tbsp. flour
- 2 tbsp. sugar
- ½ tsp. salt
- 2 eggs
- ½ cup vinegar
- 1 tbsp. butter

**Preparation**

Sift dry ingredients, add eggs slowly, vinegar and butter. Cook until it thickens. Beat smooth with egg beater.

---

### Home Salad Dressing

*Mrs. C. A. Buskirk. · Original printed p. 141, scan p. 148 · `mg1930-p148-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 tbsp. flour
- ½ cup sugar
- 1 tsp. mustard
- 1½ cups water
- 1 tsp. salt
- 1 cup vinegar
- 1/4 tsp. paprika
- 3 eggs, well beaten

**Preparation**

Mix dry ingredients, adding water, vinegar, and eggs. Cook in double boiler until thick and creamy, stirring frequently. Pour into jar and when cool, cover. Thin with whipped cream, sour or sweet cream, or French dressing.

---

### Boiled Dressing

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 141, scan p. 148 · `mg1930-p148-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ tsp. salt
- 2 egg yolks
- 1 tsp. mustard
- 1½ tbsp. melted butter
- 3 to 5 tbsp. sugar
- 1 cup milk
- 1 tbsp. flour
- 1/4 cup vinegar

**Preparation**

Mix dry ingredients, add egg yolks slightly beaten, butter, milk, and vinegar very slowly. Cook over boiling water until thick; strain and cool.

---

### Salad Dressing

*Mrs. C. L. Mohney. · Original printed p. 141, scan p. 148 · `mg1930-p148-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 egg
- 1 tsp. Colman's Dry Mustard
- ½ cup sugar
- ½ tsp. salt
- 1 tablespoon flour
- 1/4 tsp. pepper
- 1/4 cup water
- ½ cup vinegar

**Preparation**

Mix dry ingredients, add egg; mix well and stir in vinegar and water; cook in double boiler until thick. Very good and easy to make.

---

### Thousand Island Dressing

*Mrs. W. J. Herring. · Original printed p. 141, scan p. 148 · `mg1930-p148-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 tbsp. green pepper
- 4 tbsp. pimento
- 2 tbsp. onion
- 4 tbsp. parsley
- 4 hard-boiled eggs
- 4 tbsp. chili sauce
- 1 cup vinegar
- ½ cup Mazola oil
- 4 tbsp. white sugar
- 4 tbsp. brown sugar
- 1 tsp. dry mustard
- 4 tbsp. lemon juice
- ½ tsp. salt

**Preparation**

Chop green pepper, pimento, onion, parsley and eggs fine; mix in chili sauce. Beat vinegar with the Mazola oil. Add sugars, dry mustard, lemon juice and salt; mix and shake well. Serve over lettuce.

---

### Thousand Island Dressing

*Mrs. C. W. Lininger. · Original printed p. 141, scan p. 148 · `mg1930-p148-r05`*

- 1 pickle, medium size
- 1 tomato
- salt to taste
- 3 pieces celery
- paprika
- 4 radishes,button size
- 1 cup oil mayonnaise
- 1 hard boiled egg

**Preparation**

Run vegetables through a food chopper or chop fine with egg. Mix with mayonnaise and chili sauce.

---

### Thousand Island Dressing

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 142, scan p. 149 · `mg1930-p149-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 hard boiled egg
- 14 green pepper
- 3 sweet gherkins
- 14 pimento
- 6 green olives

Chop above very fine. Mix with 1/2 cup good oil mayonnaise and 1/2 cup chili sauce. Onion may be added if desired.

---

### Thousand Island Dressing

*Mrs. Geo. Williamson. · Original printed p. 142, scan p. 149 · `mg1930-p149-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 12 olives
- ½ medium-sized pimento

- ½ cup vinegar
- 1 hard-cooked egg
- 1 tbsp. mayonnaise
- Salt and white pepper

**Preparation**

Put olives, green pepper, pimento and beet through food chopper or chop fine. Press egg through sieve and add with mayonnaise vinegar and seasoning. Just before serving fold in whipped cream.

---

### One Pt. 1000 Island Dressing

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 142, scan p. 149 · `mg1930-p149-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 sweet pickles
- 2 slices pimento
- 1 small onion

- Chili sauce (⅓ of sauce mixture)
- Oil mayonnaise (⅔ of sauce mixture)

**Preparation**

Chop eggs, pickles, pimento, onion and green pepper very fine. Finish filling a pint jar with one-third chili sauce and two-thirds oil mayonnaise.

---

### Dressing For Cabbage Salad

*Mrs. CoraG. Marhoff. · Original printed p. 142, scan p. 149 · `mg1930-p149-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 tbsp. flour
- ½ cup sugar
- ½ cup vinegar
- ½ cup cream, sweet or sour
- 1 tsp. salt

**Preparation**

Mix dry ingredients, and stir in hot vinegar slowly. Boil until very thick. Add cream immediately, stirring until smooth. Condensed milk may be used in place of cream, and add a little butter. Salt chopped cabbage and add dressing. Recipe makes enough for a large head of cabbage.

CREAM CHEESE DRESSING FOR FRUIT SALAD 1 cream cheese 2 tbsp. cream 1 tbsp. lemon juice 1 glass currant jelly 3/4 cup whipped cream salt

Mix cheese to a paste with the cream, add currant jelly and stir thoroughly. Add whipped cream. Use over fruit salad that has been mixed with regular dressing.

### Fruit Salad Dressing

- 2 eggs, beaten
- ½ cup sugar
- 2 tbsp. wet mustard
- 1 cup Eagle Brand milk
- salt and pepper

**Preparation**

Mix in order given, and cook in double boiler until thick.

---

### A Good Cabbage Dressing

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 143, scan p. 150 · `mg1930-p150-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 tsp. salt
- ½ cup sweetened condensed milk
- 1/4 tsp. paprika
- ½ cup vinegar
- 1/8 tsp. white pepper
- 3 cups cabbage, cut fine

**Preparation**

To condensed milk, gradually add vinegar to which salt, paprika and pepper have been mixed. Stir until thoroughly blended. Soak cabbage at least an hour in ice water to make crisp. Dry and cut fine. Mix with dressing when ready to serve. Finely chopped green pepper may be added.
