## Salads

### Adirondack Salad

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 145, scan p. 152 · `mg1930-p152-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 can large peas, drained
- 2 medium sized onions, cut fine
- ½ lb. cheese, diced
- 2 hard cooked eggs, sliced
- 4 sweet pickles, cut fine
- ½ can pimentos
- salt to taste

**Preparation**

Mix with mayonnaise (thinned with whipped cream if desired).

---

### Almond And Pickle Salad

*Mrs. C. D. Brown. · Original printed p. 145, scan p. 152 · `mg1930-p152-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup vinegar
- 1 cup sweet cucumber pickles
- 3/4 cup sugar
- ½ cup almonds
- 2 tbsp. gelatine
- ⅔ cup crushed pineapple
- 1 cup pineapple juice

**Preparation**

Cook vinegar and sugar until it spins a thread. Soak gelatine in pineapple juice, and dissolve in hot syrup. Blanch and slice almonds, slice pickles, and add with pineapple to first mixture when it is cool. Mold. Serve on lettuce with mayonnaise. Serves 12.

---

### Almond Pineapple Salad

*Mrs. L. D. Barnhart. · Original printed p. 145, scan p. 152 · `mg1930-p152-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 slices pineapple
- ½ lb. almonds
- Dressing: 2 eggs, beaten light
- Dressing: 2 tbsp. sugar
- Dressing: 4 tbsp. pineapple juice
- Dressing: 2 tbsp. vinegar
- Dressing: pinch salt
- Dressing: 2 tbsp. butter
- ½ cup whipped cream (added before serving)

**Preparation**

Blanch almonds in boiling water, then pour off the water, cover with cold water, drain, and rub to remove skins. Split almonds and mix with sliced pineapple. For dressing, mix the eggs, sugar, pineapple juice, vinegar, salt, and butter; cook until thick. Cool and mix with fruit. Let stand overnight. Add ½ cup whipped cream before serving. Serves 4–6.

---

### Delicious Canned Asparagus Salad

*Mrs. Katherine S. Russell. · Original printed p. 145, scan p. 152 · `mg1930-p152-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- Canned asparagus tips
- French dressing, to coat
- Lettuce leaves
- Mayonnaise, for serving

**Preparation**

Heat asparagus tips. Cover with french dressing while still hot. Stand aside to chill. Serve by draining stalks, laying on lettuce with portion of mayonnaise on plate.

---

### Ripe Banana Salad

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 145, scan p. 152 · `mg1930-p152-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- Ripe bananas
- Cream cheese
- English walnuts
- Lettuce
- Cream (to thin cream cheese)
- Red cherries (for garnish)

**Preparation**

Moisten cream cheese with cream until thin enough to pipe through a pastry tube. Make roses of cream cheese in nests of cut bananas and nuts. Put red cherry pieces in each center. Serve on lettuce. Time in combining: 20 minutes.

---

### Breakfast Salad

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 146, scan p. 153 · `mg1930-p153-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2–3 lettuce leaves per serving
- 1 tomato slice, ½ inch thick, per serving
- Scrambled eggs, about 1 egg per serving
- 3 thin slices cooked ham per serving
- French dressing

**Preparation**

On two or three leaves of head lettuce, place a center slice of tomato about half inch thick. In center of tomato place some scrambled eggs. On top of eggs place 3 very thin slices of boiled ham cut in two. Lay ham on top of eggs in a row. Add French dressing.

---

### Butterfly Salad

*Mrs. Maurice Clark. · Original printed p. 146, scan p. 153 · `mg1930-p153-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- Pineapple rings, halved
- Dates, split lengthwise
- Pimiento strips
- Stuffed olives, sliced
- Lettuce leaves
- Mayonnaise

**Preparation**

Cut slices of pineapple in half, and turn outside edges in, so as to give the appearance of the wings of a butterfly. Place as the body a date, sliced lengthwise. Use at the head 2 small, thin strips of pimento. Place on each wing slices of stuffed olive. Garnish with mayonnaise and serve on lettuce.

---

### Cabbage In Sour Cream Dressing

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 146, scan p. 153 · `mg1930-p153-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 small cabbage
- 1 tbsp. finely chopped pepper
- 1 egg
- Salt and pepper
- ½ cup vinegar
- ½ cup sour cream
- 1 tsp. sugar
- 1 tbsp. melted butter
- Paprika, to finish

**Preparation**

Chop the cabbage very finely, and cook in boiling water for fifteen minutes. Drain and sprinkle with salt and pepper. Mix the egg, cream, butter, and sugar. Bring to the boiling-point and pour over the cabbage. Sprinkle with the paprika and the pepper.

---

### Cabbage And Shrimp Salad

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 146, scan p. 153 · `mg1930-p153-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ cups canned shrimp, cleaned
- 2 cups finely shredded cabbage
- 1 cup French dressing
- Mayonnaise, for moistening
- Finely chopped green pepper, for garnish

**Preparation**

Clean shrimp and remove dark vein. Break into pieces and combine with shredded cabbage. Mix with French dressing; let stand 1 hour. Chill, moisten with mayonnaise and garnish with chopped green pepper.

---

### California Salad

*Mrs. James L. Sigler. · Original printed p. 146, scan p. 153 · `mg1930-p153-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- Small head of cabbage
- 6 or 8 radishes
- 1 large green pepper
- 2 or 3 carrots

**Preparation**

Put all through food chopper raw. Press tightly into moulds. Serve on lettuce leaf and add mayonnaise on top.

---

### Cherry Salad

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 146, scan p. 153 · `mg1930-p153-r06`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 can pitted cherries
- ½ pecan meat per cherry
- Lettuce leaves
- Mayonnaise

**Preparation**

Drain 1 can of Del Monte cherries. Remove pits and put 1/2 pecan meat in each. Arrange on lettuce and serve with Mayonnaise.

---

### Overnight White Cherry Salad

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 147, scan p. 154 · `mg1930-p154-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 can white cherries
- 1 No. 2 can pears
- 1 can sliced pineapple
- ½ lb. marshmallows
- Dressing: 3 egg yolks, beaten
- Dressing: ½ tsp. salt
- Dressing: 2 tbsp. sugar
- Dressing: juice of 2 lemons
- Dressing: ¼ cup cream
- ½ pint cream, whipped, to fold in after dressing

**Preparation**

Drain the fruit and cut into pieces, halving the cherries. Cook the dressing ingredients in a double boiler until thick. Add the cooked dressing to fruit and mix thoroughly. Fold in ½ pint whipped cream; let stand until the following day.

---

### Crab Meat Salad

*Mrs. James E. Rork. · Original printed p. 147, scan p. 154 · `mg1930-p154-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 can crab meat
- 1/4 cup cut olives
- 1 cup diced celery
- 1/4 of a green pepper or pimentos
- 1 tsp. chopped onion
- 1 cup mayonnaise salad dressing
- 1 diced apple

**Preparation**

Remove bones from crab meat and separate meat. Add remaining ingredients. Mix well and pile on nests of chilled lettuce. (Lobster may be used instead of crab meat. )

---

### Stuffed Cucumber Salad

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 147, scan p. 154 · `mg1930-p154-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- Cucumbers
- 2 boned sardines
- French dressing
- 2 hard cooked eggs
- 1 tbsp. butter
- Sprig parsley, chopped

**Preparation**

Cut a cucumber in pieces about 1½ inches long. Then cut away strips horizontally, leaving striped green and white effect. Now scoop out the centers of the cucumber slices to form cups. Marinate with French Dressing, and let stand in refrigerator for 1 hour. Chop pulp fine. Chop yolks of hard cooked eggs and add butter, the sardines and parsley. Mix well and add the cucumber pulp at the last moment. Fill the cucumber cups with the mixture piled high in the centers, and top with pimento and whites of eggs cut in long strips.

---

### Cucumber, Celery, And Almond Salad

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 147, scan p. 154 · `mg1930-p154-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cucumber
- ¼ lb. salted almonds
- 2 cups diced celery
- 1 head lettuce

**Preparation**

Chill the cucumber well. Then peel and cut crosswise into thin slices. Place the washed and chilled lettuce on eight individual plates. Arrange the cucumber and diced celery on same. Top with salted nuts and Tomato French Dressing.

---

### Mixed Fruit Salad

**Ingredients**

- ¼ cup shredded pineapple
- ½ cup chopped nutmeats
- ½ cup orange pulp
- ½ cup grapefruit pulp

- ½ cup sliced bananas

**Preparation**

Mix fruit and nuts and chill. Serve with dressing. Time in combining, 15 minutes. Twice recipe enough for six large servings.

---

### Dressing

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 148, scan p. 155 · `mg1930-p155-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 egg yolks
- 1 tbsp. olive oil
- 2 tbsp. butter
- 1/2 tsp. (scant) mustard
- 2 tbsp. vinegar
- 1 tsp. salt
- ½ cup sugar
- 1/4 tsp. paprika
- 2 tbsp. lemon juice
- 3/4 cup whipped cream

**Preparation**

Beat yolks of eggs, add butter and sugar creamed together, vinegar, lemon juice, oil, mustard, salt, and paprika. Cook in double boiler until thick, stirring constantly. Cool and add cream. Pour over salad and serve.

---

### Grapefruit Salad

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 148, scan p. 155 · `mg1930-p155-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- Grapefruit segments
- Lettuce leaves
- Celery curls
- Glacé fruit dressing

**Preparation**

Arrange sections of grapefruit on individual salad plates to make a half sphere. Around in place half leaves of lettuce to form border. Just outside grapefruit, make a wreath of shreds of curled celery (stand cut celery in iced water several hours). Pour over Glace Fruit Dressing.

---

### Glacé Fruit Dressing

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 148, scan p. 155 · `mg1930-p155-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ tsp. salt
- 1 tbsp. glacé cherries
- 1/8 tsp. paprika
- 1 tbsp. glacé plums
- 1 tbsp. vinegar or lemon juice
- 1 tbsp. glacé apricots
- 2 tbsp. olive oil

**Preparation**

Make a French dressing of the first four and add the glaced fruits. Other glaced fruits may be substituted.

---

### Lobster Salad

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 148, scan p. 155 · `mg1930-p155-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- Cucumber, diced
- Cooked asparagus tips, about equal to the cucumber
- Cooked lobster meat, about equal to the cucumber
- Salt and pepper
- French dressing
- Lettuce leaves
- Chilled tomatoes, quartered
- Lobster coral, if available
- ½ cup mayonnaise

**Preparation**

Dice a cucumber. Let stand in ice water to chill, then drain and dry on a food cloth. Measure the cucumber and add an equal amount of cold cooked asparagus tips and small firm bits of lobster. Season to taste with salt, pepper, and French dressing. Arrange in a mound on bed of lettuce leaves. Arrange the flesh from the large claws and the tail of the lobster around the mound. Garnish with quarters of chilled firm tomatoes. Press the coral of the lobster a sieve and add one-half cup of mayonnaise dressing. Serve the sauce separately. Time in combining, 15 minutes. Recipe makes 8 servings.

---

### Italian Salad

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 149, scan p. 156 · `mg1930-p156-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ pkg. elbow macaroni
- 2 stalks celery
- 1 cucumber
- 2 pimentos
- 1/4 cup onion
- 1/4 cup French dressing

**Preparation**

Cook macaroni in salted water, plunge it into cold water, and drain. Soak cucumber, onion, and celery (diced) in salt and water for a few moments. Drain. Mix all ingredients and let stand in a cool place one hour before serving. Time in combining, 20 minutes. Recipe makes eight servings.

---

### Macaroni Luncheon Salad

*Mrs. E. R. Valentine. · Original printed p. 149, scan p. 156 · `mg1930-p156-r02`*

- 3 hard-boiled eggs, chopped
- 1¼ cups chopped celery

- 1 cup mayonnaise
- Salt

**Preparation**

Mix and serve on lettuce leaf. Garnish with mayonnaise, and stuffed olives. Serves 6 to 8.

### Nature's Thumb Print Salad

### Orange And Onion Salad

### Pineapple — Grapefruit Salad

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 149, scan p. 156 · `mg1930-p156-r05`*

---

### Pineapple Salad

*Mrs. C. D. Brown · Original printed p. 149, scan p. 156 · `mg1930-p156-r06`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup pineapple juice, drained from can
- Juice of 2 lemons
- 1 cup sugar
- 1 package unflavored gelatin
- ½ lb. mild cream cheese, grated
- 1 can shredded pineapple, drained
- ½ pint heavy cream, whipped
- ½ cup boiling water
- Cold water, enough to soften gelatin
- Lettuce leaves and mayonnaise, for serving

**Preparation**

juice from can of pineapple (1 cup) juice of 2 lemons 1 cup sugar 1 pkg. Knox's Gelatine ½ lb. cheese ( New York cheese or mild cream cheese) 1 can shredded pineapple drained from juice; Served with mayonnaise Cook juice with sugar until dissolved, when cool add gelatine which has been dissolved in enough cold water to moisten and dissolved with ½ cup boiling water; add juice of 2 lemons; when it starts to set fold it into shredded pineapple and grated cheese, then ½ pt. of whipping cream last. Secret is to have everything cold when mixed. Put into mold in individual molds or in large mold and cut and serve on lettuce leaf with mayonnaise.

### Princess Salad

**Ingredients**

- Lettuce leaves
- 1 ripe tomato slice, peeled, per serving
- 5 asparagus tips per serving
- Green-pepper strips
- Pimiento strips
- French dressing

**Preparation**

On bed of lettuce place a peeled slice of ripe tomato. On top of tomato place five asparagus tips. On tips put strips of green peppers and pimentos. Add French dressing.

---

### Poinsetta Salad

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 150, scan p. 157 · `mg1930-p157-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- Lettuce leaves
- 1 ripe tomato per serving
- 1 short asparagus spear per serving
- Whipped cream or soft cheese, for rosettes
- Chopped green pepper
- Cooked egg yolk, chopped
- French dressing

**Preparation**

On a bed of lettuce place a whole ripe tomato, peeled. Slit tomato 5 times from top to stem end, but base left intact. Fold back petals and place a short spear of asparagus in center of petals. Put a little rosette of whipped cream or cheese at each opening between petals. Sprinkle center with chopped green pepper and yolks of hard boiled eggs. Add French dressing.

### Snow Salad

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 150, scan p. 157 · `mg1930-p157-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 egg yolks
- 1 can pears
- 1 cup milk
- 1 lb. marshmallows
- 2 tbsp. sugar
- 1 lemon
- 1 can white cherries
- 1 pt. cream
- 1 can crushed pineapple

**Preparation**

Make a custard of the egg yolks and milk and sweeten with sugar. Cool. Remove stones from cherries. Mix pineapple and cherries and cut pears in dice. Add marshmallows cut in pieces and lemon juice to the fruit. Stir in custard and fold in cream, which has been whipped. Let stand in a cool place for twenty-four hours. Scoop out individual servings to represent snowballs. Serve on lettuce or as a dessert without the lettuce. Time in combining, 30 minutes. Recipe makes twenty- four servings.

---

### Shrimp Salad

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 150, scan p. 157 · `mg1930-p157-r04`*

- 4–6 hard-boiled eggs, chopped

- 1 cup mayonnaise
- 1 head lettuce
- 2 tbsp. India relish
- Salt, to taste

**Preparation**

Stir relish into mayonnaise and set aside briefly. Reserve outer lettuce leaves for serving; shred inner leaves. Combine shrimp, eggs, celery and shredded lettuce with salt. Fold in dressing, then serve on reserved leaves. Serves 10–12.

---

### Tuna Fish Salad

## Molded Salads

### Molded Asparagus

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 153, scan p. 160 · `mg1930-p160-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 bunch fresh asparagus
- 2 tbsp. butter
- 1 tsp. salt
- 1 tbsp. flour
- 1 cup water
- 4 eggs
- paprika
- ½ cup whipping cream

**Preparation**

Wash and scrape asparagus and cut into inch lengths. Put into a sauce pan with water and salt and cook until tender. Add boiling water to have 1 cup water on asparagus. Blend butter and flour and stir into asparagus. Stir with a fork and cook until thick. Beat eggs well with a Dover egg beater and stir until first mixture thickens. Remove from the fire and fold in the cream. Turn into six molds rubbed with olive oil, and when cool, place on ice to chill. Unmold and serve on lettuce.

---

### Jellied Chicken Salad

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 153, scan p. 160 · `mg1930-p160-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ pkg. Lemon Jello
- 1 cup boiling chicken broth, free from fat
- 1 cup chicken, cut medium
- 1 cup celery, cut fine
- 1 pimento, cut fine
- 1 tbsp. vinegar
- ½ tsp. salt
- dash cayenne pepper
- ½ cup heavy cream, whipped

**Preparation**

Dissolve Jello in boiling broth. Chill. When cold and slightly thickened, beat with egg beater until consistency of whipped cream. Mix chicken, celery, pimento, vinegar, and pepper. Serve with mayonnaise dressing mixed with 1/2 cup whipped cream.

---

### Raw Carrot And Pineapple Salad

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups grated carrots
- 1 cup pineapple

**Preparation**

pineapple juice and water to make two cups Heat until boiling and pour over 1 package of lemon jello. When cool, add carrots and pineapple. Serve with mayonnaise. Serves 12.

---

### Molded Carrot Pineapple Salad

**Ingredients**

- 1 tbsp. Knox gelatine dissolved in 1/4 cup cold water
- 1 cup diced cooked carrots
- 1 small can shredded pineapple

Melt gelatine over hot water. Combine with carrots and pineapple and chill. Serve on lettuce with salad dressing.

---

### Fancy Salad

*Mrs. D. C. Rockwell. · Original printed p. 153, scan p. 160 · `mg1930-p160-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 pkg. lemon Jello
- 1 qt. water
- 1 pint cream
- 6 macaroons
- Maraschino cherries
- nuts
- Grapes
- any fruit desired

**Preparation**

Dissolve Jello in hot water, cool, add fruit, nuts, maccaroons. Color with cherry juice and add cream whipped. Mold and chill.

---

### Fruit And Ginger Ale Salad

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 154, scan p. 161 · `mg1930-p161-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ tbsp. gelatine
- 2 tbsp. cold water
- ⅓ cup boiling water
- 1 cup ginger ale
- ¼ cup lemon juice
- 2 tbsp. sugar
- Pinch salt
- ⅓ cup Malaga grapes, skinned, seeded and halved
- ⅓ cup apple, cored, pared and diced
- 2 tbsp. Canton ginger, cut small
- 4 tbsp. canned shredded pineapple

**Preparation**

Soak gelatine in cold water and dissolve in boiling water. Add ginger ale, lemon juice, sugar and salt. When it begins to set, fold in the fruits. Mold, chill and turn out onto a serving dish. Garnish with parsley and celery tips; serve with mayonnaise.

---

### Ginger Ale Salad

*Mrs. R. A. Winslow. · Original printed p. 154, scan p. 161 · `mg1930-p161-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 package lemon-flavored gelatin
- Hot water, enough to dissolve gelatin
- 1 bottle ginger ale (about 12 oz.)
- Pineapple, diced
- Grapes
- Grapefruit pieces
- Bananas, sliced

**Preparation**

To 1 pkg. lemon jello dissolved in small amount of hot water, add 1 bottle ginger ale, and to this add pineapple, grapes, grapefruit, and bananas cut in small pieces.

---

### Frozen Fruit Salad

*Mrs. James Rork. · Original printed p. 154, scan p. 161 · `mg1930-p161-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 can fruit for salad
- 1 cup diced dates
- 1 cup diced marshmallows
- 2 diced bananas
- 1 bottle whipped cream
- 1 tbsp. salad dressing
- 2 tbsp. powdered sugar

**Preparation**

Mix, put in trays of icebox and freeze.

---

### Frozen Pineapple And Cheese Salad

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 154, scan p. 161 · `mg1930-p161-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- ¼ cup cream cheese
- Fruit salad dressing (4 tbsp. for mixing and more for serving)
- 1 cup crushed pineapple
- 12 marshmallows
- ½ cup cream
- Red Maraschino cherries, chopped
- Green Maraschino cherries, chopped

**Preparation**

Mix cheese and 4 tbsp. salad dressing until smooth, add pineapple, cherries, marshmallows cut in pieces with scissors dipped in hot water. Beat cream until stiff and fold into mixture. Pack in individual molds. Cover with a double thickness of wax paper held in place with elastic bands. Pack in equal parts of ice and salt, and let stand 3 hours or freeze in mechanical refrigerator. Unmold and put a green maraschino cherry on top. Serve on lettuce, with fruit salad dressing, or with sparkling French dressing.

---

### Sparkling French Dressing

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 154, scan p. 161 · `mg1930-p161-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ pkg. sweetened and flavored red gelatine
- ½ cup boiling water
- ⅓ cup salad oil
- 2 tbsp. lemon juice
- ⅓ tsp. salt
- ¼ tsp. paprika

**Preparation**

Dissolve gelatine in boiling water. Chill and when nearly set add remaining ingredients. Let stand until thoroughly congealed and beat just before serving.

---

### Fruit Salad Dressing

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 155, scan p. 162 · `mg1930-p162-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 egg
- 1/4 tsp. celery salt
- 1½ tbsp. butter
- 1/4 tsp. vanilla
- 1½ tbsp. lemon juice
- ⅛ tsp. paprika
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- 2 drops onion juice
- 2 tbsp. powdered sugar
- ½ cup cream

**Preparation**

Put egg in top of double boiler and beat until light. Add butter, lemon juice, and salt. Cook and stir over hot water until mixture thickens. Remove from fire and add remaining ingredients except cream. Just before serving fold in cream, which has been beaten stiff.

---

### Party Salad

*Mrs. E. R. Valentine. · Original printed p. 155, scan p. 162 · `mg1930-p162-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 tbsp. gelatine
- 1/4 cup sugar
- 1/4 cup cold water
- 1 tbsp. salt
- 1 box lemon Jello
- 1 cup chopped celery
- 1½ pt. hot water
- 1 cucumber, chopped
- 1/4 cup vinegar
- 1½ pimento, chopped
- 2 tbsp. lemon juice

**Preparation**

Soak gelatine in 1/4 cup cold water, add 1 cup hot water. When dissolved, add lemon Jello dissolved in 1 pt. hot water, vinegar, lemon juice, sugar and salt. Cool. When beginning to thicken add celery, cucumber and pimento. Mold in fancy mold or in bread tin. When ready to serve, unmold, or cut in slices, and arrange on lettuce leaf, with mayonnaise. Serves 12.

---

### Salmon Salad

*Mrs. James Rork. · Original printed p. 155, scan p. 162 · `mg1930-p162-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 envelope Knox gelatine
- 4 tbsp. cold water
- juice, 1 lemon
- 1½ cups milk
- 2 tbsp. butter
- 1 tsp. salt
- 1 tsp. dry mustard
- 1 tsp. paprika
- 2 eggs
- 4 hard boiled eggs
- 1 can salmon

**Preparation**

Soak gelatine in cold water. Heat milk and add salt, mustard, paprika, butter and lemon and bring to boiling point and pour onto beaten eggs. Cook in double boiler until it thickens, add gelatine. Let cool and mix with salmon and boiled eggs and arrange in mold. Chill. Serve with lettuce and salad dressing.

---

### Cucumber Sauce

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 155, scan p. 162 · `mg1930-p162-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cucumber, minced
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- ½ cup sour cream, beaten stiff
- 1 tbsp. vinegar

**Preparation**

Drain cucumber through cheese cloth. Add salt and a few grains pepper to whipped cream. Slowly beat in vinegar. Chill about 3/4 hour. When ready to serve add cucumber.

---

### Jellied Salmon

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 156, scan p. 163 · `mg1930-p163-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ cups flaked salmon
- 1 tsp. salt
- 1 tbsp. granulated gelatine
- ½ tsp. mustard
- 2 tbsp. cold water
- ½ tsp. paprika
- 1 egg
- 2 tbsp. melted butter
- 1½ tbsp. flour
- 4 tbsp. lemon juice
- 1 tbsp. sugar
- 3/4 cup milk

**Preparation**

If canned salmon is used, remove from can, and pour boiling water over salmon. Drain and remove skin and bones. Flake.. Mix and sift flour, mustard, sugar, salt, and paprika, and add to melted butter. Add egg slightly beaten with milk. Cook over hot water, stirring constantly until mixture is perfectly smooth and thickened. Soften gelatine in cold water for five minutes. Remove sauce from heat and stir in gelatine. Stir until gelatine is dissolved, and fold in fish flakes. Pour into individual molds and let stand several hours in cool place to set. Serve on lettuce, with cucumber sauce.

---

### Tomato Aspic

*Mrs. P. V. Sangren. · Original printed p. 156, scan p. 163 · `mg1930-p163-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 tbsp. gelatine
- 1 tsp. salt
- ½ cup cold water
- 1 tsp. powdered sugar
- 2 cups strained tomatoes
- dash cayenne pepper

**Preparation**

Soak gelatine in cold water 15 minutes. Season tomatoes with salt, sugar and pepper and bring to boiling point. Add the gelatine and stir until it is dissolved. Mold and chill. Place on lettuce leaf and serve with mayonnaise.

---

### Tomato Cheese Salad

*Mrs. John Speer. · Original printed p. 156, scan p. 163 · `mg1930-p163-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 can tomato soup
- 2 pkg. cream cheese
- 1 tbsp. Knox gelatine
- 2 tbsp. water
- ½ cup chopped olives
- ½ cup chopped celery

**Preparation**

Dissolve gelatine in cold water. Heat soup to boiling point. Cream cheese to a paste and add to the soup, add gelatine. When cool add olives and celery.

---

### Molded Tomato Salad

*Mrs. Paul Snauble. · Original printed p. 156, scan p. 163 · `mg1930-p163-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 can tomato soup
- 1 cup mayonnaise
- 3 pkg. Phil. Cream Cheese
- 1 cup celery, chopped
- 2 tbsp. gelatine
- onion, chopped

**Preparation**

Heat soup in double boiler. Add soaked gelatine. Add cheese and blend perfectly. When cool add remaining ingredients, and mold.

---

### Molded Vegetable Salad

*Mrs. D. C. Rockwell. · Original printed p. 157, scan p. 164 · `mg1930-p164-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 tbsp. Knox gelatine
- ½ cup cold water
- ½ cup liquid from canned peas
- 1/4 tsp. paprika
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- 1 cup cooked carrots, diced
- 1 cup peas
- ¼ cup vinegar
- 1½ tbsp. mint sauce

**Preparation**

Soak gelatine in cold water, dissolve in hot liquid from peas. Cook peas gently five minutes. Add salt, paprika, peas, carrots, mint sauce and vinegar to gelatine mixture. Mould and chill. Serve on lettuce with salad dressing. Garnish with stuffed olives or pimento.

---

### Vegetable Jello Salad

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 157, scan p. 164 · `mg1930-p164-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 tbsp. Knox gelatine
- 2 tbsp. lemon juice
- 1/4 cup cold water
- 1 tbsp. salt
- 1 cup boiling water
- 1 cup celery, cut fine
- 1 pkg. lemon jello
- 1 small or ⅓ large cucumber
- 1 pt. boiling water
- 1½ pimento (canned)
- 1/4 cup sugar
- 2 tbsp. onion, rounded
- 1/4 cup vinegar

**Preparation**

Soak gelatine in cold water, dissolve in cup of boiling water. Add jello which has been dissolved in pt. of boiling water. Add sugar, vinegar, lemon juice, and salt. Strain and cool, and when it thickens, add celery, cucumber, pimento and onion. Chill. Serves 18.

DRESSING FOR VEGETABLE SALAD WITH LEMON JELLO 1½ cup sugar 1 cup milk 2 tbsp. flour 4 egg yolks 1 tbsp. cornstarch 1 cup vinegar 2 tsp. mustard, dry 2 or 3 tbsp. oil 1/4 tsp. paprika whipped cream 1 tsp. salt

Put first list of ingredients in double boiler. Moisten with part of milk. Add rest of milk, mixed with egg yolks. Cook until it starts to thicken. Add vinegar, and cook until thick. Add oil as you take it off the stove. More sugar or less vinegar may be used according to taste. Add whipped cream: 1/2 pt. to 1½ cup dressing.

## Sandwiches

### Honey Cinnamon Toast

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 159, scan p. 166 · `mg1930-p166-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup honey
- ½ tsp. cinnamon
- 1/4 cup butter
- Bread

**Preparation**

Warm honey, add to creamed butter and then mix the cinnamon in thoroughly. Spread thinly sliced bread mixture. Put slices together and toast quickly. Time in combining, 10 minutes. Time in cooking, about 5 minutes. Temperature, 400 to 500 degrees.

---

### Egg Creole Sandwiches

*Ida Bailey Allen. · Original printed p. 159, scan p. 166 · `mg1930-p166-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ hard-boiled egg, chopped, per sandwich
- 1 sardine, shredded, per sandwich
- ½ tbsp. chow-chow relish per sandwich
- Mayonnaise, to bind
- Butter and sandwich bread

**Preparation**

For each sandwich allow one-half chopped bard-boiled egg, one shredded sardine, one-half tablespoon chow chow and Mazola Mayonnaise to blend. Spread on buttered bread, top with a second slice of bread, press together, and cut in triangles and serve.

---

### Cheese Dreams

*Mrs. Carl R. Johnson. · Original printed p. 159, scan p. 166 · `mg1930-p166-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- Sandwich bread
- Thinly sliced cheese
- Butter, for frying

**Preparation**

Make sandwiches with thin sliced cheese. Fry in sufficient melted butter until bread is well browned.

---

### Sandwiches

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 159, scan p. 166 · `mg1930-p166-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- Equal parts grated carrots and chopped nuts
- Mayonnaise, to bind
- Pitted dates and nuts (alternative filling)
- Orange juice, to moisten date filling
- Sandwich bread

**Preparation**

Carrots and nuts, equal parts, with mayonnaise. (Grind carrots first, then nuts. ) Dates and nuts with orange juice. Cut sandwiches round.

---

### Rolled Asparagus Sandwiches

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 159, scan p. 166 · `mg1930-p166-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- Very fresh sliced sandwich bread
- Soft butter, for spreading
- 1–2 cooked asparagus spears per sandwich
- Mayonnaise, for spreading

**Preparation**

Remove crusts from fresh sandwich bread and spread with soft butter. Put 1–2 asparagus spears cut to bread length on each slice; spread with a little mayonnaise. Roll tightly, wrap and chill until the butter firms enough to hold the rolls closed.

---

### Fruit And Cheese Sandwiches

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 159, scan p. 166 · `mg1930-p166-r06`*

**Ingredients**

- 12 dates, cut into small pieces
- 6 figs
- 1 tbsp. lemon juice
- 1 banana, mashed to pulp
- ½ pkg. cream cheese

**Preparation**

Mix all together and spread between slices of buttered bread.

---

### French Toast Chicken Sandwiches

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 160, scan p. 167 · `mg1930-p167-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 10 slices bread
- 1 cup minced chicken
- 2 egg yolks
- seasoning
- 1 cup milk
- 1 tsp. onion juice

**Preparation**

Mix the chicken, onion juice, salt, pepper, and paprika to taste. Spread between large slices of bread from which crusts have been removed. Mix the milk and egg well and dip the sandwiches one at a time into this mixture. Fry in a hot frying pan until brown on both sides.

---

### Rolled Sandwiches With Cheese

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 160, scan p. 167 · `mg1930-p167-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- Very fresh sandwich bread, cut lengthwise into thin slices
- Soft butter
- Pimiento cheese spread
- Plain cream cheese
- French dressing, for softening cheese
- Watercress, chopped
- Spinach juice or green coloring, optional

**Preparation**

Remove all crusts from a fresh loaf of bread, and cut lengthwise in very thin slices. Spread with butter, then spread one end of each slice with pimento cheese, the middle portion of the slices with plain cream cheese, moistened if necessary with a little French dressing, and the remaining third of the slice with cream cheese mixed with chopped water cress and a bit of green color paste or spinach juice. Roll from end to end like a jelly roll. Wrap in cheese cloth and put in refrigerator until needed, then cut in thin slices diagonally.

---

### Hot Ham Sandwiches

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 160, scan p. 167 · `mg1930-p167-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 12 slices sandwich bread
- Butter for bread and pan
- ¾ cup deviled ham
- 2 eggs
- 1 cup milk

**Preparation**

Lightly butter bread slices, spread half with deviled ham and top with remaining slices. Cut sandwiches in halves. Beat eggs with milk, dip sandwiches in mixture and sauté in a buttered skillet until golden on each side, about 10 minutes. Makes 6 sandwiches.

---

### Lobster Salad Sandwiches

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 160, scan p. 167 · `mg1930-p167-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- Bread
- Mayonnaise
- Butter
- Celery, diced fine
- Lobster meat
- Lettuce

**Preparation**

Seasoning

Mix equal parts of lobster and diced celery, and moisten with mayonnaise. Place lettuce leaf and salad between slices of buttered bread. Chicken and crabmeat may be prepared in the same way.

---

### Sweet Sandwiches

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 160, scan p. 167 · `mg1930-p167-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- Pitted dates, chopped
- Chopped nuts
- Sandwich bread
- Orange juice, as needed to moisten filling

**Preparation**

Make a filling of dates and nut meats run through the food chopper.

---

### Orange Sandwiches

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 161, scan p. 168 · `mg1930-p168-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup powdered sugar
- 1 tbsp. orange juice

Moisten sugar with orange juice, add finely cut crystallized orange peel. Spread thinly cut slices of bread with creamed butter and then with the mixture. Combine slices to make sandwiches.

### Dinty Moore Sandwich

- Butter two slices of bread. Place a slice of corned beef between,

and press together well. Toast outsides of sandwich on griddle, turning with a pancake turner. Use weight, such as a block of wood, to keep sandwich in shape while toasting. -Harry Cox.

## Meats

### Spanish Steak

*Mrs. Wilbur Payne. · Original printed p. 163, scan p. 170 · `mg1930-p170-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- Steak, cut 1½ inches thick
- ½ cup water
- Onions, thinly sliced
- Salt and pepper
- 3 cups strained tomatoes
- Grated cheese, for topping

**Preparation**

Steak cut 1½ inches thick, sear on both sides in hot frying pan. Put it in a casserole with 1/2 cup water and cover with thinly sliced onions. Cover the casserole and bake 30 minutes at 400 degrees, or less. Season with salt, pepper and pour 3 cups strained tomatoes over the meat. Bake about 40 minutes longer, uncovered. Place meat on platter, cover with grated cheese, and return to oven just long enough to melt cheese. Serve any gravy left in casserole right on platter with meat.

---

### Dutch Pot Roast

*Mrs. Wilbur Payne. · Original printed p. 163, scan p. 170 · `mg1930-p170-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 lb. rump, shank or chuck, cut for stew
- 2 tbsp. bacon fat or drippings
- 2 tsp. salt
- 2 cups water
- 2 cups stewed tomatoes
- 1 Spanish onion, sliced
- 1 cup chopped carrots
- ¾ cup chopped celery
- Flour, for dredging and thickening

**Preparation**

Dredge beef with flour and brown well in bacon fat or drippings. Place in a kettle with remaining ingredients, cover and cook on the stove about 3½ hours. Thicken remaining gravy with a flour-and-cold-water paste. Simmer a few minutes before serving.

---

### Tomalee Pie

*Mrs. R. A. Winslow. · Original printed p. 163, scan p. 170 · `mg1930-p170-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 onion
- seasoning
- 2 tbsp. fat
- ¾ cup cornmeal
- 1 lb. chopped beef
- 3 cups water
- 1 cup tomato juice

**Preparation**

Brown onion in fat. Add beef and brown. Add tomato juice and season. Cook cornmeal in water to a mush. Line baking dish with mush on the bottom and sides and in center put the meat and tomato mixture and put mush over top. Bake at about 325 degrees for 1½ hour.

---

### Steak Gravy

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 163, scan p. 170 · `mg1930-p170-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- Pan drippings from broiled steak
- 2 heaping tbsp. flour
- Up to 1 qt. milk
- Salt and pepper, to taste

**Preparation**

After broiling steak in spider, turning all the time, make a gravy of the juice and grease. Turn out gas, add about 2 large tbsp. dry flour to the grease. Turn on gas slowly, let flour cook, stirring constantly. Then add a quart of milk, or enough for proper consistency. Makes a lovely brown gravy.

---

### Yorkshire Pudding

*Mrs. James Rork. · Original printed p. 164, scan p. 171 · `mg1930-p171-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 eggs
- 2 cups flour
- 1 pint milk
- 1 tsp. salt

**Preparation**

Beat eggs. Add some flour, a little milk and salt until all is used. Beat 5 minutes. Pour a thin layer in a greased pan, bake 20 minutes in a moderate oven until brown. Cut in squares and put around roast beef, put dish gravy on it.

---

### Pot Roast

*Mrs. E. J. Anderson. · Original printed p. 164, scan p. 171 · `mg1930-p171-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 lb. cut of brisket

**Preparation**

Have beef fat 1/2 inch deep melted in Dutch Oven or baking dish on top of stove. Cover meat with salt and lots of paprika. Brown well in hot fat, then take the meat out. Add 2 cups of onion chopped real fine with salt and more paprika. Simmer onions in fat until done. Add 2 cans tomato pulp (seasoned) and 2 cans water. When this boils put the meat back in to cook about 3 hours. Serves 8.

---

### Mock Duck

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 164, scan p. 171 · `mg1930-p171-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- ⅔ lb. top of round
- Bread dressing

**Preparation**

Have meat cut in a thin slice. Spread with dressing, roll up and tie securely. Dredge with flour and sprinkle with salt and pepper. Put in roasting-pan and sear in a hot oven. Reduce heat, add a little water and cook until meat is tender (2 to 3 hours), basting frequently and adding more water from time to time if necessary.

---

### Savory Beef Loaf

*Mrs. L. D. Barnhart. · Original printed p. 164, scan p. 171 · `mg1930-p171-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- ¼ lb. salt pork, diced fine
- 2 lb. round steak, ground
- ½ cup Minute Tapioca, uncooked
- 2 cups canned tomatoes
- 2½ tsp. salt
- ¼ tsp. pepper
- Worcestershire sauce, if desired

**Preparation**

Fry pork until brown. Add it to the other ingredients and mix. Bake quickly for 15 minutes, 400 degrees, then lower heat a little for 30 minutes. Serves 8.

---

### Beefsteak With Mushroom Sauce

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 164, scan p. 171 · `mg1930-p171-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 tbsp. butter 1 tbsp. lemon juice
- 2 tbsp. flour
- salt and pepper
- 1 cup cold water 1 can mushrooms

**Preparation**

Beefsteak

Cream butter and the flour together in a frying-pan and let brown well, being careful not to burn. When well browned add cold water and lemon juice, parsley, salt, pepper, mushrooms, which have been drained and cut in quarters and browned in small amount of butter. Broil steak in the usual way, place on hot platter, and pour mushroom sauce over. Serve immediately. Time in combining, 40 minutes. One pound rump steak serves five generously.

---

### Meat Patties

*Mrs. C. L. Mohney. · Original printed p. 165, scan p. 172 · `mg1930-p172-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lb. ground round steak
- Fat, for frying
- 1 can condensed tomato soup
- Salt and pepper

**Preparation**

Take one pound round steak ground and shape in patties. Fry on both sides in fat. Add one can of tomato soup and cook 3/4 hour. Salt and pepper to taste. Very good.

---

### Meat Balls

*Mrs. V. Paul Van Keuren. · Original printed p. 165, scan p. 172 · `mg1930-p172-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ lbs. round steak, ground
- 1 lb. salt or fresh pork, ground
- 2 eggs, beaten
- 1 stalk celery
- 1 tbsp. flour
- Butter and seasoning
- 2 cans tomato soup

**Preparation**

Mix all together and make into balls (about 16). Put soup and 1 can of water in kettle and bring to boil. Put in meat balls and cook slowly about two hours. Take out balls and thicken liquid, which makes a lovely tomato sauce.

---

### Baked Swift's Premium Ham

*Mrs. Paul Snauble. · Original printed p. 165, scan p. 172 · `mg1930-p172-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- Swift’s Premium ham (10–12 lb.)
- 1 bay leaf
- 3 tbsp. sugar
- ½ tsp. cinnamon
- 1 tsp. onion juice
- Grated rind of 1 lemon
- 1 cup brown sugar, for rubbing fat
- ½ tsp. cinnamon, for rubbing fat
- ½ tsp. allspice, for rubbing fat
- Whole cloves
- 2 cups sugar, for basting syrup
- ¼ cup vinegar, for basting syrup
- ¼ cup water, for basting syrup
- 1 tsp. mustard, for basting syrup

**Preparation**

Plunge the ham into boiling water to cover, with bay leaf, 3 tablespoons sugar, ½ teaspoon cinnamon, onion juice, and grated lemon rind. Reduce heat and simmer until tender, allowing about 25 minutes per pound for a 10–12-pound ham.

Remove from water, peel rind and trim. Rub fat with 1 cup brown sugar, ½ teaspoon cinnamon and ½ teaspoon allspice. Pierce fat with whole cloves.

Bake in a 350°F oven 45 minutes to 1 hour, or until nicely browned. Baste frequently with syrup made by boiling 2 cups sugar, ¼ cup vinegar, ¼ cup water, and 1 teaspoon mustard.

---

### Luncheon Dish

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 165, scan p. 172 · `mg1930-p172-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup ham, ground, about ½ lb.
- 2 cups milk
- 1 cup American cheese, cut fine
- 2 cups bread crumbs

**Preparation**

Use center of fresh bread for crumbs. Mix with milk, and let stand 2 hours. Add cheese and let stand 1 hour. Add ham and bake 3/4 hour in buttered casserole at 350 degrees.

---

### Baked Slice Of Ham

*Mrs. J. W. Spalsbury. · Original printed p. 165, scan p. 172 · `mg1930-p172-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 thick slice ham
- Milk, sweet cider, or diluted peach-pickle juice, enough to cover

**Preparation**

A slice of ham may be baked until tender, covered with milk, sweet cider, or thinned peach pickle juice.

---

### Escalloped Ham And Potatoes

*Mrs. D. C. Rockwell. · Original printed p. 166, scan p. 173 · `mg1930-p173-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lb. sliced ham
- 1 qt. sliced potatoes
- 2 small onions
- 1 pt. milk
- 1 tbsp. fat
- 2 tbsp. flour
- ¼ tsp. pepper

**Preparation**

Cut ham into six portions. Melt fat, add flour and milk, cook until thick, stirring constantly. Put layer of potatoes in buttered casserole using one-third of them. Over these, slice onion and sprinkle with pepper. Put on these three pieces of ham, repeat these layers. Pour the milk sauce over all and bake 1½ hours in a moderate oven, partially covered.

---

### Baked Slice Of Premium Ham

- 1 cup vinegar
- 2 cups water

**Preparation**

Slice of Swift's Premium Ham, 1 inch thick. Dot with Brookfield Butter. Cover with liquid made from: 4 tsp. dry mustard 1 cup light brown sugar 1 cup vinegar 2 cups water

Bake one hour, or longer, at 350 degrees.

### Ham Loaf

### Ham Loaf

Mix, and bake 2 hours at 350 degrees. Makes a large loaf, serving about 10.

### Ham Loaf

*Mrs. E. Ray Winters. · Original printed p. 167, scan p. 174 · `mg1930-p174-r01`*

Mix thoroughly and bake in moderate oven, 350 degrees about 1 hour.

### Swift's Premium Ham Loaf

*Mrs. L. D. Barnhart. · Original printed p. 167, scan p. 174 · `mg1930-p174-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lb. lean fresh pork, ground
- 1 tbsp. Worcestershire Sauce
- 1 lb. lean Swift's Premium Ham,
- ¼ tsp. paprika
- ground
- 1/4 tsp. pepper
- 2 cups milk
- grated onion to taste
- ½ cup Minute Tapioca

**Preparation**

Mix and bake 1 hour. Easy to slice cold. Serves about 10.

---

### Swift's Premium Ham Supreme

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 167, scan p. 174 · `mg1930-p174-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 Swift’s Premium ham, 10–12 lb.
- Brown sugar, to rub onto ham
- Whole cloves
- Currant jelly
- Hot water, equal in volume to jelly
- Flour and water, for gravy
- Cinnamon apple rings, optional garnish

**Preparation**

Currant Jelly Place a 10 to 12 pound Swift's Premium Ham, fat side up, in an uncovered baking pan, in a 300 ° F. oven, allowing about 20 minutes per pound. Take from oven, remove rind, trim, and rub with brown sugar. Dot with cloves. Pour off the extra fat which is in the pan, and replace ham in oven. Baste frequently with currant jelly which has been mixed with an equal amount of hot water. When nicely browned, remove from oven. The drippings in the pan make an excellent gravy. Allow 2 tablespoons flour to each 3 tablespoons fat, blend, and add 1 cup water for each 2 tablespoons flour used. Garnish with cinnamon apple rings with currant jelly on the center of each ring.

---

### Arabian Stew

*Mrs. E. J. Anderson. · Original printed p. 167, scan p. 174 · `mg1930-p174-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- One pork chop for each serving cut about 1 inch thick. Brown

**Preparation**

in hot fat on each side. Place in a tight covered baking dish. On top of each pork chop put 1 tbsp. of washed uncooked rice; 1 large slice of onion, one slice of tomato cooked tomatoes may be used; and a slice of green pepper on top of all. Season well; add 4 cups water, let bake slowly for 2 hours.

---

### Pork Chops Italiane

*Mrs. E. D. Ganyard. · Original printed p. 167, scan p. 174 · `mg1930-p174-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 pork chops
- 6 tbsp. raw rice
- 6 slices tomato
- 6 slices onion
- 6 slices green pepper
- salt

**Preparation**

Brown chops, put in baking dish and lay a slice of tomato, onion and pepper and 1 tbsp. rice on each one. Add some water, cover and bake 1 hour.

---

### New England Style Sausage

*Mrs. D. C. Rockwell. · Original printed p. 168, scan p. 175 · `mg1930-p175-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lb. link sausage
- 6 medium sized sweet potatoes
- 6 tart apples
- ½ cup brown sugar
- 1/4 cup butter
- 1/3 cup hot water

**Preparation**

Boil sweet potatoes until tender. Peel and cut potatoes into ½ inch slices lengthwise. Place a layer of them on bottom of buttered baking dish. Cover with slices of apples which have been peeled and cored. Sprinkle with sugar and dot with butter. Fry sausage until golden brown. Cut in pieces. Lay upon the apple. Repeat with another layer of potato, apple and sausage and continue until dish is filled. Pour water and 2 tbsp. sausage fat over all. Cover dish and bake slowly 1½ hours or until apples are done. It may be necessary to add more water to prevent burning. Serve six.

---

### Pork Chops With Tomato Sauce

*Mrs. D. C. Rockwell. · Original printed p. 168, scan p. 175 · `mg1930-p175-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 pork chops
- 1 onion
- 1 cup tomato sauce
- 1 cup water

**Preparation**

Place Pork Chops in baking dish. Lay slice of onion on each chop. Cover with tomato sauce and water. Bake in slow oven 1½ hours.

---

### Smothered Pork Chops

*Mrs. John Speer. · Original printed p. 168, scan p. 175 · `mg1930-p175-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- Pork chops
- Water or stock, for gravy
- Salt
- Flour, if needed to thicken gravy
- Fat, for browning

**Preparation**

Brown chops lightly, and put in baking dish. Put water in frying pan and make a gravy, pour over chops and bake about 2 hours. Salt.

---

### Creamed Pork Tenderloin

*Esther Winters. · Original printed p. 168, scan p. 175 · `mg1930-p175-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- Pork tenderloin, cubed
- Butter or fat, for frying
- Medium white sauce
- Pimientos, chopped
- Toast, for serving

**Preparation**

Cut tenderloin in small pieces and fry. Make medium white sauce and add tenderloin and few pimentoes. Serve on toast.

---

### Pork Chops Baked

*Mrs. L. L. Taylor. · Original printed p. 168, scan p. 175 · `mg1930-p175-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 8 pork chops
- 1 can peas
- 1 can tomato soup

**Preparation**

Place pork chops in roaster. Cover with tomato soup, 1 can water, and peas. Bake in moderate oven, 325 degrees, about 1 hour.

---

### Baked Breaded Pork Chops

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 168, scan p. 175 · `mg1930-p175-r06`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 pork chops crumbs
- 1 egg milk
- salt pepper

**Preparation**

Chops should be cut evenly but not very thick. Beat egg, adding 1 tbsp. milk. Dip chop in egg, then crumbs. Heap crumbs on chop and press down well. Turn chop over and repeat. (This may be done several hours before time of cooking.) Sear breaded chops in hot fat (about 1/4 inch deep) until nicely brown. Remove to baking dish and add enough milk or milk and water to just cover chops. Bake 50 or 60 minutes at 375°. Serve hot.

---

### Stuffed Pork Tenderloin

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 169, scan p. 176 · `mg1930-p176-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 pork tenderloins
- 1 cup chopped apples
- 3 cups bread crumbs
- ½ tsp. cinnamon
- ½ cup water
- 2 tbsp. sugar
- 1 cup cooked prunes
- 4 tbsp. butter or drippings
- 1 tsp. salt

**Preparation**

Select tenderloins of equal length and weight. Split lengthwise and pound out as flat as possible. Fill and round out with well mixed stuffing. Place another tenderloin on top and fasten both ends and sides together. Dust slightly with flour, salt and pepper. Sear in hot oven 450 degrees 10 minutes reduce to moderate heat, 350 degrees, and bake 1½ hours. Baste during last half with 1 pt. water. When baked remove from pan and strain liquid. Stir in 2 tbsp. flour in remaining fat. Add liquid slowly, and cook gently until smooth and clear. Serve with meat.

---

### Stuffed Pork Tenderloin

*Mrs. K. S. Russell. · Original printed p. 169, scan p. 176 · `mg1930-p176-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- Pork tenderloin, split lengthwise
- Prepared bread dressing
- Flour, for dredging
- Melted butter, for brushing

**Preparation**

Split tenderloin lengthwise. Fill with dressing. Tie and dredge with flour. Brush with butter and roast until tender in a closed roaster. Open roaster the last half hour and brown.

---

### Mock Chicken Legs

*Mrs. L. D. Barnhart. · Original printed p. 169, scan p. 176 · `mg1930-p176-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lb. veal, cut 1 inch thick
- salt and pepper
- 1 lb. pork, cut 1 inch thick
- flour
- skewers
- butter

**Preparation**

Cut cubes of meat and string alternately on the skewers, about 5 to each stick. Roll each "leg" in flour, and saute in butter until brown. Season well, and place in waterless cooker or in roaster with a little water. Cook slowly 1 hour at 325 degrees. May be varied by cooking onions, potatoes and carrots with it.

---

### City Chicken

*Mrs. J. W. Spalsbury. · Original printed p. 169, scan p. 176 · `mg1930-p176-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lb. veal steak
- 1 lb. pork steak
- 1 egg
- cracker crumbs

**Preparation**

Cut meat in small squares and arrange alternately on wooden skewers. Dip in egg and crumbs, arrange in baking dish, cover with water and a little butter and bake until done.

---

### Veal Croquettes

*Mrs. C. V. Pierce. · Original printed p. 169, scan p. 176 · `mg1930-p176-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 cups ground veal, cooked and cold
- ½ cup cracker crumbs
- 3 cups white sauce
- lemon juice

**Preparation**

Mix all ingredients while white sauce is warm. When cool, form into long, round croquettes. Roll in egg and cracker crumbs, and fry in deep fat. These are fine with Tartar Sauce.

---

### Tartar Sauce

*Mrs. C. V. Pierce. · Original printed p. 170, scan p. 177 · `mg1930-p177-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 chopped hard boiled eggs
- dash cayenne pepper
- 3 chopped small sweet pickles
- ½ tsp. mustard, dry
- 2 teaspoons minced onion
- 2 tbsp. vinegar
- ½ tsp. salt
- ½ cup olive oil (or Mazola oil )

**Preparation**

Chop hard-boiled eggs and sweet pickles. Whisk vinegar, dry mustard, salt and cayenne together, then gradually whisk in olive oil. Stir in the eggs, pickles and minced onion; refrigerate until serving. For a smoother sauce, mash the eggs into the dressing.

---

### Veal Birds ( Beef Birds )

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 170, scan p. 177 · `mg1930-p177-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- Veal cutlets or thin beef steak, ½ inch thick
- ½ loaf bread, moistened, for dressing
- 1 small onion
- Salt and pepper
- 1 tsp. dried sage
- 2 tbsp. melted butter
- Fat for browning
- Stock or water for gravy
- Flour, for thickening gravy

**Preparation**

Have veal (or sirloin or round steak) cut 1/2 inch thick. Cut steak in squares and roll around dressing, fastening with toothpicks. Dressing: ½ loaf of bread, moistened, seasoned with small onion, salt, pepper 1 tsp. sage 2 tbsp. butter, melted. Taste for seasoning. Fry birds in lots of lard or butter until brown all over. Remove birds to roaster, make gravy in spider, by adding water and thickening. Pour over meat, and cover. Cook about 1 hour at 350 degrees.

---

### Pressed Veal

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 170, scan p. 177 · `mg1930-p177-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 5 lbs. veal cooked in salted water with celery and onion until

**Preparation**

tender. Let cool and put through grinder. Dissolve 1 tbsp. Knox gelatine in 1/4 cup cold water. Pour 2 cups boiling stock over gelatine and add meat. Put salt and pepper to taste. Place ½ meat in bread tin. Place hard boiled eggs on meat and fill dish with remaining meat. Press down, cover and chill.,

---

### Veal Loaf

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 170, scan p. 177 · `mg1930-p177-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 lbs. ground veal
- 3 lb. ground pork
- 1 egg
- 4 rolled crackers
- 4 tbsp. cream
- ½ tbsp. lemon juice
- 1 tbsp. salt
- 1 tsp. pepper

**Preparation**

shake of nutmeg Mix pack in pan. Brush top with white of egg and butter. Bake 2½ hours.

---

### Veal Stew And Dumpling

*Mrs. P. V. Sangren. · Original printed p. 170, scan p. 177 · `mg1930-p177-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ lbs. veal for stewing
- 1/4 tsp. pepper
- 4 tbsp. flour
- 1 bay leaf
- 4 cups water
- 1/4 cup chopped onion
- 2 tsp. salt
- 4 cups cubed carrots
- 2 cups green peas

**Preparation**

Dredge veal with 2 tbsp. flour, and brown in hot fat. Add water, salt, pepper, bay leaf, and onion, and stew until tender. Add carrots last 20 minutes of cooking. When tender, remove the bones from the stew, thicken gravy with 2 tbsp. flour, mixed with a little cold water, add peas. Place dumplings on top of meat. Cover tightly, and cook until dumplings are done. Time 1½ hours.

---

### Dumplings

*Mrs. P. V. Sangren. · Original printed p. 171, scan p. 178 · `mg1930-p178-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups flour
- 1 tbsp. shortening
- 4 tsp. baking powder
- 3/4 cup milk
- ½ tsp. salt

**Preparation**

Sift flour before measuring. Mix and sift flour, baking powder, and salt. Cut in shortening. Add milk gradually to make a soft dough. Roll and cut with biscuit cutter, or drop by spoonfuls on top of stew. Dumplings should rest on meat and vegetables, not settle into liquid. Cover kettle closely, and cook without lifting cover, for 15 minutes.

---

### Dumplings

*Mrs. James L. Sigler. · Original printed p. 171, scan p. 178 · `mg1930-p178-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pt. flour
- ½ tsp. salt
- 2 tsp. baking powder, heaping
- milk

**Preparation**

Mix and sift dry ingredients, and add just enough milk for a very stiff dough. Drop in lumps the size of an egg on top of meat and cook for 10 minutes without removing cover.

---

### Dumplings

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 171, scan p. 178 · `mg1930-p178-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 egg
- 2 tsp. baking powder
- 1 cup milk
- flour

**Preparation**

Mix ingredients in order given. Drop from a wet spoon on top of meat. Boil without taking cover off for 15 to 20 minutes.

---

### Chipped Beef And Canned Corn

Chop beef fine, add corn, milk, and pepper. Pour into greased dish. Cover with 1 cup crumbs, mixed with 2 tbsp. melted butter. Bake ½ hour at 350 degrees.

### Chinese Meat Loaf

Mix and bake about 1 hour at 375 degrees.

### Meat Loaf For Twelve

- 1 onion cut up

Mix thoroughly, bake in bread tin 2 hours

### Meat Loaf

*Mrs. Harry Becker. · Original printed p. 172, scan p. 179 · `mg1930-p179-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ lbs. ground beef
- ½ lb. lean pork, ground
- Onion, to season
- Salt and pepper
- Juice of ½ lemon
- 2 eggs
- 1 cup bread or cracker crumbs, soaked in as much milk as they absorb

**Preparation**

To the ground beef and pork, add onion, lemon juice, salt and pepper to taste. Add the two eggs, well beaten; then crumbs soaked inmilk. Bake in moderate oven from 30 to 40 minutes. This makes a good sized loaf.

---

### Meat Loaf

*Mrs. M. Ellis. · Original printed p. 172, scan p. 179 · `mg1930-p179-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups bread crumbs
- 2 tbsp. flour
- 2½ tsp. salt
- ¾ tsp. pepper

- ¼ tsp. paprika
- ¼ tsp. mustard
- ⅛ tsp. sage
- ½ lb. ground beef
- ½ lb. ground pork
- ½ lb. ground veal
- 1½ cups milk
- 1 egg (mentioned in method, not listed among printed ingredients)

**Preparation**

Mix bread crumbs, flour, salt, pepper, onion, paprika, mustard and sage together and work into meat. Add the beaten egg to the milk and knead into the meat mixture. Mold into a loaf and place strips of bacon over the top. Bake two hours. Serves 8.

---

### Meat Loaf

*Mrs. P. V. Sangren. · Original printed p. 172, scan p. 179 · `mg1930-p179-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups bread crumbs
- ½ tsp. sage
- 2 tbsp. flour

- ¼ tsp. pepper
- ½ lb. pork, ground
- 4 tbsp. onion, chopped fine
- 1 egg
- ¼ tsp. paprika
- 1½ cups milk
- 1/4 tsp. mustard

**Preparation**

Mix bread crumbs, flour, salt and all seasonings together, and work in meat. Add the beaten egg to the milk and knead into meat mixture gradually. Mold into a loaf and place strips of bacon over the top. Bake 2 hours at 325 degrees.

---

### Meat Loaf

- 1 can tomato soup

**Preparation**

Mix all together, and add a small amount of water. Place in a baking dish, and bake about one hour. Pour over this 1 can of tomato soup, and cook fifteen minutes longer. Serve hot. Serves about ten.

### Meat Loaf

*Mrs. L. L. Taylor. · Original printed p. 173, scan p. 180 · `mg1930-p180-r01`*

- salt and pepper
- ½ lb. pork steak, ground
- 1/2 to 3/4 cup tomato, crushed,

- but not strained

**Preparation**

Shape into loaf, cover with water, and bake 1½ hours at 325 degrees.

### Creamed Sweetbreads

*Mrs. J. E. Rork. · Original printed p. 173, scan p. 180 · `mg1930-p180-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lb. sweetbreads
- ½ cup butter
- 1 can mushrooms
- ¼ diced pepper
- 1 pt. rich milk
- 2 tsp. salt

**Preparation**

Remove tough fibre from sweetbreads; wash and boil five minutes. Drain, pull apart and dredge in egg and flour. Brown in pan with the butter, adding mushrooms, milk and seasonings. Thicken with flour. Serve in cases or on toast.

---

### Meat Souffle

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 173, scan p. 180 · `mg1930-p180-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1⅓ cups white sauce
- ⅔ cup meat, put through food chopper
- 2 eggs
- Salt and pepper

**Preparation**

Beat eggs separately. Add yolks to meat, and well seasoned white sauce. Fold in whites. Put in well buttered baking dish. Sprinkle top with paprika, or buttered crumbs. Bake in pan of hot water at 300 degrees until eggs are set.

---

### For Left- Over Meat

*Mrs. James L. Sigler. · Original printed p. 173, scan p. 180 · `mg1930-p180-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- Leftover cooked meat, chopped
- 1 egg per muffin-cup portion
- Salt and pepper, to taste

**Preparation**

Chop meat in food chopper, and press into gem tins, leaving a hole in the center. Break 1 egg in each. Bake at 325 degrees for 20 minutes.

---

### Shepherds Pie

*Mrs. M. Ellis. · Original printed p. 173, scan p. 180 · `mg1930-p180-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- Leftover mashed potatoes
- Milk, for loosening potatoes
- 2 cups diced cooked meat
- 2 cups milk, for cream sauce
- 4 tbsp. butter
- 4 tbsp. flour
- Leftover gravy, optional
- Onion, green pepper or pimiento, optional

**Preparation**

On a well buttered pan place mounds of left over mashed potatoes, which have been mixed with milk to the right consistency; cut left over meat into small pieces, combine with equal amount of medium cream sauce (2 cups milk, 4 tbsp. butter and 4 tbsp. flour). If there is any left over gravy, mix with milk to make right consistency. Add 2 cups of the meat; onion, green pepper or pimento may be added for better flavor. Pour around the molds of mashed potatoes. Bake in a moderate oven until browned. If desired, the dish may be lined with the mashed potato and filled with the creamed mixture.

---

### Luncheon Dish

*Mrs. Carlos Shepard. · Original printed p. 174, scan p. 181 · `mg1930-p181-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- Cooked cold meat, preferably roast beef, diced
- 1 small onion, chopped
- ½ green pepper, diced
- 1 can tomatoes
- Buttered toast, for serving

**Preparation**

Any cold meat (preferably roast beef), diced; 1 small onion; ½ green pepper, diced; and 1 can tomatoes. Add meat, onion and pepper to tomatoes and simmer until tender, about 20 minutes. Serve on buttered toast.

---

### Lamb And String Beans

*Mrs. K. S. Russell. · Original printed p. 174, scan p. 181 · `mg1930-p181-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 7
- 1 lb. lamb breast
- 1 qt. tomatoes
- 2 tbsp. fat
- 1 lb. string beans
- 1 minced onion
- boiled rice

**Preparation**

Cut lamb into cubes and brown in fat. Add onion, and when brown add tomatoes and seasoning. Cook about 20 minutes. Add beans, broken in small pieces. Simmer until tender. Serve with a border of boiled rice.

---

### Swift's Premium Ham Smothered In Mushrooms

*Mrs. E. R. Valentine. · Original printed p. 166, scan p. 173 · `mg1930-p173-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 slice Swift’s Premium Savor-tite ham, about ½ inch thick
- Brookfield butter, for browning
- Mushrooms, fresh or canned
- Sliced pineapple, for garnish

**Preparation**

Brown one side of ham in butter. Turn so the browned side faces up. Cover with mushrooms, cover the pan and simmer about 15 minutes, or until mushrooms are tender. Serve on a platter with sliced pineapple. If using canned mushrooms, reserve their liquid for gravy.

---

## Meat Substitutes, Pasta, and Rice

### Chili Con Carne

*Mrs. B. C. Wilson. · Original printed p. 175, scan p. 182 · `mg1930-p182-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 lbs. veal, ground
- 1 can red kidney beans
- butter
- 1 can tomatoes
- onion
- ½ box spaghetti
- 1 qt. hot water
- 1 tsp. chili powder

**Preparation**

Fry veal and onion in butter, add hot water and simmer. Add beans, tomatoes, and spaghetti, and continue to simmer. 15 minutes before serving add chili powder to taste.

---

### Chili Con Carne

*Mrs. P. V. Sangren. · Original printed p. 175, scan p. 182 · `mg1930-p182-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 can kidney beans
- 4 or 5 onions chopped fine
- 1 can tomatoes
- 1½ lbs. ground beef
- salt
- 2 tbsp. chili powder (to taste)

**Preparation**

Brown meat, add other ingredients, and cook 2 or 3 hours.

---

### Mexican Goulash

*Mrs. M. E. Clark. · Original printed p. 175, scan p. 182 · `mg1930-p182-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- ¾ lb. ground round steak
- 1 tbsp. bacon fat
- 3 medium onions, sliced
- 1 tsp. salt
- 1 cup or more diced celery
- 1 can tomatoes
- 1 pkg. spaghetti, cooked in boiling salted water until tender
- 2 tbsp. sugar
- dash chili powder or ¼ tsp. paprika
- ½ cup water, if needed

**Preparation**

Fry ground steak in bacon fat until brown, with the sliced onions. Add salt, celery, tomatoes, cooked spaghetti, sugar, and seasoning. Simmer slowly 1 hour, stirring occasionally. If too dry, add ½ cup water.

---

### Chop Suey

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 175, scan p. 182 · `mg1930-p182-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- ¼ lb. pork or bacon
- ½ cup molasses
- 1 lb. veal, cut fine
- 1 can mushrooms
- 2 cups onions, chopped
- 1 can bean sprouts, optional
- 3 cups celery, chopped
- Chop Suey Sauce
- 1 green pepper

**Preparation**

Fry bacon or pork in pan slowly, add veal, frying for 10 minutes. Remove meat to another pan, leaving fat in frying pan. Fry onion and celery in frying pan. Put all in pan with meat, adding water to nearly cover, and chopped green pepper. Simmer 1 hour, adding more water if necessary. Add molasses and seasonings, and mushrooms. Thicken, with cornstarch or flour. Serve with Chop Suey Sauce. -Jane Rork.

---

### Chop Suey

*Mrs. Walter Graham. · Original printed p. 176, scan p. 183 · `mg1930-p183-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lb. veal
- Show You Chop Suey Sauce
- 1 lb. pork
- 1 can mushrooms
- 6 medium sized onions
- Bamboo Sprouts may be added
- 2 or 3 stalks celery

**Preparation**

Cut meat, onions, and celery in small pieces. Put oil or fat in pan and heat. Then put in meat and brown on all sides. Then add water and cook 10 or 15 minutes. Then add onions and celery, and sauce to taste. Cook about 1 hour. Just before it has finished cooking, thicken a little with flour and water. Then add mushrooms. Have rice boiled Chinese style to serve with this.

---

### Chop Suey

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 176, scan p. 183 · `mg1930-p183-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lb. veal steak
- ½ lb. fresh ham
- 2 cups chopped celery
- 2 cups chopped onion

**Preparation**

Cook meat 5 minutes in onion juice. Pour over it 2 cups water, 2 tbsp. brown sugar and 4 tbsp. "Show You" sauce. Thicken slightly with flour when meat is done. Add salt and pepper. Serve with rice.

---

### Chop Suey

*Mrs. Alfred Moerdyk. · Original printed p. 176, scan p. 183 · `mg1930-p183-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ lb. of veal or beef
- 2 tbsp. brown sugar
- ½ lb. lean pork
- 1 scant tsp. salt
- 2 cups of celery
- 1 cup water
- 2 cups onion
- 6 tsp. chop suey sauce

**Preparation**

Put in an iron spider 1 tbsp. lard and 1 tbsp. butter, add meat cut in small pieces, and cook ten minutes. Then add all the other ingredients, and cook slowly ½ hour. Add more water if needed. Thicken with a little flour. Serve with boiled rice. Serves four.

---

### Italian Macaroni

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 176, scan p. 183 · `mg1930-p183-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lb. beef, diced
- 1 can mushrooms
- 6 large red onions
- 2 cups water
- 1 clove garlic
- 1 lb. macaroni
- 1 No. 3 can tomatoes
- 1 lb. grated cheese

**Preparation**

Brown diced beef in a little fat. Add sliced onions and garlic and cook until softened. Add tomatoes, mushrooms and water; simmer gently, partly covered, about 2–3 hours, stirring and adding water as needed, until the meat is tender and sauce thick. (The original version simmered 6 hours.) Cook macaroni in salted water until just tender. In a buttered baking dish alternate layers of macaroni, sauce and grated cheese. Bake at 325°F for 35–45 minutes until bubbling. Makes about 12–16 servings.

---

### Macaroni With Chipped Beef

*Mrs. J. W. Spalsbury. · Original printed p. 177, scan p. 184 · `mg1930-p184-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup macaroni
- 1¼ cups milk
- 1 tbsp. grated cheese
- ¼ lb. dried beef
- ⅛ tsp. mustard
- Pepper

**Preparation**

Break macaroni in pieces, cook in boiling salted water. Butter a baking dish, put in a layer of macaroni and beef with a sprinkle of mustard. Continue until all is used, having a layer of macaroni last. Pour over milk, sprinkle with cheese and bake 35 minutes in quick oven.

---

### Creamettes ( Or Shell Macaroni )

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 177, scan p. 184 · `mg1930-p184-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup dry creamettes or shell macaroni
- 1 cup scalded cream

- 3 eggs, separated
- 1 pimiento, chopped
- Minced parsley, to taste
- Onion juice, to taste
- ½ tsp. salt
- 1 cup grated cheese
- For sauce: 1 can mushrooms
- For sauce: 1 cup white sauce

**Preparation**

WITH MUSHROOM SAUCE 1 cup creamettes 1 pimento, cut up 1 cup scalded cream parsley to taste, minced 1/4 cup melted butter onion or onion juice to taste 1 cup soft bread crumbs ½ tsp. salt 3 eggs, beaten separately 1 cup grated cheese, Ame. or Nippy Cook creamettes as usual in large quantity of boiling salted water. Mix ingredients, folding in eggs last. Put in well buttered mold. Set in pan of hot water and bake 3/4 hour at 300 degrees. Turn out on chop plate, garnished with parsley. Use spatula to get out of mold. Fill center with mushroom sauce, made by adding 1 can mushrooms to 1 cup white sauce. Serve with vegetable salad (tomatoes and cucumber).

---

### Macaroni With Vegetables

*Mrs. Carlos Shepard. · Original printed p. 177, scan p. 184 · `mg1930-p184-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 lb. round steak
- 1 can tomatoes
- 2 small onions
- 1 can peas
- 1 can mushrooms, drained
- 2 pkgs. macaroni
- 1 pt. water, boiling
- 2 tbsp. butter

**Preparation**

Brown the butter in an iron skillet, and add meat, dice fine, and onions. When brown, add mushrooms and boiling water. Add tomatoes and cook 5 minutes. Add peas and cook 5 minutes. Add mushroom liquor and 1 cup boiling water and cook 5 minutes. Add macaroni and pour all into a butterd baking dish. Slice cheese on top and bake ½ hour in a moderate oven. Serves 8, generously.

---

### Egg Noodles

*Mrs. C. L. Mohney. · Original printed p. 177, scan p. 184 · `mg1930-p184-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 egg

**Preparation**

Beat well and add: 1/4 tsp. salt and 1 tbsp. cold water Beat again and add flour to make stiff dough

Roll very thin on well floured board and stand two hours; roll and cut in narrow strips.

---

### Noodle Ring

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 178, scan p. 185 · `mg1930-p185-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 box Richelieu noodles, fine cut
- 2 tbsp. melted butter
- 3 eggs
- 1 cup grated cheese
- 2 cups milk

**Preparation**

Boil noodles in salted water until tender, drain, and pour over cold water, and drain again. Beat eggs separately, add yolks to whites. Add milk, butter and cheese to noodles. Fold in eggs. Bake in buttered ring in hot water for 45 minutes at 300 degrees. Put plate over and turn upside down. In center put chicken a la king.

---

### Leftover Ham

*Mrs. R. A. Winslow. · Original printed p. 178, scan p. 185 · `mg1930-p185-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pkg. noodles
- 1 cup milk
- ham, chopped
- 1 egg
- grated cheese
- green pepper

**Preparation**

Cook noodles. Make alternate layers of noodles, ham, grated cheese in deep baking dish. Add beaten egg to milk, and pour over mixture. Put a few slices of green pepper on top and bake at 350 degrees for about 3/4 hour.

---

### Creamed Noodle With Cheese

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 178, scan p. 185 · `mg1930-p185-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 egg
- Noodles
- 1 tsp. water
- 1 cup cream
- ½ tsp. salt
- 4 tbsp. grated cheese
- 1 cup flour
- 4 tbsp. buttered crumbs

**Preparation**

Beat egg slightly with salt and water. Add flour, cutting mixture with a knife. Knead until smooth and stiff. One or two more tbsp. flour may be necessary. Roll on a slightly floured molding board until as thin as possible. Cover and let stand 20 minutes. Cut in tiny squares, and let dry for 30 minutes. Drop into 2 quarts of boiling water, salted, and cook 15 minutes, boiling rapidly. Drain and put into buttered baking dish. Pour over cream to make moist, sprinkle with cheese, and cover with buttered crumbs. Bake in a hot oven, 300 degrees, until brown.

---

- 1 diced green pepper

**Preparation**

Cook spaghetti in boiling salted water, and drain. Add vegetables and chopped ham, seasonings and milk, then add beaten egg and place in casserole and bake until brown.

Cook spaghetti in usual way. Put butter in frying pan, add chopped onion, then tuna fish and cook for 5 minutes. Add rest of ingredients and heat thoroughly. Put into hot dish and garnish with hard boiled egg pressed through potato ricer.

### Spaghetti

*Mrs. Harry Becker. · Original printed p. 179, scan p. 186 · `mg1930-p186-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pkg. spaghetti
- 2 small onions
- 1 can tomatoes
- butter
- ½ can peas
- pepper
- 1 cup cheese
- salt

**Preparation**

Boil in salted water the required amount of spaghetti for twentyfive minutes. Drain, pour cold water on, and drain again. ( This keeps it from getting sticky.) Butter the baking dish, and put in a layer of spaghetti, a layer of tomatoes, a layer of cheese, a layer of peas, a few slices of onion and bits of butter, pepper and salt, and so on till dish is full. Bake in a moderate oven until brown, about forty- five minutes. Serves 6 or 8.

### Spaghetti Rarebit

Melt butter in double boiler; add cut up cheese and melt; then tomato soup and chopped onion; then two cans of spaghetti and let cool for at least twenty minutes. Serve hot on wafers. This serves six. Mrs. D. Boudeman.

### Spaghetti With Mushrooms

Cook spaghetti in boiling salted water until tender. Drain, rinse with cold water and add to tomato soup. Brown mushrooms in fat and add with cheese to spaghetti. Heat thoroughly and serve. Time in combining, 15 minutes. Time in cooking, 20 minutes. Recipe makes four servings.

### Spanish Rice

Wash rice six times; boil in salted water until nearly soft. Cut up green pepper, onion, tomatoes and bacon; add to rice, seasoning with salt and pepper. Use butter if not using bacon. Bake 1 hour at 325°F.

Cook for two hours in the double boiler.

### Spanish Rice

### French Rice

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 181, scan p. 188 · `mg1930-p188-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup fresh green peas
- 4 tbsp. butter
- 1 small onion
- 1 cup peeled and diced tomatoes
- ½ tsp. salt
- 1 hard cooked egg
- 2 cups boiled rice

**Preparation**

Peel and mince onion. Add to peas and cook until tender in boiling water. Add salt last 10 minutes of cooking. Drain if necessary. Combine with rice, butter, and tomatoes, mixing well with fork. Turn into a well buttered baking dish and bake 15 minutes in a hot oven, 375 to 400 degrees. Garnish with hard cooked egg. Serve from baking dish.

### Baked Cheese And Bread Custard (Cheese Soufflé)

*Mrs. D. C. Rockwell. · Original printed p. 181, scan p. 188 · `mg1930-p188-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 slices buttered bread
- ¼ lb. cheese
- 2 tbsp. butter
- 2 eggs
- 1 pt. milk

**Preparation**

Heat oven to 350°F and butter a baking dish with the 2 tbsp. butter. Arrange alternating layers of buttered bread and diced cheese, ending with cheese. Beat eggs and milk together and pour evenly over the layers. Rest 10 minutes to soak, then bake 30–40 minutes until puffed and set in the center. Serve hot.

### Casserole Of Rice And Meat

Season meat mixed with crumbs and beaten egg, and add meat stock to make mixture pour. Line greased mold or baking dish with 2 or 3 cups of rice.
