## Fish, Poultry, and Game

### Egg And Cheese Dainty

*Mrs. Harris Meyer. · Original printed p. 185, scan p. 192 · `mg1930-p192-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 plain rusk or crisp toast, crumbled
- Grated or broken sharp cheese, enough for 2 layers
- 4 eggs
- Milk, enough to come halfway up the dish
- Butter
- Salt and pepper

**Preparation**

Butter a small baking dish. Crumble rusk over the base and scatter with a layer of cheese. Dot with butter, season, then crack four eggs evenly over the surface, breaking the yolks slightly. Top with another layer of cheese and rusk, dot with butter and pour milk down the side to reach halfway up the dish. Bake at 375°F for about 20 minutes, until eggs are set. Serves 4.

---

### Stuffed Eggs Au Gratin

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 185, scan p. 192 · `mg1930-p192-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 eggs
- 2 tbsp. bread crumbs
- salt and pepper
- 2 tbsp. butter
- parsley
- 1 cup white sauce

**Preparation**

mustard

Boil eggs hard, and throw in cold water. Remove shells. Cut lengthwise. Take out yolks, and rub them to a paste with seasonings, crumbs and 1 tbsp. butter. Fill whites and place eggs in greased pie dish. Pour over 1 cup white sauce, sprinkle with more crumbs and dot with butter. Bake until browned in quite a hot oven, 375 degrees. Serve in this dish.

---

### Baked Omelet

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 185, scan p. 192 · `mg1930-p192-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 eggs
- ½ cup milk
- ½ tbsp. butter
- ½ tsp. salt
- ½ tbsp. flour

**Preparation**

Make white sauce of butter, flour, milk, and salt. Cook to custard stage. Fold in beaten yolks and beaten whites. Pour into hot, buttered baking dish. Bake in hot oven, about 375 degrees, about 15 minutes. Try with knife.

---

### Horse Radish Eggs

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 185, scan p. 192 · `mg1930-p192-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 hard-boiled eggs
- 2 tbsp. thick mayonnaise
- 1 tbsp. freshly grated horseradish
- Bread rounds, 12
- Butter, for frying bread, optional
- Paprika

**Preparation**

Boil and prepare eggs as for deviled eggs. With the yolks mix 2 tbsp. thick mayonnaise and 1 tbsp. freshly ground horse radish to each 6 yolks. Fill egg whites. Serve on ovals of bread, which have been hollowed out to fit the egg. Either toast the bread ovals or fry it in butter until browned. Sprinkle top of egg with paprika.

---

### Roast Turkey And Dressing

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 186, scan p. 193 · `mg1930-p193-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 turkey, about 12–14 lb.
- 8–10 cups dry bread cubes for dressing
- 1 small onion, minced
- 3 tbsp. diced celery
- 8 tbsp. melted butter
- Sage, parsley, salt and pepper, to taste
- Stock or water, as needed to moisten dressing
- Butter or bacon fat, for rubbing bird
- Water or stock, for pan and gravy
- Turkey giblets, optional

**Preparation**

Heat oven to 325°F. Remove giblets and pat the turkey dry. For dressing, toss bread cubes with sautéed onion and celery, melted butter, sage, parsley, salt and pepper; moisten lightly with stock. Bake the dressing separately in a buttered dish at 350°F for 30–40 minutes, until heated through and browned.

Season the turkey with salt and pepper and rub with butter or bacon fat. Place on a rack in a roasting pan with a little water or stock. Roast at 325°F, basting occasionally, until the thickest part of breast registers 165°F and the thigh is cooked through (roughly 3–4 hours for a 12–14 lb. unstuffed bird; check early). Rest 20–30 minutes before carving.

For gravy, simmer giblets separately if using. Skim fat from pan juices, whisk flour into a few tablespoons of fat, and add pan juices and stock until the desired thickness is reached. Season to taste.

---

### Wild Duck

*Mrs. Paul Sangren. · Original printed p. 186, scan p. 193 · `mg1930-p193-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cleaned wild duck
- 1 onion or carrot, for parboiling
- Bacon strips, for lining and topping
- Prepared bread dressing, enough to fill cavity

**Preparation**

Simmer the cleaned wild duck with onion or carrot in water for about 1 hour, then drain. Line the cavity with bacon and fill loosely with prepared dressing. Lay bacon strips over the breast. Roast at 350°F until thoroughly cooked, about 45–75 minutes depending on size.

---

### Pheasant

*Mrs. James Fleugel. · Original printed p. 186, scan p. 193 · `mg1930-p193-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cleaned pheasant
- Butter or fat, for basting
- Salt and pepper

**Preparation**

Heat oven to 375°F. Season pheasant with salt and pepper and rub with a little butter. Roast in a shallow pan, basting occasionally, about 35–50 minutes depending on size, until the thickest part reaches 165°F. Rest briefly before carving.

---

### Rabbit

*Mrs. J. B. Fleugel. · Original printed p. 186, scan p. 193 · `mg1930-p193-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 dressed rabbit, jointed
- Cold water and salt, for soaking
- 1 onion
- 1 bay leaf
- Water for simmering
- Butter or fat, if frying

**Preparation**

Skin, cut up rabbit and soak all night in cold salted water. Rinse rabbit and bring to boil in cold water with 1 tbsp. soda. Take rabbit out and go over each piece cleaning it well. Cook until tender in fresh salted water with 1 onion and a bay leaf. The rabbit can be made into a pie or can be fried brown.

---

### Parsley Bread Sauce

*Mrs. J. B. Fleugel. · Original printed p. 186, scan p. 193 · `mg1930-p193-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup milk
- 1 onion
- ½ cup chopped parsley
- 2 slices bread
- salt
- 2 tbsp. butter

**Preparation**

Heat milk with onion in it. Remove onion, add bread cut up, butter, salt and beat to a cream, add parsley. Serve with fowl.

---

### Chicken Mousse

*Mrs. James E. Rork. · Original printed p. 187, scan p. 194 · `mg1930-p194-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup rich chicken stock
- 1½ cups diced cooked white chicken meat
- ½ tsp. salt
- Dash paprika and celery salt
- 2 tbsp. gelatine
- 2 tbsp. cold water
- ½ cup chopped olives
- 1 cup whipped cream
- 2 egg whites, beaten stiff

**Preparation**

Soak gelatine in cold water. Add chicken stock brought to boiling point. Add seasonings and chicken. When ready to set add cream, eggs and olives. Put in mold and chill for three hours. (Lobster may be substituted instead of chicken. )

---

### Chicken A La King

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 187, scan p. 194 · `mg1930-p194-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cooked chicken, meat diced
- 3 cups chicken stock, or 2 cups stock plus 1 cup cream
- 2 tbsp. chicken fat
- 1 sweet pepper
- 2 tbsp. flour
- 4 hard-boiled eggs, diced
- 1 can mushrooms
- Salt, to taste

**Preparation**

Cook chicken in stock until tender; cool and dice meat, reserving stock. Cook chopped sweet pepper briefly until nearly tender, then soften in chicken fat. Stir in flour, gradually whisk in 3 cups stock (or 2 cups stock and 1 cup cream), and cook until thickened. Add diced chicken, hard-boiled eggs and mushrooms; heat through and salt to taste. Serve over toast, rice or biscuits.

---

### Timbles, Chicken Or Veal

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 187, scan p. 194 · `mg1930-p194-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 tbsp. butter
- ½ tbsp. chopped parsley
- 1/4 cup bread crumbs
- 2 eggs
- ⅔ cup milk
- salt
- 1 cup chopped meat
- pepper

**Preparation**

Melt butter, add bread crumbs, and milk. Cook 5 minutes, stir, ring. Add meat, eggs, slightly beaten, and parsley. Season to taste. Turn into buttered, individual ramekins, making them ⅔ full. Set in pan of hot water. Cover with buttered paper and bake 20 minutes. Serve with Sauce Bechamel,

---

### Sauce Bechamel

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 187, scan p. 194 · `mg1930-p194-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ cups chicken or veal stock
- 6 peppercorns
- 1 onion slice
- 1 carrot slice
- 1 small bay leaf
- 1 parsley sprig
- ¼ cup butter
- ¼ cup flour
- 1 cup scalded milk
- Salt and pepper

**Preparation**

Cook stock 20 minutes with all the seasonings. Strain. There should be one cup. Melt butter, add flour, and, gradually, stock and milk. Use equal parts of stock and milk if you haven't enough stock.

- pepper and salt
- finely

### Baked Fish

- Lake Trout or White Fish
- butter
- flour
- salt and pepper

Clean and split fish down the back, remove bones. Dredge with flour on both sides. Spread baking pan with butter, or mixture of butter and meat fryings, generously. Place fish in pan, skin side down. Season with salt and pepper, and dot with butter. Bake about 50 minutes at 350 degrees. Make oven a little hotter at last if fish is not browned on top.

- 3 cups mashed potatoes

### Broiled Whitefish

*Mrs. C. A. Buskirk. · Original printed p. 188, scan p. 195 · `mg1930-p195-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- Fresh whitefish, 2½–3 lbs.
- ½ cup water
- 3 tbsp. butter

**Preparation**

Clean, split, wash, dry and salt a fresh whitefish, 2½ to 3 lbs. Place in a shallow pan and baste thoroughly with a sauce made of ½ cup water, 3 tbsp. butter, juice of 1 lemon, 1 tbsp. Worcestershire sauce and cayenne pepper.

Place in broiler; broil 10–15 minutes, turn and baste thoroughly, then broil 10–15 minutes more. Turn again if necessary to cook thoroughly.

### Crab Meat A La Newberg

### Lobster A La Newberg

### Scalloped Oysters

*Mrs. A. L. Moerdyke. · Original printed p. 190, scan p. 197 · `mg1930-p197-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- Shucked oysters
- Coarsely crushed crackers
- Butter, melted and in small pieces
- Cream, to moisten
- Salt and paprika

**Preparation**

Use a shallow baking dish ( about 1/2 inches deep). Put a little melted butter in the bottom, and a layer of crushed (not pulverized ) crackers, then a layer of oysters. Season with salt and paprika, and pour over this cream. Add crackers, lumps of butter, oysters, and seasoning, until the dish is full. Over the last layer of crackers pour cream enough to moisten. Bake ten minutes in a very hot oven. The secret of good scalloped oysters, lies in using a shallow dish, so that the crumbs, rich with butter and cream, may be crisped with the cooking and the oysters not over cooked.

---

### Shrimps Baked With Tomatoes

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 190, scan p. 197 · `mg1930-p197-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pt. shrimps, cleaned
- salt and pepper
- cracker crumbs
- butter
- 2 cups canned tomatoes
- 1 tsp. sugar

**Preparation**

Put a layer of crumbs in a well buttered baking dish, add a layer of shrimps, cover with tomatoes, season with salt and pepper and sugar and dot with butter. Repeat, and cover with crumbs, dotting generously with butter and brown in a moderately hot oven, 375 degrees. A few drops of onion juice and a little minced parsley may be used. Minced celery is another variation.

---

### Deviled Shrimp

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 190, scan p. 197 · `mg1930-p197-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 tbsp. butter
- ½ tsp. paprika
- 4 tbsp. flour
- 6 eggs, hard cooked
- 2 cups milk, scant
- 2 cans shrimp
- 1 tsp. salt
- ¾ cup crumbs
- 1 tsp. mustard
- 1/4 cup melted butter

**Preparation**

Make white sauce of first butter, milk and flour in double boiler. Add seasonings, and remove from hot water. Clean and cut shrimp. Dice eggs. Add eggs and shrimp to white sauce. Place in casserole, and cover with crumbs which have been mixed with melted butter. Bake about 25 minutes at 375 degrees, or until a nice brown. Serves 6 to 8. Salmon and Crab meat may be used the same way.

---

### Shrimp A La Creole

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 190, scan p. 197 · `mg1930-p197-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 tbsp. butter
- 2 pimentoes
- 1 tbsp. flour
- ¼ cup onion, grated
- 1 cup milk
- ½ cup tomato
- 1 egg
- bit of garlic
- salt and pepper
- 1 can shrimps

**Preparation**

Melt butter, add flour and blend thoroughly, add milk gradually, stirring until smooth. Cook until thickened, add eggs slightly beaten, salt and pepper. Cook three minutes. Add chopped pimentos, grated onions, and canned or chopped tomatoes, shrimps, and seasonings. Re-heat. Serve in large deep clam shells. Sprinkle with paprika and finely minced parsley. Time in combining, 15 minutes. Time in cooking, 20 minutes. Recipe makes six servings.

---

### Salmon Timbles

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 191, scan p. 198 · `mg1930-p198-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 tbsp. butter
- 1 tbsp. lemon juice
- ½ cup stale bread crumbs
- 1 egg
- 3/4 cup milk
- salt and pepper
- 1 cup flaked salmon

**Preparation**

Melt butter, add bread crumbs and milk and cook, stirring constantly until thick and smooth. Add lemon juice to salmon and mix well with a fork. Add egg slightly beaten to sauce, season with salt and pepper and remove from fire. Add fish. Fill buttered individual mold ⅔ full with the mixture and put into a pan of hot water. Cover with buttered paper and bake 30 minutes in a hot oven, 400 degrees. Remove from molds to serve.

---

### Creamed Salmon

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 191, scan p. 198 · `mg1930-p198-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups milk
- 1 can salmon
- 2 tbsp. flour
- 1 egg
- 2 tbsp. butter
- 1 tsp. mustard
- 1 tsp. salt

**Preparation**

Make a white sauce. Flake salmon and add to it. Stir in beaten egg and mustard dissolved in a little water. Serve on toast.

---

### Salmon Loaf

*Mrs. Walter Graham. · Original printed p. 191, scan p. 198 · `mg1930-p198-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 can red salmon
- 1 egg
- 1 lemon
- Crumbs from 6 crackers or ½ cup bread crumbs
- Pinch salt
- 1 cup milk

**Preparation**

Remove bones from salmon. Then add egg, beaten, salt and crumbs. Pour over this the milk, which has been thickened and made into white sauce. Last of all squeeze a few drops of lemon juice over it. This may be baked in an oven for 30 minutes or steamed for 1 hour.

---

### Ring Of Salmon

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 191, scan p. 198 · `mg1930-p198-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pint (or 1-lb. can) salmon
- ½ cup cream
- ½ tsp. salt
- ½ cup dry bread crumbs
- ⅛ tsp. pepper
- ⅛ tsp. ground mace
- 1 tbsp. lemon juice
- 3 egg whites

**Preparation**

Remove skin and bones from salmon and force through colander. Add bread crumbs, which should be from soft part of loaf, cream, add seasonings, and enough hot water to make moist. Mix thoroughly and fold in stiffly beaten egg whites. Turn into a well buttered ring mold, and steam for 30 minutes. Remove from mold and fill center with creamed peas.

---

### Creamed Tuna Fish

*Mrs. J. B. Fleugel. · Original printed p. 192, scan p. 199 · `mg1930-p199-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 can tuna, flaked
- 1 cup diced celery
- 2 hard-boiled eggs, chopped
- Mushrooms
- A little raw onion
- Pimiento, chopped
- Cream sauce, enough to coat

**Preparation**

Flake a can of Tuna Fish. Add 1 cup diced celery, 2 hard boiled eggs, mushrooms, a dash of raw onion and a little pimento. Cover with cream sauce.

---

### California Chicken Pie

*Mrs. A. L. Kingston. · Original printed p. 192, scan p. 199 · `mg1930-p199-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 potatoes
- 1 can tuna (chicken of the sea)
- 2 carrots
- 2 cups white sauce
- 1 cup peas

**Preparation**

Mix all together. Line a deep dish with crust, add the mixture, and cover with pie crust, which has been pierced to let steam escape. Bake 30 minutes or until crust is done at about 400 degrees.

---

### Baked Tuna Fish And Macaroni

*Mrs. J. B. Fleugel. · Original printed p. 192, scan p. 199 · `mg1930-p199-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pkg. macaroni
- 1 can tuna fish
- 1 qt. white sauce
- 1½ cups diced celery
- a little onion

**Preparation**

Cook macaroni until tender. Add crumbled tuna fish, celery, white sauce and onion. Bake until brown in buttered baking dish.

## Vegetables and Soups

### Deviled Apples

*Mrs. K. S. Russell. · Original printed p. 195, scan p. 202 · `mg1930-p202-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- Large baking apples
- Canned deviled ham, for filling
- Water, for baking dish

**Preparation**

Core large apples. Fill centers with canned deviled ham. Put in baking dish with a little water and bake until tender.

---

### New England Baked Beans

*Mrs. J. B. Fleugel. · Original printed p. 195, scan p. 202 · `mg1930-p202-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 cups navy beans
- 1½ lb. salt pork
- 2 tbsp. molasses
- salt

**Preparation**

Cover beans with water and soak over night. Next morning parboil them until when you blow on some in a spoon they crack. Put half beans in bean pot, put salt pork in and rest of the beans. Add molasses and cover with boiling water. Cover pot and bake very slowly 6 hours, filling dish with water as needed. If pork seems very salt it may be parboiled with beans.

---

### Baked Beans

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 195, scan p. 202 · `mg1930-p202-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 qts. navy beans
- 2 large tsp. soda
- Salt pork (small piece for soup, plus 1½ lb. for beans)
- Bacon and onion, for layering
- 1 qt. boiling water (for sauce)
- 1 tsp. mustard (for sauce)
- 1 cup catsup (for sauce)
- 2 tbsp. molasses (for sauce)
- 1 cup oyster cocktail sauce (for sauce)
- salt and pepper

**Preparation**

Soak 2 qts. navy beans overnight. Wash and drain. Cover with boiling water; at the boil add 2 large tsp. soda and boil a few minutes. Drain, add fresh water, and boil until skins curl.

If desired, reserve about 1½ cups beans for soup in some cooking water with a small piece of salt pork, seasoning, and onion. Boil soup about 2 hours, adding water as needed.

Layer bacon, onion, and beans in a large crock, burying 1½ lb. salt pork (part lean) just below the surface. Combine boiling water, mustard, catsup, molasses, oyster cocktail sauce, salt, and pepper. Pour over beans. Add reserved sauce as needed. Bake about six hours at 325°F. Serves 25.

---

### Baked Kidney Beans

*Mrs. C. W. Lininger. · Original printed p. 195, scan p. 202 · `mg1930-p202-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 can Heinz kidney beans
- 1/4 cup catsup
- 1 green pepper

**Preparation**

Place all in glass baking dish. Sprinkle with brown sugar over the top. Bake about 1/2 hour at 325 degrees.

---

### Sour Beans

*Mrs. C. D. Brown. · Original printed p. 196, scan p. 203 · `mg1930-p203-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 qt. beans ( wax are best)
- ½ cup water
- ⅔ cup vinegar
- 1 small onion.
- ½ cup sugar

**Preparation**

Boil water, sugar and vinegar until sugar is dissolved, when cold add to beans which have been cooked until tender, add onion finely chopped. Nice to serve with cold meat lunches. Serves 6. This will keep a long time.

---

### Luncheon Beans And Spaghetti

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 196, scan p. 203 · `mg1930-p203-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 8 slices Swift's Premium Bacon
- 3 whole cloves
- 6 large tomatoes
- 2 cups lima beans
- 1 green pepper
- 3 cups cooked spaghetti
- 1 onion
- 1 tsp. salt
- ½ cup celery, diced
- 1/4 tsp. pepper

**Preparation**

Fry bacon crisp. Remove from fat and keep hot. Peel and cut tomatoes into small pieces, and add to bacon fat. Add pepper minced, onion finely shredded, celery, salt, pepper, cloves (optional) and sugar. Simmer, stirring to prevent sticking, until a thick sauce is made. Cook beans until tender in slightly salted, boiling water. Let water cook away without draining beans. Add beans and spaghetti to tomato sauce, and bring to boiling point. Garnish with Swift's Premium Bacon strips.

---

### Scalloped Lima Beans

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 196, scan p. 203 · `mg1930-p203-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1/4 cup diced green pepper
- 1 tsp. salt
- 1/4 cup diced onion
- 1/4 cup butter
- 2 cups cooked lima beans
- ⅛ tsp. pepper
- 1 cup tomato

**Preparation**

Parboil the green pepper and onion five minutes in one-half cup of water. Add beans, tomato, and seasonings. Put in a casserole and dot with butter. Cover and bake in a moderately hot oven. Time in combining, 15 minutes. Time in cooking, 40 minutes. Temperature, 375 °. Recipe makes six servings.

---

### Scalloped Lima Beans

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 196, scan p. 203 · `mg1930-p203-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups dry lima beans
- 2–4 hard-cooked eggs
- 1 can pimento
- ½ onion
- ¼ lb. strong cheese
- 1 quart white sauce (8 tbsp. butter or margarine, 8 tbsp. flour and 1 quart milk)

**Preparation**

Wash beans and soak overnight in cold water. Cook until tender in boiling salted water; drain. Place half the beans in a buttered baking dish. Add a layer of sliced hard-cooked eggs, chopped pimento, onion and grated cheese. Cover with white sauce and repeat, leaving grated cheese on top. Bake in a moderate oven until brown.

Time in combining: 30 minutes. Time in cooking: 40 minutes. Temperature: 350°F. Makes 10 servings.

---

### Beets With Sour Sauce

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 197, scan p. 204 · `mg1930-p204-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 8 beets
- Salted boiling water
- ½ cup reserved beet-cooking liquid
- Sour sauce (see accompanying recipe)

**Preparation**

Wash beets ( 8) and cook in boiling, salted water until soft. Drain, reserving ½ cup of water in which. beets were cooked Plunge into cold water, rub off skins, and cut into cubes. Reheat in sour sauce.

---

### Sour Sauce

*Mrs. C. A. Buskirk. · Original printed p. 197, scan p. 204 · `mg1930-p204-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 tbsp. butter
- 2 tbsp. flour
- ½ cup reserved beet-cooking water
- ¼ cup vinegar
- ¼ cup cream
- 1 tsp. sugar
- 1 tsp. salt
- ¼ tsp. pepper

**Preparation**

Melt butter and stir in flour; add reserved beet water, vinegar, cream, sugar, salt and pepper. Cook until thickened.

---

### Stuffed Beets

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 197, scan p. 204 · `mg1930-p204-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- Small beets
- Sweet spiced vinegar, for marinating
- Sardines or caviar, chopped
- Hard-boiled eggs, chopped
- Onion, finely chopped
- Sweet gherkins, chopped
- Mayonnaise and pimiento strips, to garnish

**Preparation**

Select small beets and boil until tender. Cool and peel. Remove enough of center to form case. Then let them stand in a sweetened and spiced vinegar, such as you would pickle beets in. Let them stand for two hours or more. Drain and fill with chopped sardines or caviar, and hard boiled eggs, onions, and sweet gherkins. Garnish with a little mayonnaise and strips of pimento.

---

### Dressing For Beets

*Louise Reifsnyder. · Original printed p. 197, scan p. 204 · `mg1930-p204-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 tbsp. butter
- ⅓ cup vinegar
- ½ cup sugar
- 1 tbsp. cornstarch
- 1 cup boiling water

**Preparation**

Beat all ingredients together before adding boiling water; then cook until thick.

### Cabbage Au Gratin

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 197, scan p. 204 · `mg1930-p204-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups boiled, minced cabbage
- 1 cup milk
- 2 tbsp. butter
- 2 tbsp. flour
- ½ tsp. salt
- ¼ tsp. pepper
- ¼ cup cheese

**Preparation**

Make a white sauce with milk, butter and flour; add seasonings, cabbage and cheese. Pour into a buttered baking dish, sprinkle buttered crumbs on top and bake at 425°F until brown, about 20 minutes.

---

### Scalloped Cabbage

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 197, scan p. 204 · `mg1930-p204-r06`*

- 2½ cups cooked shredded
- 4 tbsp. butter
- cabbage
- 1/3 cup flour
- 1 cup soft bread crumbs
- 1 tsp. salt
- 2 cups milk
- 2 tsp. poultry dressing

**Preparation**

Arrange cabbage and crumbs in layers in a baking dish. Make a white sauce of the remaining ingredients and pour over. Bake in a moderate oven. Time in combining, 5 minutes. Time in cooking, 20 minutes. Temperature, 350°. Recipe makes eight servings.

---

### Carrots Au Gratin

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 198, scan p. 205 · `mg1930-p205-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups carrots
- 1½ tbsp. butter
- 1 tbsp. green pepper, finely chopped
- 1 tbsp. onion, finely chopped
- ¾ cup stock
- ¼ cup cream
- Salt and pepper
- Buttered crumbs

**Preparation**

Melt butter, add green pepper and onion and cook three minutes. Add flour and blend thoroughly. Add liquid slowly and stir until thickened. Season with salt and pepper. Put carrots in a baking dish, pour sauce over them, cover with buttered crumbs, and bake until crumbs are brown. Time in cooking, 20 minutes. Temperature, 350°. Recipe makes 4 servings.

### Carrot Fritters

*Mrs. Katherine S. Russell. · Original printed p. 198, scan p. 205 · `mg1930-p205-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- Large carrots
- Prepared fritter batter
- Oil or fat, for deep-frying

**Preparation**

Scrape large carrots. Cook until tender. Slice about ½ inch thick. Dip in fritter batter, and fry in deep fat. Delicious with lamb chops or fish.

---

### Glazed Carrots

*Mrs. Walter Fuller. · Original printed p. 198, scan p. 205 · `mg1930-p205-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 carrots
- ½ cup butter
- ½ cup sugar
- Peas, to serve, optional

**Preparation**

Parboil carrots for fifteen minutes. Drain, place in a saucepan with equal parts butter and sugar (½ cup each for 3 carrots). Cook slowly until tender. Serve with peas.

---

### Scalloped Cauliflower With Mushrooms

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 198, scan p. 205 · `mg1930-p205-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 large cauliflowers
- 2 lbs. mushrooms
- 1 small onion, finely minced
- 10 tbsp. butter or margarine
- 6 tbsp. flour
- 2½ cups milk
- ½ cup mushroom liquor
- 1 tsp. salt
- ⅛ tsp. paprika
- ½ cup fine fresh bread crumbs

**Preparation**

Break cauliflower into flowerets and cook until tender. Drain and arrange in two greased casseroles.

Wash mushroom stems, cover with boiling water and simmer about 30 minutes or until the liquid is reduced to ½ cup. Cut mushroom caps into thin slices and cook for 5 minutes in 4 tbsp. fat, turning frequently.

To 6 tbsp. melted butter add the minced onion and cook until tender. Add flour, stirring constantly. When smooth, gradually add milk and mushroom liquor, stirring until thickened. Add salt, paprika and mushroom slices. Pour sauce over cauliflower, cover with bread crumbs and bake 30 minutes at 350°F, or until browned.

---

### Stuffed Celery

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 198, scan p. 205 · `mg1930-p205-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 tbsp. butter
- Salt
- 3 tbsp. soft cheese
- Paprika

**Preparation**

celery Cream butter, add cheese, and work until thoroughly blended. Season with salt and paprika. Wash celery, cut into desired lengths, and chill. Fill inside of stalks with cheese mixture. Roquefort cheese is especially good for this purpose.

---

### Corn Fritters

*Mrs. K. S. Russell. · Original printed p. 199, scan p. 206 · `mg1930-p206-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup canned corn
- pinch salt
- 1 tsp. sugar

Beat first four well, and add flour and baking powder. Bake like pancakes. Serve with butter and syrup or plain.

---

### Corn Mock Oysters

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 199, scan p. 206 · `mg1930-p206-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups canned corn
- 2 tbsp. butter
- 3 tbsp. milk
- 1 tsp. salt
- 1 cup flour
- pinch pepper
- ½ tsp. baking powder
- 1. egg

**Preparation**

Beat egg, and melt the butter. Mix dry ingredients, add corn, melted butter and milk, and beat until smooth. Add egg. Drop from spoon into hot fat in a frying pan. Brown first on one side and then on other. Serve on hot platter, or as a garnish for meat.

---

### Mock Oysters

*Mrs. Harry Becker. · Original printed p. 199, scan p. 206 · `mg1930-p206-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pint grated green corn
- 1/2 cup butter
- 1 egg
- salt
- 1 cup flour (level)
- pepper

**Preparation**

To the grated green corn add the egg well beaten, flour, butter, salt and pepper, and mix them well together. A tablespoonful of the batter will make the size of an oyster. Fry them a light brown, and when done, butter. Serves four.

---

### Green Corn Pudding

*Mrs. Geo. Williamson. · Original printed p. 199, scan p. 206 · `mg1930-p206-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 ears young sweet corn
- 1 tbsp. butter
- 1 tsp. flour
- 1 cup milk
- 2 eggs, separated
- ½ tsp. salt

**Preparation**

(6 to 8 Servings) Cut corn from cob, selecting four firm young ears of good sized corn, with a keen beaded knife, cutting only the depth of half the kernel. Scrape off the rest. This leaves a large portion of the husk of the kernel on the cob and removes none of the cob. Melt one tbsp. butter and add 1 tsp flour, 1 cup milk and add the yolks of two eggs well beaten and added carefully to milk after it has begun to boil. Stir in the corn, 1/2 tsp. salt and the whites of the eggs beaten stiffly. Pour mixture into two deep pie plates and bake for 25 minutes in hot oven. I parboil my corn before using it.

---

### Spanish Corn Pudding

*Mrs. Paul Snauble. · Original printed p. 199, scan p. 206 · `mg1930-p206-r05`*

- ½ cup Borden's Condensed milk
- 1 tbsp. chopped onion
- 1 can (2½ cups) green corn
- 1 tsp. salt
- 2 tbsp. pimento, chopped
- 2 eggs beaten

**Preparation**

Blend all together. Pour into buttered baking dish and bake in a moderate oven, 350 degrees, for about 25 minutes. Serve as entree, or as main course for a light dinner.

---

### Scalloped Corn

*Mrs. M. Ellis. · Original printed p. 200, scan p. 207 · `mg1930-p207-r01`*

- 1 can corn
- 1 cup bread or cracker crumbs, lightly browned

- 1 cup milk

- Salt and pepper

**Preparation**

Heat oven to 350°F and butter a baking dish. Whisk egg into milk with salt and pepper. Layer corn, onion and green pepper with the browned crumbs, ending with crumbs. Pour egg-milk mixture evenly over. Bake 25–35 minutes, until set and lightly browned.

---

### Corn And Cheese Souffle

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 200, scan p. 207 · `mg1930-p207-r02`*

- 2 tbsp. butter
- 1/4 tsp. salt
- 2 tbsp. flour, about
- pepper
- 1 cup milk
- 2 eggs

- 1 can corn

**Preparation**

Melt butter, stir in enough flour to make a salve. Add milk, cheese and seasonings, then beaten yolks of eggs. Strain corn, and add, stirring all the while. Beat whites and add last. Bake 30 minutes at 325 degrees, increasing at the last so that it will brown.

---

### Cucumbers Baked

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 200, scan p. 207 · `mg1930-p207-r03`*

- 4 cucumbers, 4 or 5 inches long bread crumbs

Peel cucumbers, and cut in half. Take out center and stand in salt water 12 day. Peel tomatoes and slice. Fry in butter with onion, and thicken with crumbs. ### Scalloped Eggplant

Shred lettuce. Fry bacon in small pieces. Pour over lettuce, add a little vinegar. Hard boiled eggs diced. Pepper and salt.

### Mushroom Woodchuck

### Parsnip Patties

Parsnips

### Scalloped Parsnips

- Cracker crumbs

Boil parsnips until fork-tender, then drain and slice. Butter a baking dish. Layer parsnips, salt and pepper, grated cheese and cracker crumbs; repeat until about 1 inch below the rim. Dot with butter and add milk to almost cover. Bake at 400°F for about 20 minutes, or until bubbly and golden.

### Stuffed Baked Potatoes

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 202, scan p. 209 · `mg1930-p209-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 good sized potatoes
- 1 Brookfield egg
- 6 tbsp. finely minced, uncooked
- 2 drops onion juice

- 1 slice Swift's Premium Bacon,

**Preparation**

finely minced Wash potatoes well, and remove centers, the long way of potatoes. An apple corer may be used, not cutting through the potato. Combine bacon and veal with crumbs, and mix thoroughly. Add onion juice and egg, unbeaten. Mix well, and fill potatoes with mixture. Place on end in a dripping pan, and bake 1 hour in a hot oven. The cut end of the potato should be up. Serve like ordinary baked potatoes. Bake at about 400 degrees.

---

### Potato Chowder

*Mrs. K. S. Russell. · Original printed p. 202, scan p. 209 · `mg1930-p209-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ lb. Swift's Premium bacon, cut in squares
- 2 large onions
- 6 good sized potatoes
- ½ cup boiling water

**Preparation**

Fry bacon in kettle until brown and crisp. Add sliced onions, and cook until brown. Then add cubed potatoes and boiling water: Cover and cook until potatoes are tender, adding more water if necessary.

---

### Spanish Potato

*Mrs. K. S. Russell. · Original printed p. 202, scan p. 209 · `mg1930-p209-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- Potatoes, enough for desired servings
- 2 medium onions
- 3 green peppers
- 1 cup milk
- 1 tbsp. butter
- 1 tbsp. flour
- ½ cup grated cheese
- Bread crumbs for topping

**Preparation**

Peel and boil enough potatoes for meal for 10 minutes. Drain, and, while hot, chop and mix 2 medium sized onions and 3 green peppers which have been run through the grinder. Fry all together until a light brown. Put in baking dish and mix with a cream sauce made of 1 cup milk, 1 tbsp. butter, 1 tbsp. flour and 1/2 cup cheese. Sprinkle top with bread crumbs, and bake until potatoes are tender and dish is well browned.

---

### Fancy Baked Potato

*Mrs. James Sigler. · Original printed p. 202, scan p. 209 · `mg1930-p209-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- Medium potatoes
- 1 strip bacon per potato (or sausage/frankfurter)

**Preparation**

Select medium sized potatoes. Make a hole in each one lengthwise with an apple corer. Put a slice of bacon in the hole. Bake until potato is done. Sausages or frankfurters may be substituted for bacon. Sweet potatoes may be used.

---

### Potatoes, Egg Timbales

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 203, scan p. 210 · `mg1930-p210-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups cold boiled potatoes,
- 2 level tbsp. butter, or other fat
- chopped
- salt, pepper, 4 eggs
- 5 tbsp. milk or cream
- 1 level tbsp. chopped parsley
- 2 level tbsp. flour
- 2 level tbsp. minced ham

**Preparation**

Time: Preparation and cooking, 30 minutes. Number served: 4 persons. Season the potatoes, sprinkle with flour, add milk, mix lightly. Put the butter into a saucepan; when melted and hot (not brown) put in the potatoes; pack down lightly, cook until deep brown on bottom; fold over as you would an omelet, cook five minutes longer, turn out carefully on a hot platter. While potatoes are browning prepare eggs. Grease small molds or cups, cover inside with parsley and ham; break an egg into each. Stand in a pan half full of water; cook slowly-on top of stove, or in oven-until eggs are set. Turn out on potatoes.

---

### Glacé Sweet Potatoes

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 203, scan p. 210 · `mg1930-p210-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 medium sized sweet potatoes
- 1 tbsp. butter
- ½ cup light brown sugar
- ⅛ tsp. nutmeg
- 1/4 cup water

**Preparation**

Pare potatoes and cut in slices 1/4 inch thick. Parboil 10 minutes. Make a syrup of the sugar, water and butter. Put potatoes in a buttered baking dish, and pour over half the syrup. Sprinkle with nutmeg and bake in a hot oven, 375 to 400 degrees, until a pale straw color. Baste with remaining syrup.

---

### Sweet Potatoes

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 203, scan p. 210 · `mg1930-p210-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- Boiled sweet potatoes
- Hot water, to loosen mash
- Butter
- Salt and other seasoning, to taste
- Marshmallows, for topping

**Preparation**

Mash boiled sweet potatoes, adding hot water, seasoning and butter. Put in a buttered Pyrex dish with a few marshmallows on top and brown in oven.

---

### Sweet Potatoes En Surprise

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 203, scan p. 210 · `mg1930-p210-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 sweet potatoes
- pepper
- 2 tbsp. butter
- speck cinnamon
- 1/4 cup milk
- 12 cooked sausages
- 1 tsp. salt

**Preparation**

Bake or boil the potatoes, remove skins and mash thoroughly. Add butter, milk, and seasonings. Fill flat remekins with potato mixture, bury two pieces of cooked sausage in each. Brush tops with melted drippings. Heat through and brown in hot oven. Time in combining, 15 minutes. Time in cooking, 30 and 15 minutes. Temperature, 350°. Makes six servings.

---

### Sweet Potatoes With Ham

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 204, scan p. 211 · `mg1930-p211-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 pound ham
- 3/4 cup sugar
- 2 cups sliced sweet potatoes
- 4 cup water

**Preparation**

Have the ham cut in a slice about two inches thick, put into cold water and let it scald. Pare and slice the sweet potatoes. Cut ham into pieces for serving, but fit into a bottom of baking-dish as one slice, put in potatoes, sprinkle sugar over them, and pour hot water over all. Bake in a moderate oven, basting often with the syrup in dish.

---

### Sweet Potatoes And Sausages

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 204, scan p. 211 · `mg1930-p211-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 sweet potatoes
- 1 pound sausage

**Preparation**

Parboil sweet potatoes, remove skins, cut in halves. Place pieces of sweet potato in a shallow casserole, place partially cooked sausage on top. Finish cooking in a hot oven or under broiler. Time in combining, 15 minutes. Time in cooking, 10 and 20 minutes. Temperature, 375 degrees. Recipe makes six servings.

---

### Stuffed Sweet Potato

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 204, scan p. 211 · `mg1930-p211-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 medium- sized sweet potatoes
- 1 egg
- 1 cup sausage meat

**Preparation**

Wash and bake sweet potatoes. When soft cut in two, take out the potato and mix with the cooked sausage meat and any fat extracted during cooking and the well-beaten egg. Refill potato shells, sprinkle with crumbs, put in oven to reheat and brown.

---

### Stuffed Onions

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 204, scan p. 211 · `mg1930-p211-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 6 large Bermuda onions
- 1 cup chopped nutmeats
- 1 cup fine dried bread crumbs
- 5 tsp. melted butter
- ½ tsp. salt
- ⅛ tsp. pepper
- 1 tbsp. minced parsley
- For sauce: 2 tbsp. butter
- For sauce: 2 tbsp. flour
- For sauce: 1½ cups milk
- For sauce: ¼ tsp. salt
- For sauce: ⅛ tsp. pepper
- For sauce: 2 tbsp. grated cheese
- For sauce: 1 egg yolk

**Preparation**

Peel onions and cook in boiling salted water for 45 minutes. Cool, cut a slice from the top of each and scoop out the centers, leaving a shell about ¾ inch thick. Combine nutmeats, bread crumbs, butter, salt, pepper and parsley; fill onion shells. Put in a buttered baking dish, add 1 cup boiling water and bake 20 minutes. Baste with 1 tbsp. butter melted in a little hot water.

For the sauce, melt 2 tbsp. butter, stir in flour and cook, stirring constantly until blended and bubbly. Slowly add milk and bring to a boil. Stir in cheese until melted; remove from heat and beat in egg yolk. Do not boil after cheese and yolk are added. Serve stuffed onions surrounded by sauce.

---

### Baked Onions

*Mrs. J. B. Fleugel. · Original printed p. 205, scan p. 212 · `mg1930-p212-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 3 cups onions
- 1 tbsp. flour
- 1 cup milk
- 2 tbsp. butter
- salt
- butter

**Preparation**

Mix and put in buttered baking dish and bake 1 hour.

---

### Baked Onions

*Mrs. K. S. Russell. · Original printed p. 205, scan p. 212 · `mg1930-p212-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- Medium onions, all about the same size, skins on

**Preparation**

Select onions the same size. Leave all dry skin on without cutting. Bake in a dripping pan until tender. Then squeeze at the root end, and a juicy onion will slip out from the outer skin all ready to garnish platter around the roast.

---

### Onion Souffle

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 205, scan p. 212 · `mg1930-p212-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup cream sauce
- 1 cup boiled onions
- ½ cup bread crumbs
- 2 eggs
- 1 tsp. chopped parsley

**Preparation**

Cream Sauce: 1 tbsp. butter 1 tbsp. flour 1 cup milk Make cream sauce with butter, flour, and milk. Chop onions rather fine. Add parsley and combine with sauce. Stir in beaten egg yolks, and last of all beaten whites. Pile lightly in buttered baking dish. Sprinkle top with crumbs. Bake in moderate oven until set. Time in combining, 5 minutes. Time in cooking, 20 minutes. Temperature, 212-300 °.

---

### Spinach Luncheon Dish

*Mrs. James E. Rork. · Original printed p. 205, scan p. 212 · `mg1930-p212-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1½ cup drained cooked spinach
- ½ tsp. salt and pepper
- 1 tbsp. grated onion
- 1 tbsp. flour
- ½ cup grated cheese
- ½ cup milk
- 6 eggs
- 1/4 cup butter

**Preparation**

Make a white sauce of the milk, butter, flour and seasonings. Add 2 egg yolks when cooked. To this add the spinach chopped very fine. Then add two beaten egg whites. Put in large Pyrex baking dish, making four dents for eggs. Bake in moderate oven for about 25 minutes, then break eggs on the top and sprinkle with grated cheese. Return to oven for about seven minutes. Serve immediately from baking dish.

---

### Mustard Sauce For Spinach

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 205, scan p. 212 · `mg1930-p212-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- scant tbsp. brown sugar
- 1 beaten egg
- ¼ cup vinegar
- ½ tsp. mustard

**Preparation**

Heat vinegar and sugar. Add beaten egg, mustard. Cook until thick.

---

### Baked Squash With Sausage

*Mrs. M. Ellis. · Original printed p. 206, scan p. 213 · `mg1930-p213-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- Winter squash, halved and seeded
- Link or bulk sausage
- Salt, pepper and butter, to season

**Preparation**

Cut squash in half and remove seeds. Place sausage in squash and bake until squash is done. Remove squash from shell and mash, add salt, pepper and butter. Arrange squash on platter and place sausage around and on top of squash. Link sausage is desired but the bulk can be used.

---

### Hubbard Squash In Casserole

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 206, scan p. 213 · `mg1930-p213-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- Butter casserole. Slice into it a layer of squash, small pieces.

**Preparation**

Sprinkle with brown sugar, a little butter and salt. Add another layer and season as before. Pour over all about 1/2 cup boiling water. Bake 1 hour in slow oven, 325 degrees, basting with hot liquor or boiling water, if it becomes dry. Cover first half of cooking time.

---

### Baked Summer Squash

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 206, scan p. 213 · `mg1930-p213-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 4 tbsp. butter
- 1 small onion
- 3 tomatoes
- 1 lb. summer squash
- 4 ears corn
- Salt and pepper

**Preparation**

Melt butter and cook chopped onion gently until yellow. Transfer to a casserole. Add corn cut from the cob, chopped tomatoes and summer squash cut into small pieces. Season with salt and pepper. Cover and bake 30 minutes at 350°F.

---

### Baked Sauer Kraut

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 206, scan p. 213 · `mg1930-p213-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 can sauer kraut
- 1½ tbsp. butter
- ½ cup brown sugar
- pork chops

**Preparation**

Add sugar to kraut, and place in casserole. Place chops and butter on top. Cover and bake about 1 hour at 375 degrees.

---

### Tomato And Cabbage Mulligan

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 206, scan p. 213 · `mg1930-p213-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 tbsp. butter
- 1 small cabbage
- 2 large onions
- 1 qt. tomatoes
- 2 sweet peppers
- buttered crumbs

**Preparation**

Melt butter, add minced onion and peppers. Cook until a delicate brown. Shred or chop cabbage very fine. Stir into onion mixture and brown. Turn into greased baking dish, pour over the well seasoned tomatoes, cover with buttered crumbs and bake in moderate oven until cabbage is tender. Bake 45 minutes at 350 degrees. Serves 8.

### Vegetable Casserole

*Mrs. Torrance Phelps. · Original printed p. 207, scan p. 214 · `mg1930-p214-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups corn
- Green pepper
- 3 cups tomatoes
- Salt
- 4 onions
- Pepper

**Preparation**

Arrange in 6 layers in greased baking dish, sprinkling each layer with chopped green pepper. Dot top with butter. Bake 30 minutes at 350 degrees.

---

### Vegetable Dish For Oven Meal

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. 207, scan p. 214 · `mg1930-p214-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- Tomatoes, fresh or canned
- Onions, sliced
- Green peppers, sliced
- Uncooked rice, for layering
- Butter
- Bread crumbs
- Salt and pepper
- Water or stock, as needed to moisten rice

**Preparation**

Fill a baking dish with layers of tomatoes, fresh or canned, onions, greenpeppers, season, sprinkle with raw rice, bits of butter, and repeat layers. Sprinkle top with bread crumbs and bake about an hour and a half. Potatoes, and biscuits may be baked at the same time, and the broiler used for chops or steak. Mrs. K. S. Russell.

### Vegetable Hash

*Mrs. E. D. Ganyard. · Original printed p. 207, scan p. 214 · `mg1930-p214-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 5 medium potatoes
- 1 lb. round steak, ground
- 3 medium carrots
- 2 Holland rusks
- 3 medium onions

**Preparation**

Grind the vegetables and mix well with the hamburg, and add one rusk crushed fine. Put in casserole, and cover with the other rusk, crushed fine. Cover with milk and bake 2 hours in a slow oven, 300 degrees. Serves 6.

---

### Cream Of Mushroom Soup

*Mrs. Harry Davisson. · Original printed p. 207, scan p. 214 · `mg1930-p214-r04`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 qt. thin white sauce
- 1 can mushrooms, cut up

**Preparation**

Prepare 1 quart of thin white sauce (about 2 tbsp. butter, 2 tbsp. flour and 4 cups milk, cooked until lightly thickened). Drain and chop mushrooms, reserving a little of their liquid if desired. Stir mushrooms into the white sauce, simmer gently 5–10 minutes and season with salt and pepper. Thin with milk or mushroom liquid to serving consistency.

---

### Cream Of Onion Soup

*Mrs. Hubert Anderson. · Original printed p. 207, scan p. 214 · `mg1930-p214-r05`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 cup thinly sliced onions
- 2½ cups milk
- 2 tbsp. butter
- 1 egg
- 2 tbsp. bacon fat
- salt and pepper
- 2 tbsp. flour
- ½ cup cream
- 1 tbsp. minced parsley

**Preparation**

Melt butter and fat in pan without letting the fats bubble, add onion slices and cook over a low fire 30 minutes. Keep the pan closely covered, letting the onions cook in their own juice and steam. When the onions are soft add flour and stir with a fork until thoroughly blended. Add milk, salt and pepper and bring to the boiling point, stirring constantly. Let boil 5 minutes, and rub through a strainer. Add egg slightly beaten and reheat over hot water if necessary. Whip cream and serve a spoonful sprinkled with parsley on each portion.

---

### Cream Liquid Life Soup

*Mrs. Marguerite Hale. · Original printed p. 208, scan p. 215 · `mg1930-p215-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- 1 tin Liquid Life
- 1 pint whole milk

**Preparation**

Add milk to Liquid Life and season with butter, celery and parsley leaves. Heat 15 minutes, but do not boil. Just before serving add 1/3 tin Whole Grain Wheat.

---

### Chicken Soup

*Mrs. H. D. Cox. · Original printed p. 208, scan p. 215 · `mg1930-p215-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 qts. chicken stock
- 2 bay leaves
- 3 cups diced celery
- ⅓ cup rice, washed
- 1 Bermuda onion, diced

**Preparation**

Add celery, onion, and bay leaves to stock, and cook for about an hour. Then add rice and cook about half an hour longer. This makes a thick and tasty soup.

## Advertising recipes (Royal Baking Powder)

### Providence Muffins

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. None, scan p. 71 · `mg1930-p071-r01`*

**Ingredients**

- ½ cup graham flour
- ½ cup bran
- ½ teaspoon salt
- 2 teaspoons sugar
- 2 teaspoons Royal Baking Powder
- 1 teaspoon butter
- 1 tablespoon peanut butter
- 1 egg
- ½ cup milk

**Preparation**

Mix flour, bran, salt, sugar, and baking powder. Melt butter and peanut butter together, then add to dry mixture. Add the well-beaten egg, followed by milk; beat well. Bake in hot greased muffin tins at 375°F for 12 minutes. Makes six good-sized muffins.

---

### Mahogany Cake

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. None, scan p. 71 · `mg1930-p071-r02`*

**Ingredients**

- Chocolate mixture: ½ cup milk
- Chocolate mixture: ½ cup cocoa
- ½ cup butter
- 1½ cups sugar
- 3 eggs
- 2 cups pastry flour
- ¾ teaspoon baking soda
- 2 teaspoons Royal Baking Powder
- ⅔ cup sweet milk
- 1 teaspoon vanilla extract

**Preparation**

Cook the first ½ cup milk with cocoa until thick and smooth; cool. Cream butter and sugar, then add beaten egg yolks. Sift together flour, baking soda, and baking powder, and add alternately with milk. Add cooled chocolate mixture and vanilla. Fold in stiffly beaten egg whites. Bake in two greased nine-inch layer pans at 350°F for about 30 minutes. Spread Fudge Frosting between layers and over the cake.

---

### Fudge Frosting

*Contributor not recorded · Original printed p. None, scan p. 71 · `mg1930-p071-r03`*

**Ingredients**

- 2 cups sugar
- 3 squares (3 oz.) unsweetened chocolate
- 2 tablespoons corn syrup
- ½ cup milk
- 2 tablespoons butter
- 1 teaspoon vanilla extract
- ½ teaspoon Royal Baking Powder

**Preparation**

Cook sugar, chocolate, corn syrup, and milk, stirring until sugar dissolves. Stir occasionally to prevent burning. Cook to 232°F or to the very-soft-ball stage. Remove from heat and add baking powder and butter. Cool to lukewarm, add vanilla, and beat until creamy and spreadable.

---
